### [1] [A reciprocal-Gamma Mehler--Heine limit for Krawtchouk--Sobolev type orthogonal polynomials with an exterior mass point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25060)
*Anier Soria Lorente,Junior Michel*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究离散Krawtchouk内积的Sobolev型修正下首一正交多项式的Mehler–Heine公式。在n,N→∞、n/N→r（0<p<r<1，倒数Gamma区）的联合尺度下，证明极限恰通过线性因子z−α保留外部质量，且极限与λ>0、j≥1无关；Uvarov情形j=0同样成立，并讨论定理尺度及λ=0处不连续性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散Krawtchouk测度在Sobolev型（外部点质量经前向差分）修正下的正交多项式尚无Mehler–Heine渐近。作者希望刻画外部质量在大次数极限中的存留方式、其普适常数，并检验对修正参数的依赖，从而衔接经典（纯离散）与Sobolev/Uvarov正交多项式理论。

Method: 采用点态、自足方法：利用秩一连接公式、再生核递推、Dominici点态极限与经典三项递推。固定N时，归一化Sobolev修正满足精确仿射递推，借助两个后向窗口的三角收缩论证及显式边界估计加以控制；收缩比ϱ<1恰等价于条件r>p。

Result: 证明该倒数Gamma区间的Mehler–Heine公式：外部质量在极限中仅以线性因子z−α出现，含显式普适常数C*，极限不依赖λ>0与j≥1，Uvarov情形j=0同理。该归一化下经典项渐近可忽略；λ无关性针对每个固定λ>0，而非λ=0处连续性。多精度计算验证收敛性，并在p=0.3、r=3/5、α=−2.5、j=2、λ=5时给出C*=2.8。

Conclusion: 在倒数Gamma尺度下，Sobolev型离散Krawtchouk正交多项式的Mehler–Heine极限具有普适性与参数稳健性：外部质量以z−α形式保留，常数C*显式且与λ、j无关，j=0的Uvarov情形一致；λ无关性仅指固定λ>0，λ=0处不连续。

Abstract: We study the monic polynomials $\Sob{n}$ orthogonal with respect to a Sobolev-type modification of the discrete Krawtchouk inner product in which a single point mass at an exterior point $α<0$, outside the support $\{0,1,\dots,N\}$ of the binomial measure, acts through the $j$-th forward difference $\dif^{j}$ (here $\Nzero=\{0,1,2,\dots\}$, $\Z_{+}=\{1,2,\dots\}$, $\R_{+}=(0,\infty)$). In the joint scaling $n,N\to\infty$, $n/N\to r$ with $0<p<r<1$ (the \emph{reciprocal-Gamma} regime of Dominici's classification), we prove the Mehler--Heine formula. The exterior mass survives the large-degree limit exactly through the linear factor $z-α$, with the explicit universal constant $C_{\ast}$, and the limit is independent of both $λ>0$ and $j\ge1$; the same conclusion holds in the Uvarov case $j=0$ (Corollary~\ref{cor:uvarov}). The derivation is pointwise and self-contained: it uses only a rank-one connection formula (Theorem~\ref{thm:connection}), the reproducing-kernel recursion, Dominici's pointwise limit and the classical three-term recurrence; the normalised Sobolev correction obeys an exact affine recursion at fixed $N$, controlled by a triangular contraction argument over two backward windows with an explicit boundary estimate, and the contraction ratio $\varrho<1$ is exactly the condition $r>p$. We also discuss the scale of the theorem: in this normalisation the classical term is asymptotically negligible, and the independence of $λ$ concerns each fixed $λ>0$, not the continuity at $λ=0$. Multiprecision computations illustrate the convergence and the exact value $C_{\ast}=2.8$ for $p=0.3$, $r=3/5$, $α=-2.5$, $j=2$, $λ=5$.

</details>


### [2] [Spectral synthesis with the complexity parameter in $\mathbb{Z}_N^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25404)
*Alex Iosevich,Zhihe Li,Ke Yu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文利用Fourier比值研究 $\mathbb{Z}_N^d$ 上的渐近谱合成，证明有限尺度合成估计，给出临界指数、精确合成常数，并联系Salem估计、加性能量与延拓估计的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $\mathbb{Z}_N^d$ 上渐近谱合成问题，借助Fourier比值（计数测度 $\ell^1$ 与 $\ell^2$ 范数之比）刻画支撑集上权重的合成性质，并确定临界指数与合成常数。

Method: 引入Fourier-ratio concentration exponent $\kappa$，对基数 $N^{\alpha+o(1)}$ 的支撑得到 $0\leq\kappa\leq\alpha/2$；建立基于函数或其支撑指示函数Fourier比值的有限尺度合成估计，使指示条件对任意权重一致成立；利用均匀 $\ell^p$ 界导出版最大Fourier系数的衰减；以余集随机并集构造反例；对具传递仿射对称群的集合确定精确合成常数；对抛物面给出显式除数和公式；并将Fourier集中与广义Salem估计、加性能量、Fourier代数范数及延拓估计必要条件相联系。

Result: 在 $2\leq p<2(d-2\kappa)/(\alpha-2\kappa)$ 时得到Fourier系数衰减，最大集中时允许所有有限 $p\geq2$；随机余集并集证明有限临界指数在每个给定环境维数、沿素数幂模数对每个可行对均为锐利。对传递仿射对称群集合给出精确合成常数；抛物面的常数为显式除数和，该公式证明沿固定奇素数幂的端点合成，但沿素数失败；在互异小素数乘积上合成可延拓至更大指数；这些族具有相同的Fourier-ratio指数。

Conclusion: Fourier比值是刻画 $\mathbb{Z}_N^d$ 上谱合成强度与临界的有效工具，临界指数在素数幂模数下通常锐利，而合成常数与对称结构、模数算术性质密切相关；同时Fourier集中与Salem估计、加性能量、Fourier代数范数及延拓估计必要条件存在深层联系。

Abstract: We study asymptotic spectral synthesis on $\mathbb Z_N^d$ using the Fourier ratio, the quotient of the counting-measure $\ell^1$ and $\ell^2$ norms of the unitary Fourier transform. For supports of cardinality $N^{α+o(1)}$, the Fourier-ratio concentration exponent satisfies $0\leqκ\leq\fracα{2}$. We prove a finite-scale synthesis estimate that uses either the Fourier ratio of a function or that of its support indicator. In particular, the indicator condition applies uniformly to arbitrary weights on the support. Uniform $\ell^p$ bounds imply decay of the largest Fourier coefficient when $2\leq p<\frac{2(d-2κ)}{α-2κ}$, with every finite $p\geq2$ allowed at maximal concentration. Random unions of cosets show that the finite critical exponent is sharp for every admissible pair in each prescribed ambient dimension, along prime-power moduli.
  We also determine the exact synthesis constant for sets with a transitive group of affine symmetries. For paraboloids this constant is an explicit divisor sum. The formula proves endpoint synthesis along powers of a fixed odd prime, although the endpoint fails along primes. Along products of distinct small primes, synthesis extends to a larger exponent. These families have the same Fourier-ratio exponent. Finally, we relate Fourier concentration to generalized Salem estimates, additive energy, Fourier algebra norms, and necessary conditions for extension estimates.

</details>


### [3] [Dynamical Sampling in Graph Paley--Wiener Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25472)
*Iulia Martina Bulai,Carlos Cabrelli,Elena Cordero,Ursula Molter,Sandra Saliani*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文利用归一化图拉普拉斯的谱理论研究图信号的动力采样：定义图Paley-Wiener空间GPW_w，研究在算子迭代生成测量下的重构，对格图Z给出显式谱分解与经典采样定理，并构造动力框架与重构公式，推广至Z^n，最后对有限图以路径图P_N为数值模型分析迭代次数与采样点分布对框架界与条件数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典动力采样（dynamical sampling）理论从欧氏空间推广到图信号处理，需要一套基于归一化图拉普拉斯谱的框架来定义带限空间并刻画从算子轨道迭代测量中的重构问题。

Method: 采用归一化图拉普拉斯的谱理论；定义与带宽参数w相关的图Paley-Wiener空间GPW_w（谱子空间）；要求在保持该空间不变的有界算子迭代下进行测量与重构；对格图Z求显式谱分解并将GPW_w(Z)与环面上的经典带限空间对应，用算子轨道的框架构造动力框架并导出重构公式；推广至Z^n（带宽区域由拉普拉斯符号的下水平集描述）；对有限图用图算子迭代生成的有限框架刻画GPW_w(G)中的动力恢复。

Result: 对Z给出了归一化拉普拉斯的显式谱分解，识别出GPW_w(Z)即环面上的经典带限空间并恢复经典采样定理；构造了GPW_w(Z)的动力框架与重构公式；将结果推广到Z^n，带宽区域由拉普拉斯符号的下水平集刻画；对有限图给出了以算子迭代生成有限框架的动力恢复表述；以路径图P_N为数值模型，揭示了迭代次数与采样顶点空间分布对最优框架界及动力框架条件数的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了图信号动力采样的谱理论框架，统一了格图上的经典采样结果并推广到高维格与有限图，表明迭代次数与采样点分布是决定重构稳定性的关键因素。

Abstract: We study dynamical sampling for graph signals using the spectral theory of the normalized graph Laplacian. Graph Paley-Wiener spaces GPW_w are defined as spectral subspaces associated with a bandwidth parameter w, and reconstruction is studied from measurements generated by iterates of bounded operators leaving these spaces invariant.
  For the lattice graph Z, we give an explicit spectral decomposition of the normalized Laplacian, identify the corresponding Graph Paley--Wiener spaces with classical bandlimited spaces on the torus, and recover the classical sampling theorem in this setting. Using frames of operator orbits, we construct dynamical frames for GPW_w(Z) and derive reconstruction formulas. We also discuss the extension to Z^n, where the bandwidth region is described by a sublevel set of the Laplacian symbol.
  Finally, for finite graphs, we formulate dynamical recovery in GPW_w(G)\) in terms of finite frames generated by iterates of the graph operator. Using the path graph P_N as a numerical model, we study how the number of iterates and the spatial distribution of the sampled vertices affect the optimal frame bounds and the conditioning of the resulting dynamical frame.

</details>


### [4] [Matrix Continued Fractions from Bidiagonal Factorizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26066)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文基于有序双对角分解构造矩阵连分式，通过循环线性化与有理高度排序得到块三对角算子，截断后获得与回归级数前2N+1次一致的Padé型逼近，并给出收敛性与残差估计及两类谱实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典标量连分式理论推广到矩阵与多分支情形，把有序双对角分解的各个参数作为连分式系数，从而为矩阵函数、Weyl矩阵及q×p矩形谱问题提供结构化逼近与收敛工具。

Method: 从有序双对角分解T=L_1…L_pU_q…U_1出发构造矩阵连分式；用循环线性化把T的单次作用分解为p+q个基本步骤；按有理高度排序将算子化为块三对角形式；在高度块N处截断得到Padé型逼近；利用Schur补枢轴的行列式分解分析分母，并通过算子与有限截面假设证明收敛、给出残差估计；对非负系数与正混合Favard假设情形分别讨论单调性、M-矩阵性与矩形投影。

Result: 截断逼近与回归级数在2N+1次内一致；分母行列式可分解为逐次Schur补枢轴行列式之积；识别出首个被舍去系数；在给定假设下证明收敛并给出可计算残差估计；非负系数时收敛式在谱半径圆盘内正实区间上逐元素单调且分母为非奇异M-矩阵；正混合Favard假设下可由矩形投影得到q×p Weyl矩阵的Padé型逼近；q=1退化为p分支Stieltjes连分式，p,q>1时尾部为矩阵值；给出混合Piñeiro系统与Jacobi型推广两种显式谱实现，后者可经Gamma因子完全抵消恢复前者。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了双对角分解、矩阵连分式与块三对角截断之间的系统联系，统一并推广了Stieltjes连分式与Weyl矩阵逼近理论，并为收敛性和误差控制提供了可计算框架。

Abstract: Starting from an ordered bidiagonal factorization $T=L_1\cdots L_pU_q\cdots U_1$, this paper constructs a matrix continued fraction whose coefficients are the individual bidiagonal parameters. A cyclic linearization resolves one action of $T$ into $p+q$ elementary steps, and an ordering by rational height converts the resulting operator into block-tridiagonal form. Truncation after height block $N$ gives a structured Padé-type approximant that agrees with the return series through degree $2N+1$. Its denominator determinant factors into the determinants of successive Schur-complement pivots. The first omitted coefficient is identified, and operator and finite-section hypotheses yield convergence with a computable residual estimate. For nonnegative coefficients, the convergents are entrywise monotone on the positive real interval inside the spectral-radius disc, and their denominators are nonsingular $M$-matrices. Under positive mixed Favard hypotheses, rectangular projection produces Padé-type approximants to the $q\times p$ Weyl matrix. When $q=1$, the construction reduces to the $p$-branched Stieltjes fraction; when $p,q>1$, its tails are matrix-valued. Two explicit spectral realizations are developed. The mixed Piñeiro system provides closed factor coefficients and a rational $(3,2)$ test of the convergence and residual estimates. A Jacobi-like extension yields a second family of rectangular Weyl matrices, and complete Gamma-factor cancellation recovers the Piñeiro case.

</details>


### [5] [Hypergeometric Mixed-Type Multiple Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26110)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 在正实轴上为正秩一 $q\times p$ 权矩阵构造了两类混合型多重正交形式（Jacobi-型与Laguerre-型），显式给出容许近对角范围内的高次超几何表示、递推系数、Christoffel变换与Gauss–Borel双对角分解，并讨论合流极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合型（mixed-type）多重正交形式在多矩阵权与任意 $p,q$ 秩一情形的显式超几何构造与递推结构尚不完备，需要在统一框架下给出Jacobi-/Laguerre-型族的表示、递推与因子分解，以连接已知多重正交与合流极限。

Method: 在正实轴上取秩一 $q\times p$ 权矩阵，构造Jacobi-型与Laguerre-型混合型多重正交形式；在近对角容许范围内求显式表达式：幂向量分量为终止广义超几何多项式，超几何向量分量为有限和；Jacobi-型化为终止Kampé de Fériet多项式在 $(x,1)$ 的有限组合，beta–Euler Laguerre-型由有限三角连接系统控制；推导Gamma商Mellin公式、Meijer $G$ 表示与Rodrigues型公式；在step-line上用双递推与Gamma–Pochhammer显式系数描述双正交序列；用Christoffel变换与Gauss–Borel分解得到递推矩阵的双对角分解与tau-行列式/矩次式交比表达。

Result: 两类混合形式被显式构造；幂向量分量为单一终止广义超几何多项式，超几何向量分量为有限和（Jacobi-型为Kampé de Fériet在 $(x,1)$ 的组合；Laguerre-型由有限三角连接系统控制）；得到完整形式的Gamma商Mellin公式、Meijer $G$ 表示与Rodrigues型公式；step-line上双正交序列满足具 $p$ 条次对角与 $q$ 条超对角的双递推，各带系数由有限Gamma–Pochhammer显式给出；递推矩阵具双对角分解，下因子有闭Pochhammer公式，上因子由Christoffel tau-行列式或移位矩次式交比表达；两条链在混合Piñeiro特化下显式闭合。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了正实轴上秩一 $q\times p$ 权矩阵的两类混合型多重正交形式的完整超几何与递推理论，并建立Christoffel–Gauss–Borel分解框架；合流极限将Jacobi-型与Laguerre-型、Hermite-型系统联系起来，混合Piñeiro特化提供显式闭合案例。

Abstract: Two hypergeometric families of mixed-type multiple orthogonal forms are constructed on the positive real line for rank-one $q\times p$ matrices of weights, with arbitrary $p$ and $q$: Jacobi-like and Laguerre-like systems. In the admissible near-diagonal range, both mixed forms are obtained explicitly. Their power-vector components are single terminating generalized hypergeometric polynomials, whereas their hypergeometric-vector components are finite sums of such polynomials. In the Jacobi-like case these sums reduce to finite combinations of terminating Kampé de Fériet polynomials evaluated at $(x,1)$; in the mixed beta--Euler Laguerre-like case they are governed by a finite triangular connection system. Gamma-quotient Mellin formulas, Meijer $G$-representations, and Rodrigues-type formulas are obtained for the complete forms. On the step-line, the associated biorthogonal sequences satisfy dual recurrences with $p$ subdiagonals and $q$ superdiagonals, and every band coefficient is given explicitly by finite Gamma--Pochhammer expressions. Christoffel transformations and Gauss--Borel factorization yield bidiagonal factorizations of the recurrence matrices. The lower factors have closed Pochhammer formulas, while the upper factors are expressed through Christoffel tau-determinants or cross-ratios of shifted moment minors. Both chains close explicitly in the mixed Piñeiro specialization. Confluent limits connect the Jacobi-like systems with the Laguerre-like and Hermite-like systems.

</details>


### [6] [Dunkl theory, convolution algebras, and related Markov processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26394)
*Margit Rösler,Michael Voit*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文是理性Dunkl算子理论的讲义式导论，系统介绍Dunkl算子、 intertwining算子、Dunkl核与变换、Dunkl Laplacian及热半群，并探讨乘积公式、广义平移、Weyl室上的交换超群结构，以及欧氏型Riemann对称空间与正半定矩阵锥上Bessel超群的联系，最后讨论相关Markov过程、鞅特征与Appell特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为介绍Dunkl算子、相关特殊函数及Markov过程提供一份综合性导论，重点通过欧氏型Riemann对称空间与正半定矩阵锥上Bessel超群的例子，阐明Dunkl理论与超群结构及概率论之间的联系。

Method: 采用讲义综述形式，从Dunkl算子、intertwining算子及其正性、Dunkl核与Dunkl变换、Dunkl Laplacian及热半群入手，概述与Calogero-Moser-Sutherland模型和广义Hermite多项式的联系；重点分析乘积公式、广义平移及闭Weyl室上的交换超群结构，并说明特定重数下Dunkl理论与欧氏型Riemann对称空间、矩阵锥上Bessel超群的关系；最后讨论R^N上与Dunkl理论相关的Markov过程、鞅、鞅特征、矩函数与Appell特征。

Result: 构建了Dunkl理论的系统框架，并揭示：对某类重数，Weyl群不变的Dunkl理论在闭Weyl室上具有保概率的广义平移及交换超群结构；阐明了其与欧氏型Riemann对称空间及正半定矩阵锥上Bessel超群的联系；建立了与Dunkl理论相关的Markov过程、随机游走、鞅特征及以Dunkl Laplacian为生成元的扩散-反射过程之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: Dunkl理论为特殊函数、超群结构与Markov过程提供了统一框架，特别地，某些重数下可实现保概率平移与闭Weyl室上的交换超群结构，并与Riemann对称空间、Bessel超群及随机游走和扩散-反射过程密切相关。

Abstract: These lecture notes are intended as an introduction to the theory of rational Dunkl operators, the associated special functions and related Markov processes with an emphasis on examples which are related to Riemannian symmetric spaces of Euclidean type and Bessel hypergroups on the matrix cones of positive semidefinite matrices.
  We start with a comprehensive introduction into Dunkl theory: Dunkl operators, the intertwining operator and its positivity, the Dunkl kernel and the Dunkl transform, the Dunkl Laplacian and the associated heat semigroup. We further give an outline of the connection with Calogero-Moser-Sutherland models and generalized Hermite polynomials. Moreover, of central interest will be product formulas, generalized translations and associated commutative hypergroup structures on closed Weyl chambers. In particular, we explain how Dunkl theory for particular multiplicities is related to Riemannian symmetric spaces of Euclidean type and Bessel hypergroups on the matrix cones, and how this leads to a bunch of multiplicities for which the Weyl-group invariant Dunkl theory admits a probability preserving translation and an associated commutative hypergroup structure on the closed Weyl chamber. We finally discuss Markov processes on R^N which are related with Dunkl theory with an emphasis on connections to random walk on groups and hypergroups. In particular associated martingales, martingale characterizations, moment functions and Appell characters are studied, i.e. diffusion-reflection processes with the Dunkl Laplacians as generators.

</details>


### [7] [Voltage and current density in a rectangle with point in- and ejection via Green's functions and $q$-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26649)
*P. Van Mieghem*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文给出了二维矩形中电势与电流密度大小的精确且计算高效的解析公式，借助q-分析（高斯多项式与Jacobi theta函数）克服了双Fourier级数收敛极慢的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维矩形中电流密度的计算是经典问题：在源s与汇d放置等量异号点电荷，电流密度为满足Poisson方程（Maxwell第一定律）的电势梯度。然而原有方法（如双Fourier级数）收敛极慢，存在计算瓶颈。

Method: 采用q-分析方法，引入高斯多项式与Jacobi theta函数——它们是任意椭圆函数的基本构件，以此对电势与电流密度进行解析求解。

Result: 得到了矩形内电势及电流密度大小的精确解析公式，计算效率显著提高。

Conclusion: q-分析为二维矩形电流密度问题提供了精确、高效的解析表达，缓解了传统级数方法的收敛障碍。

Abstract: The current density in a rectangle in the $D=2$ dimensional Euclidean space, where opposite point charges are placed in a source $s$ and a destination $d$, is computed as the gradient of a potential that obeys the Poisson equation (first law of Maxwell). Although the computation of the current density in a 2D rectangle is a classical problem, several barriers (e.g. very slow convergence of double Fourier series) were eventually alleviated by a $q$-analysis, related to Gaussian polynomials and Jacobi's theta-functions, that form the basic building blocks for any elliptic function. We present an exact and computationally very efficient analytic formula of the potential and the magnitude of the current density in a rectangle.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 得到 $C_{\mathrm{sq0}}(\alpha)=\pi\sin\alpha/(2\alpha)$，并由 $2\times2$ 矩阵达到等号；直角情形下在任意维数证明了完整回文二次族的 $\sqrt2$ 界；对复自同构后圆盘、完整虚直径、边界触及相位弧、对称与非对称三节点可容许核等参数族给出精确有理证书；另获严格中心界 $|w^2|\le\kappa_0<\sqrt2$、双小零扩展、剩余边界层的精确双矩判据以及角度相关包络。

Conclusion: 结果与维数无关，给出了多个可解族上的尖锐或严格常数及可验证证书，但仍未确定无限制固定透镜常数 $C(\alpha)$，为该问题留下开放部分。

Abstract: For the intersection of two disks meeting at angle $2α$, let $C(α)$ be the least constant in the associated spectral-set inequality. We give a self-contained M"obius reduction to the corresponding numerical-range problem on a sector and determine the sharp constant for affine square-zero operators $B=λI+N$, $N^2=0$: $C_{\mathrm{sq0}}(α)=π\sinα/(2α)$. A $2\times2$ matrix attains equality and yields an explicit lens lower-bound certificate. At the right angle, we prove the conjectural $\sqrt2$ bound in arbitrary dimension for the full palindromic quadratic family, and give exact rational certificates for several larger parameter families, including a complex post-automorphism disk, the complete imaginary diameter, boundary-reaching phase arcs, and symmetric and asymmetric three-node admissible-kernel problems. We also obtain a strict central bound $|w^2|\leqκ_0<\sqrt2$, a two-small-zero extension, an exact two-moment criterion for the remaining boundary layer, and a verified angle-dependent envelope. Every computer-assisted assertion has an exact rational verifier. These results are dimension-free but do not determine the unrestricted fixed-lens constant.

</details>

Abstract: We analyse Schrödinger operators with accretive potentials in weighted spaces. We find conditions on potentials and weights for which the Dirichlet realisation, introduced by generalised form methods, has non-empty resolvent set. We establish a domain and graph norm separation property, as well as sufficient conditions for the compactness and Schatten class of the resolvent. Moreover, we investigate the relation between discrete spectra and eigenfunctions of operators in standard and weighted spaces. As applications we extend results on the completeness of eigensystems of operators with accretive potentials from standard to weighted spaces and analyse operator matrices exhibiting a Schur dominance property, in particular, related to a wave equation with strong accretive damping.

From a discrete Green identity we derive a matrix-valued Maass--Selberg formula for the Hermitian matrix $iS(θ)^*\partial_θS(θ)$, where $S(θ)$ is the scattering matrix. Its trace is determined by $\det S(θ)$, while the full matrix retains additional cusp-to-cusp information.

Using their resonance computations as input, we distinguish eigenvalues supported entirely in the finite core from poles of the scattering matrix. Conclusion: Ashbaugh所记录的高指标Payne--Pólya--Weinberger型猜想不成立，本文通过构造光滑边界区域给出了系统性反例。

Abstract: Let $λ_k(Ω)$ denote the $k$th Dirichlet Laplacian eigenvalue of a bounded planar domain $Ω$, with eigenvalues counted with multiplicity. We disprove a conjectured higher-index extension of the Payne--Pólya--Weinberger inequality that was recorded as an open problem by Ashbaugh. For every integer $m\geq3$, we construct a bounded planar domain $Ω_m$ with $C^\infty$ boundary such that $$ \frac{λ_{2m}(Ω_m)}{λ_m(Ω_m)} >\frac{13}{5} >\frac{j_{1,1}^2}{j_{0,1}^2}, $$ where $j_{ν,1}$ is the first positive zero of $J_ν$, the Bessel function of the first kind of order $ν$. For $m=3$, the annulus $\{x\in\mathbb{R}^2:1/10<|x|<1\}$ already provides such a counterexample.

</details>

*Francesco Mantovani,Peter Schlosser*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究Clifford代数Hilbert模上双扇形右线性算子，建立谱投影分解理论，给出投影算子有界性的两个实用判据，并应用于梯度算子。

Method: 对右Hilbert模上的双扇形右线性算子T，构造与双扇形两个锥对应的谱投影P_±，从而将模分解为V=V_+⊕V_-、T分解为两个扇形算子T|_±；通过平方算子有界H^∞函数演算或m-增生性两个条件证明投影有界；再将结果用于非定常系数梯度算子∇_a，并对常系数情形在Fourier空间给出显式表示。

Abstract: We consider right-linear operators $T$ on a right Hilbert module $V$ over the Clifford algebra R_n, whose S-spectrum lies in an acute double sector. For these bisectorial operators, we introduce spectral projectors P_\pm associated with the two cones of the double sector. They decompose the Hilbert module into two submodules V=V_++V_-, and the bisectorial operator T into two sectorial operators T|_\pm. A crucial but non-trivial, cornerstone in this theory is the boundedness of the projectors P_\pm, which is, in turn connected to a bounded H^\infty-functional calculus of the operator T. We provide two practical criteria: either the squared operator admits a bounded H^\infty-functional calculus, or the operator is m-accretive. Finally, we apply these results to the gradient operator \nabla_a with nonconstant coefficients. For the particular gradient with constant coefficients, we are even able to derive explicit representations of the submodules V_\pm and the projectors P_\pm in Fourier space. Moreover, we identify the sign of the gradient operator with the Clifford-Hilbert transform. This sign plays a central role in the fractional powers of vector operators, which are used, for instance, in the non-local Fourier law of heat propagation.

</details>

Abstract: Let $X$ be a three-dimensional real Banach space and let $f:S_X\to S_Y$ be a surjective isometry. We study the propagation of an affine formula for $f$ on a relatively open part of $S_X$. A finite family of antipodal distance coordinates propagates a linear anchor except at three explicitly described degeneracies: a facet, an open face-star, or a family of chord cones with a common cylindrical kernel. We prove that, if the norm has no fixed-direction cylindrical open cone, every affine open anchor is linear and global. This yields a source-geometric criterion for the Mazur--Ulam property and covers, among other non-strictly-convex examples, the Euclidean double cone. We also give a segment-saturation criterion which proves the property for every prism $Z\oplus_\infty\mathbb R$, where $Z$ is an arbitrary real Banach plane. Finally, in the remaining cylindrical regime, we prove that a chord-saturated same-kernel network cannot be confined to one proper projective quotient arc. We then show that this multi-arc difficulty and the transverse-ruled part of producing an initial affine anchor reach the same final obstruction: upgrading norm calibration on an ambient open cone, together with calibration on a few spherical segments, to pointwise calibration of the sphere map on a two-dimensional open patch.

</details>

Abstract: For every nonzero real number $a,$ we prove that the integral Hankel operator on $L^2(\mathbb R_{+})$ with kernel $k(t)=t^{-1+ia}$ is noncompact quasinilpotent. Moreover, we obtain the corresponding Hankel operators on the Hardy space with symbols $\left((1-z)/(1+z)\right)^{ia}.$

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 严格下降集可决定加法半群 \(\langle k,\ell\rangle\)，完整数值不变量序列可决定无序对 \(\{k,\ell\}\)；由于坐标交换对称性，两个指数的顺序不可恢复。

Abstract: We study Yang's higher numerical invariants for the quasi-homogeneous submodules \[ M_{k,\ell}=[z^k-w^\ell]\subset H^2(\mathbb D^2), \qquad k,\ell\in\mathbb N,\quad k\neq\ell. \] The Hilbert--Schmidt property and the low-order invariants $Σ_0$ and $Σ_1$ for this family follow from earlier results on $M_{θ,\varphi}$-type submodules. Our aim is to determine the complete higher-order sequence. Using the weighted homogeneous decomposition associated with $z^k-w^\ell$, we construct explicit orthonormal bases for the two defect spaces and determine the exact support of the shifted defect inner products. For $j\geq1$, set \[ A_j=\left\lceil\frac{j}{\ell}\right\rceil, \qquad B_j=\left\lceil\frac{j}{k}\right\rceil. \] We obtain \[ Σ_0(M_{k,\ell})=\frac{π^2}{6}, \qquad Σ_j(M_{k,\ell})=F(A_j,B_j), \] where $F$ is an explicitly evaluated symmetric function on $\mathbb N^2$. The sequence is nonincreasing, tends to zero, and satisfies $Σ_j>Σ_{j+1} \Longleftrightarrow k\mid j\ \text{or}\ \ell\mid j$. Hence its strict descent set is $k\mathbb N\cup\ell\mathbb N$. This descent set determines the additive semigroup $\langle k,\ell\rangle$, while the complete numerical invariant sequence determines the unordered pair $\{k,\ell\}$. Abstract: We study Lipschitz mappings associated with group actions by similarities, extending the orbit-quotient approach for point-preserving isometric actions. In particular, we prove that the space of positively homogeneous Lipschitz functions form a $1$-complemented subspace of the Lipschitz space (strenghthening the earlier existed result) and interpret existing projections onto linear and bilinear mappings as invariant-mean averages. For amenable groups, the same approach yields isometric realizations of the quotient spaces in the biduals of the original linearization spaces.

Abstract: We prove a Kolmogorov--Riesz compactness theorem for Banach lattices over spaces of homogeneous type. Especially, we consider the one for $L^1$. The result requires neither the Vitali covering property nor continuity assumptions on the measure of balls, lower volume bounds, or finiteness of the underlying measure space, thereby extending several recent compactness criteria in the literature.

We then establish an approximation theorem based on Christ's dyadic cubes and use it to characterize the closure of compactly supported continuous functions in general ball Banach function spaces. Applications include compactness criteria for rearrangement-invariant Banach function spaces and Morrey spaces. In the Euclidean setting, we also identify the resulting approximation space with the classical heat-semigroup (equivalently, mollifier) closure.

</details>


### [20] [The Complex Banach Isometric Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26286)
*Xinan Dai,Wendao Deng,Yidong Shi,Tailin Wu,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Banach 关于“固定维度子空间均等距则可赋 Hilbert 结构”猜测的复情形：复 $(n+1)$-空间中若所有复超平面截口互相复线性等价，则凸体必为 Hermitian 椭球，进而推出复 Banach 空间结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banach 提出：若对某个大于 1 的固定维度，赋范空间的所有该维子空间都线性等距，则该空间必为 Hilbert 空间。实情形已有进展，本文旨在解决其复情形。

Result: 得到精确截面映射的全局 Lipschitz 族；证明 $p_S^{2n+2}$ 与 $p_S^{2n+4}$ 是实齐次多项式，唯一分解迫使 $p_S^2$ 为二次型，相位不变性使其成为 Hermitian 型；凸体被证明是 Hermitian 椭球，并由此得到复 Banach 空间的一般结论。

Conclusion: 复情形的 Banach 问题获得肯定答案：复空间若某固定大于 1 维度的所有子空间均复线性等距，则空间为 Hilbert 空间。

Abstract: Banach asked whether a normed space must be Hilbert if, for one fixed dimension greater than one, all subspaces of that dimension are linearly isometric. We prove the complex case. By the codimension-one reduction, the essential finite-dimensional problem is to characterize a balanced convex body in a complex $(n+1)$-space whose complex hyperplane sections are all complex-linearly equivalent. We show that such a body is a Hermitian ellipsoid. The proof adapts the recent bundle--degree method of Lu and Yang for the real problem, with two features specific to the complex setting. After covariance normalization, write $G=\operatorname{Aut}{\mathbb C}(S)$ for the complex-linear symmetry group of a model section $S$. The exact section maps form a principal $G$-bundle over $S^{2n+1}$; reduction to $G^{\circ}$ places its obstruction in $π{2n}(G^{\circ})$, which is finite. Pulling back by a suitable positive-degree self-map trivializes this bundle and yields a global Lipschitz family of exact complex-linear section maps. Brouwer degree and a signed degree formula then imply that $p_S^{2n+2}$ and $p_S^{2n+4}$ are real homogeneous polynomials, where $p_S$ is the norm of a model section. Unique factorization forces $p_S^2$ to be quadratic, and phase invariance makes the resulting quadratic form Hermitian. The parallelogram identity then yields the general complex Banach-space statement.

</details>


### [21] [Sharp $p/\log p$ Bounds for Metric $X_p$ Inequalities and Arbitrary Rademacher Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26287)
*Yutong Zhang,Yaoran Yang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在Hamming立方体上、均值零函数的固定基数子集期望范数不等式，其p依赖的最优阶为 p/log p，从而匹配Naor所问的 $X_p$ 度量不等式的下界，得到最优上界 $O(p/\log p)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Naor证明了 $L_p$ 满足度量 $X_p$ 不等式并给出锐利的尺度参数，但询问其常数对 $p$ 的最优依赖。此前已知上界为 $O(p^4/\log p)$，而下界由一次混沌给出，阶为 $p/\log p$，二者存在显著差距，因此需要确定最优上界。

Method: 证明结合了固定基数下的锐利Rosenthal比较，以及精确重构恒等式 $h=\sum_j D_j\Delta^{-1}h$；随后通过对Walsh热半群的逐点反向Poincaré不等式进行积分，得到与维数无关的逆梯度平方函数估计；最后应用Naor的transferrence论证。

Result: 得到了匹配的下界与上界：当 $h$ 为Hamming立方体上均值零标量函数、$p\geq2$、$S$ 均匀分布于 $[n]$ 的 $k$ 子集时，证明了 $(\mathbb{E}_S\|\mathbb{E}_{[n]\setminus S}h\|_p^p)^{1/p}\lesssim \frac{p}{\log p}[\frac{k}{n}\sum_{j=1}^n\|\partial_jh\|_p^p+(\frac{k}{n})^{p/2}\|h\|_p^p]^{1/p}$，并由此通过Naor的转移动论证得到度量 $X_p$ 的最优界 $O(p/\log p)$。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了 $L_p$ 的度量 $X_p$ 不等式常数对 $p$ 的最优依赖，将上界从 $O(p^4/\log p)$ 改进到与下界匹配的 $O(p/\log p)$，解决了Naor提出的问题。

Abstract: Naor proved that $L_p$ satisfies the metric $X_p$ inequality with the sharp scaling parameter and asked for the optimal dependence of its constant on $p$. The best previously known upper bound was $O(p^4/\log p)$, while first-degree chaos gives a lower bound of order $p/\log p$. We prove the matching upper bound. More generally, if $h$ is a mean-zero scalar function on the Hamming cube, $p\geq2$, and $S$ is uniformly distributed over the $k$-subsets of $[n]$, then \[
  \bigl(\E_S\|\E_{[n]\setminus S}h\|_p^p\bigr)^{1/p}
  \lesssim \frac p{\log p}
  \left[\frac{k}{n}\sum_{j=1}^n\|\partial_jh\|_p^p+
  \left(\frac{k}{n}\right)^{p/2}\|h\|_p^p\right]^{1/p}. \] The proof combines a sharp fixed-cardinality Rosenthal comparison with the exact reconstruction $h=\sum_jD_jΔ^{-1}h$. A dimension-free inverse-gradient square-function estimate follows by integrating a pointwise reverse Poincaré inequality for the Walsh heat semigroup. Naor's transference argument then yields the optimal metric bound $O(p/\log p)$.

</details>

Conclusion: 该分析完整刻画了带正现金的联合行权美式condor的最优行权结构与边界性质，为定价和风险管理提供理论基础与数值依据。

Abstract: We study a jointly exercised American condor contract whose payoff is a call condor payoff plus a positive cash amount. Its plateau and positive tails produce two inner exercise boundaries and two outer cash-exercise boundaries. We construct the unique bounded solution of the associated variational inequality, strong on each side of the plateau, and identify it with the optimal stopping value. We prove monotonicity and continuity of the four boundaries and determine their limits at expiry. Following Broadie and Detemple, we express the inner boundaries as cutoffs of the boundaries of uncapped contracts with the same cash components. We also prove call-side convexity and volatility monotonicity under a sufficient condition, solve the stationary problems explicitly and present numerical results.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes--Cahn--Hilliard (NSCH) system in domains perforated by no-slip obstacles of diameter \(O(\varepsilon^3)\) separated by distances of order \(\varepsilon\). Both viscosity and mobility may depend on the phase variable. At this critical Stokes-capacity scale, the limiting velocity \(\boldsymbol u\) and phase field \(φ\) satisfy an NSCH system with the additional Brinkman resistance \(ν(φ)\mathbf B\boldsymbol u\), where \(\mathbf B\) is determined by the exterior Stokes capacity of the reference obstacle. The principal analytical difficulty is the interaction between the phase-dependent viscosity and the order-one energy concentration of the critical Stokes correctors. The variational chemical-potential identity yields, after scalar extension, strong convergence of the phase in \(L^2(0,T;H^1(Ω))\), and hence strong convergence of the phase-dependent viscosity in the same topology. Combined with a cellwise Hardy multiplier estimate, this compactness can be transferred through the concentrated corrector layer. The resulting weighted-capacity statement applies to uniformly positive and bounded coefficients with \(L^2(0,T;H^1(Ω))\) regularity and simultaneously identifies the effective Brinkman force and the corresponding viscous dissipation lower bound. We also investigate a vanishing capillary coefficient \(λ_\varepsilon\to0\). After the natural velocity scaling, the limit is an unsteady Stokes--Brinkman equation coupled to an unadvected Cahn--Hilliard equation.

</details>

Motivation: 研究洛伦兹-闵可夫斯基空间中σ_k(A[u])=u^p型类空图的整体性质，旨在在尽量弱的假设下获得高度、梯度的先验界以及刘维尔型刚性结论。

Abstract: We study nonnegative entire spacelike graphs in Lorentz--Minkowski space satisfying $σ_k(A[u])=u^p$, with $h_{ij}=-Wu_{ij}$ and $W=(1-|Du|^2)^{-1/2}$. Abstract: Nirenberg's theorem gives interior $C^{2,α}$ regularity for continuous viscosity solutions of fully nonlinear uniformly elliptic equations $F(D^2u)=0$ in dimension two. Assuming a suitable relation between $\mathcal{F}$ and the $κ$-th power of the principal part of $E_{λ_1}$ near this eigenspace, we describe the behavior of $E_{λ_1}$ according to the relations $pκ<q$, $pκ=q$, or $pκ>q$. In particular, the functional is unbounded from below in the first case, and has a negative infimum in the last case. We then study how sufficiently small $q$-homogeneous perturbations of $\mathcal{F}$ influence the geometry of $E_λ$. In this way, we describe assumptions guaranteeing the existence of three critical points in a left neighborhood of $λ_1$ and two critical points in a right neighborhood of $λ_1$, which indicates an $S$-shaped structure of the solution set. The results are applied to double-phase functionals and the nonlinear Fredholm alternative.

The key results enclose the second order regularity of harmonic mappings, the Bochner identity, the weak Harnack estimates for coordinate functions of a harmonic mapping, as well as the Caccioppoli estimates and the Liouville type theorem. Furthermore, we discuss several examples of (Grushin) harmonic mappings and, in particular, relate them to holomorphic mappings in the plane.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 强剪切极限下双稳前波的传播由退化极限方程与剪切的非退化性共同决定；双稳反应的变号特性带来中间平衡态分裂风险，需借助不稳定性与比较论证排除。双稳情形中极限波速的符号对剪切极不敏感/不定，与燃烧情形形成鲜明对比，剪切可在大振幅下反转传播方向。

Abstract: We study the strong-shear limit of bistable traveling fronts in infinite cylinders with periodic transverse boundary conditions. We prove that, for every sufficiently regular periodic shear profile, the front speed normalized by the flow amplitude converges as the amplitude tends to infinity. After suitable longitudinal rescaling and translations, the corresponding profiles converge along subsequences to full fronts of the limiting degenerate equation. Furthermore, under a Hörmander-type non-degeneracy condition on the shear, the limiting front is proved to be regular and unique up to translation, and the whole normalized family converges uniformly. A main difficulty is that the sign-changing bistable reaction allows the limiting transition to split through intermediate transverse equilibria. We rule out this possibility by exploiting the instability of such equilibria together with suitable regularization and comparison arguments. Finally, in contrast with the combustion case, where every nonconstant mean-zero shear yields a positive limiting speed, we construct an example showing that, for a fixed bistable reaction, smooth mean-zero shears can produce negative, zero, or positive limiting speeds. In particular, the example shows that a shear which accelerates propagation at small amplitudes may reverse the propagation direction when its amplitude becomes large.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构造Green势热流：由当前映射的基于能量的源项在每个时刻重构共形因子，得到抛物-椭圆耦合系统。对H^3初值证明局部适定性并给出基于能量集中的延拓准则；通过大耦合参数排除有限时间气泡获得整体解；利用能量恒等式、集中图表局部化、流的packet图表表示及气泡树分解分析极限能量。

Result: H^3初值局部适定且具能量集中型延拓准则；耦合足够大时可排除有限时间bubbling并得到整体解；无穷时刻能量恒等式仅给出张力场的加权L^2控制；在集中图表的正则区域沿指定时刻子列得到调和轮廓，可表示为packet图表中的有限气泡树，极限能量分解为调和分量能量与neck能量；临界归一化径向例子显示该流可避免有限时间爆破并产生具no-neck性质的和气泡。

Conclusion: Green势热流将重正化机制嵌入调和映射热流而不改变Dirichlet能量与调和临界点，提供了控制二维能量集中与长时间气泡形成的有效框架，并在大耦合及临界径向情形下验证了防爆破与no-neck气泡现象。

Abstract: We introduce a Green--potential heat flow for maps from a closed Riemannian surface. Inspired by Parker--Wolfson renormalization~\cite{ParkerWolfson1993,Parker1996}, the conformal factor is reconstructed at each time from an energy-based source determined by the current map. The resulting parabolic--elliptic system incorporates renormalization into the evolution while leaving the Dirichlet energy functional and its harmonic critical points unchanged. We establish local well-posedness for $H^3$ initial data and a continuation criterion in terms of energy concentration. For sufficiently large coupling, we exclude finite-time bubbling and obtain global solutions. At infinite time, the energy identity provides only weighted $L^2$ control of the tension field. Localizing to regular regions of concentration charts yields harmonic profiles along subsequences of prescribed times. We represent these profiles in the flow's packet charts, organize them into a finite bubble tree, and decompose the limiting energy into harmonic component energies and neck energies. A critically normalized radial example illustrates how the modified flow can prevent finite-time blow-up and produce harmonic bubbles with no-neck property.

</details>

Abstract: Given three normed spaces $X\_0 , X\_1 , \tilde{X\_1}$, with $\tilde{X\_1}$ a closed subspace of $X\_1$ with same norm, how does $[X\_0 , X\_1 ]\_θ$ compare to $[X\_0 , X\_1 ]\_θ\,? This classical problem of interpolation theory has been mostly considered in the case of finite codimensional subspaces. We show here that quotient J norms are a useful tool which allows to tackle several relevant cases.

Abstract: We establish a structure theorem for measures satisfying left-invariant PDE constraints in Carnot groups. Our generalization, that takes inspiration from the paper arxiv:1601.06543 by the first named author and F. Rindler, requires the introduction of the hypoelliptic wave cone, a sub-Riemannian version of the wave cone from the theory of Compensated Compactness, and it employs classical results in Harmonic Analysis on homogeneous spaces. Then, we utilize this result to extend the Rank-one Theorem for functions of bounded horizontal variation ($BV_H$) to all Carnot groups.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider variational image denoising with spatially dependent Orlicz regularization and introduce an Orlicz--Sobolev approximation of the ROF model based on a scaled $L\log L$-type density. For a general class of uniformly superlinear integrands satisfying a $Δ_2$-condition, we establish existence and uniqueness of minimizers and derive a Fenchel dual problem with dual attainment and pointwise optimality conditions. We then specialize the framework to a logarithmic density whose correction is activated above a prescribed local gradient scale. For this model, we obtain explicit expressions of the Fenchel dual and optimality conditions as well as a pointwise radial formula for the dual proximal map involving the Lambert $W$-function. As the logarithmic parameter tends to zero, we prove equicoercivity and $Γ$-convergence to the ROF functional, together with convergence of the corresponding minimizers. Numerical illustrations indicate that the logarithmic correction can reduce staircasing for diffuse transitions, while retaining behavior comparable to ROF for sharp interfaces and a natural image.

Abstract: Buoyancy points straight upward, yet in the absence of external forces, a rising air bubble may trace a spiral through the water. This phenomenon was already observed by Leonardo da Vinci. For every nonnegative inner-to-outer density ratio and every sufficiently small positive axial Weber number, we construct smooth near-spherical bubbles and drops with compact interfaces and phasewise irrotational flow. They are stationary in a frame translating along and rotating about a fixed axis, while their centroids lie off that axis and therefore trace genuine circular helices in laboratory coordinates. The mathematical construction exploits an overdetermined free-boundary formulation, its variational structure, and an $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction. Particular difficulties in the form of resonances arise at a discrete set of density ratios, including the frequently studied vacuum case with vanishing inner density. Complementary to our existence result, in the absence of surface tension and if the inner density is not larger than the outer density we prove that the model admits neither nontrivial translating relative equilibria nor relative equilibria with helical centroid trajectories. As a byproduct, we show that every regular finite-energy stationary vortex sheet of spherical topology for the homogeneous three-dimensional Euler equations, with irrotational flow on both sides, is trivial.

As an application, we establish the existence of ground state solutions for a class of quasilinear elliptic equations on Riemannian vector bundles with generalized \((p,q)\)-growth. The proposed framework unifies several important geometric operators, including the Bochner \(p\)-Laplacian, the \((p,q)\)-Bochner Laplacian, and prescribed mean curvature-type operators.

Established classification theorems then leave only the constant, pure-jump, triple-junction, and crack-tip models. The planar interior regularity conjecture follows from the known equivalence between this classification and local regularity. No homogeneity assumption is imposed on the generalized global minimizer.

</details>

Abstract: In a recent preprint (arXiv:2511.09556), we exhibited an inverse energy cascade for the 3D Navier-Stokes equations which results in "instantaneous" Type I blow-up and failure of uniqueness in certain sharp regularity classes. Our purpose here is to elucidate this phenomenon further in the setting of the Obukhov dyadic model, and to compare it to the better-known phenomenon of finite-time blow-up. When intermittency is low ($α\leq 2$), we recover our previous result from the Navier-Stokes setting; in the energy-supercritical case where intermittency is high ($α>2$), we show that the same can occur in the class of finite energy solutions. We present two different proofs: a soft approach using a Lyapunov function and an explicit multiscale construction. For the inviscid system we illustrate that a similar phenomenon occurs at lower regularity. Finally, we state a finite-time blow-up theorem for a mixed Desnyansky-Novikov-Obukhov model, in which a forward cascade from finitely supported data and no force produces a singularity that is of Type II only by a small margin, with features comparable to the recent forced Navier-Stokes blow-up of OpenAI.

</details>

Abstract: We consider an action of an inverse semigroup on a C*-algebra $A$ and use it to construct a groupoid of germs with unit space the spectrum of $A$. Motivated by the representation theory of C*-algebras of groupoids, we construct a concrete family of covariant representations for the action. We use this family to give new definitions of the reduced and essential crossed product C*-algebras that avoid, respectively, passing to the double commutant and local multiplier algebra of $A$. Our reduced crossed product is isomorphic to the one defined by Exel, Buss and Meyer, and when the inverse semigroup is quasi-countable our essential crossed product is isomorphic to the one defined by Kwaśniewski and Meyer.

Abstract: Using operator-valued subordination, we derive a formula for the $2$-norm distance on reflexive lattices generated by double triangles of projections under freeness assumptions on their graph coefficients, and provide explicit evaluations in both the scalar and free-projection cases.

### [56] [Solution to Dixmier's Problem about spectra of C*-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26319)
*Martino Lupini*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文解决了Dixmier 1967年关于单C*-代数谱的问题：构造了两类单、可分、含单位、精确、反初等的C*-代数A（核）与B（非核），证明不存在从B的谱到A的谱的Borel可定义单射；并回答Simon Thomas的问题，证明任意可数顺从群H的酉对偶与F_∞的酉对偶之间不存在Borel可定义单射；同时反向证明任意两个可分核非I型C*-代数的谱之间存在Borel可定义双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dixmier在1967年提出关于单C*-代数谱结构的问题，属于算子代数与描述集合论交叉的核心难题。作者旨在通过构造具有不同逼近性质（核与非核）的单C*-代数，揭示其谱在Borel可定义意义下不可互相嵌入的现象，并回答Simon Thomas关于自由群酉对偶嵌入的问题，同时刻画核非I型C*-代数谱的统一性。

Method: 取Γ=SL_3(Z)，K为其模2^n同余核的完备化，A为典型反交换关系（CAR）代数，B=C(K)⋊_rΓ为约化交叉积。利用描述集合论中的Borel可定义函数与Borel提升概念，结合Γ的刚性性质与不变测度技巧，构造纯态空间上的概率测度，证明任何Borel可定义映射的Borel提升几乎处处落入单一酉等价类；并借助顺从群与F_∞的酉对偶比较，证明不存在Borel可定义单射。最后通过统一的Borel可定义双射论证核非I型情形的普适性。

Result: A与B均为单、可分、含单位、精确、反初等C*-代数，A是核的而B是非核的；不存在从B的谱到A的谱的Borel可定义单射，且存在纯态空间上的概率测度使任何Borel提升几乎处处取值于单一酉等价类。对任意可数顺从群H，不存在从F_∞酉对偶到H酉对偶的Borel可定义单射，并存在固定概率测度使Borel提升几乎处处落入单一酉等价类。相反地，任意两个可分核非I型C*-代数的谱之间存在Borel可定义双射。

Conclusion: 核与非核性质在谱的Borel可定义结构上产生本质差异：非核单C*-代数的谱无法Borel可定义地嵌入核情形，且存在测度集中现象；该结论亦适用于自由群与顺从群酉对偶的比较。另一方面，所有可分核非I型C*-代数的谱在Borel可定义意义下彼此同构，表明核非I型类具有统一的谱分类。

Abstract: We solve Dixmier's 1967 problem about spectra of simple $C^*$-algebras. A function between spectra is called Borel-definable when it is induced by a Borel map between standard Borel spaces of unitary representations. Let $Γ=\mathrm{SL}_3(\mathbb{Z})$, let $K$ be its completion with respect to the congruence kernels modulo $2^n$, let $A$ be the canonical anticommutation relations (CAR) algebra, and let $B=C(K)\rtimes_rΓ$ be the reduced crossed product. The algebras $A$ and $B$ are simple, separable, unital, exact, and antiliminary; $A$ is nuclear, whereas $B$ is nonnuclear. There is no Borel-definable injection from the spectrum of $B$ to the spectrum of $A$. In fact, there is a probability measure on the pure-state space of $B$ such that every Borel lift of a Borel-definable function from the spectrum of $B$ to the spectrum of $A$ takes values in a single unitary-equivalence class almost everywhere.
  Answering a question of Simon Thomas, we also prove that, for every countable amenable group $H$, there is no Borel-definable injection from the unitary dual of the free group $F_\infty $ on infinitely many generators to the unitary dual of $H$. There is a fixed probability measure on a family of infinite-dimensional irreducible representations of $F_\infty$ such that every Borel lift of a Borel-definable function takes values in a single unitary-equivalence class almost everywhere.
  We also show that, in contrast, the spectra of any two separable nuclear non-type-I $C^*$-algebras admit a Borel-definable bijection.

</details>


### [57] [Coarse rigidity in von Neumann algebras via derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26496)
*Manish Kumar,Melchior Wirth*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文提出σ-有限von Neumann代数的相对粗刚性概念，借助模导子与GNS对称量子Markov半群，将Peterson的L²-刚性推广到非迹情形，并证明相对可均直和项缺失导致形变在超幂中相对交换子上收敛，对可分预对偶的扩散代数给出可均直和项与中心序列刚性的二分法，统一处理丰满性、相对ω-固体性与相对稳定固体性，并应用于融合自由积、算子值自由Araki-Woods因子与余圈交叉积等例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peterson的L²-刚性框架依赖迹结构，难以直接处理一般σ-有限von Neumann代数。论文旨在突破迹假设，在非迹（nontracial）情形下建立相对粗刚性理论，并统一刻画多种不可分解性性质。

Method: 通过模导子（modular derivations）及其关联的GNS对称量子Markov半群来定义相对粗刚性；利用超幂（ultrapowers）中的相对交换子分析形变的收敛行为，并将其与相对可均直和项的存在性联系起来。

Result: 主结果：相对可均直和项缺失迫使形变在超幂的相对交换子上收敛。对可分预对偶的扩散von Neumann代数，得到可均直和项存在性与中心序列刚性之间的二分法。该框架可统一证明丰满性、相对ω-固体性与相对稳定固体性等不可分解性。

Conclusion: 论文给出非迹情形下相对粗刚性的统一方法，推广了L²-刚性，并提供可验证的不可分解性判据；方法适用于融合自由积、算子值自由Araki-Woods因子和余圈交叉积等具体例子。

Abstract: We propose a notion of relative coarse rigidity for $σ$-finite von Neumann algebras through modular derivations and their associated GNS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups, extending Peterson's framework of $L^2$-rigidity to the nontracial setting. Our main result shows that the absence of relatively amenable summands forces the associated deformations to converge on relative commutants in ultrapowers. For diffuse von Neumann algebras with separable predual, this yields a dichotomy between the presence of an amenable summand and rigidity in terms of central sequences. These results give a novel unified approach for proving indecomposability properties such as fullness, relative $ω$-solidity and relative stable solidity. ### [62] [Carmichael numbers of the form $2^n p^m+1$ with odd exponent $m$: refinements of a theorem of Florian Luca, a general independence lemma, and a computational study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25104)
*Pagdame Tiebekabe*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Florian Luca关于Carmichael数集$\mathcal{K}$（使$2^n k+1$为Carmichael数的奇数$k$）的研究基础上，从三个方向推广其结果：给出显式定量界、将乘法独立性步骤推广到任意偶基$b$，并通过穷举计算报告小范围结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Luca证明：固定奇数$m\ge5$时，仅有有限多个素数$p$满足$p^m\in\mathcal{K}$。本文作为对他的纪念，旨在仅依赖其已发表工作，进一步推广这一结果。

Method: 第一，从Luca的论证中提取显式定量界；第二，将其乘法独立性步骤从基2推广到任意偶基$b$；第三，进行穷举计算验证小范围内$2^n p^m+1$形式的Carmichael数。

Result: （1）定量界：任意$N=2^n p^m+1$（$m$奇数）满足$n<2^{2\cdot10^7(m+1)^2m^2(\log p)^2}$；若$p>m$则$n<25m^4\log p$；若$p>500m^5\log m$则$\omega(N)\le26m^7$，且此类Carmichael数至多$O_m(x^{1/m})$个。（2）推广：$b$偶数、$m\ge3$奇数且与$b$互素、$p>m$素数、$N=b^n p^m+1$为Carmichael数时，$b^n p^m$与$N$的任何素因子对应的$b^a p^\beta$乘法独立。（3）计算：$2^n p^m+1\le10^{18}$、$3\le m\le37$奇数时唯一例子为$1729=2^6\cdot3^3+1$；$5\le m\le37$无例子（$5\le m\le15$推广至$10^{21}$）；偶数$4\le m\le36$唯一例子为$46657=2^6\cdot3^6+1=13\cdot37\cdot97$。

Conclusion: 本文在Luca有限性结果基础上给出了更精细的定量刻画、将核心方法推广到偶基情形，并以计算补充了小范围验证，确认了少数具体例子。

Abstract: Florian Luca devoted the last paper of his life to the set $\mathcal K$ of odd positive integers $k$ such that $2^n k+1$ is a Carmichael number for some positive integer $n$. He proved that if $m\ge 5$ is fixed and odd, then there are only finitely many primes $p$ with $p^m\in\mathcal K$. In this note, written as a small tribute to his memory, we extend his result in three directions, using only his published work as our reference base. First, we extract from his arguments explicit quantitative bounds: every Carmichael number $N=2^n p^m+1$ with $m$ odd satisfies $n<2^{2\cdot10^7(m+1)^2m^2(\log p)^2}$, and if $p>m$ the much sharper bound $n<25m^4\log p$ holds; for $p>500m^5\log m$ we further record the uniform bounds $ω(N)\le 26m^7$ on the number of prime factors. As an application, the number of such Carmichael numbers up to $x$ is $O_m(x^{1/m})$. Second, we generalize the multiplicative independence step of Luca's proof from base $2$ to an arbitrary even base $b$: if $b$ is even, $m\ge3$ is odd and coprime to $b$, $p>m$ is prime and $N=b^n p^m+1$ is Carmichael, then $b^n p^m$ and $b^a p^β$ are multiplicatively independent whenever $b^a p^β+1$ is a prime factor of $N$. Third, we report an exhaustive computation showing that the only Carmichael number $2^n p^m+1\le 10^{18}$ with odd $3\le m\le 37$ is $1729=2^6\cdot 3^3+1$, that no such number exists with odd $5\le m\le 37$ (extended to $10^{21}$ for $5\le m\le 15$), and that the only example with even $4\le m\le 36$ is $46657=2^6\cdot 3^6+1=13\cdot 37\cdot 97$, where $3^6=27^2$ is the square of the smallest element of $\mathcal K$.

</details>

Abstract: The Lie algebra of multiple zeta values is realized as the space $\operatorname{ARI}^{\operatorname{pol}}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}$ of alternal moulds whose swap is alternil up to a constant mould, equipped with the $ari$ bracket. We propose an explicit formula for a Lie bracket $uri$ on this space. Moreover, we prove that the Lie algebra $\operatorname{ARI}^{\operatorname{pol}}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}$ embeds into $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$, with the $ari$ bracket translating directly into the $uri$ bracket. Finally, we examine the associated-depth graded of $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$, extending the known depth-graded setup for $\operatorname{ARI}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}^{\operatorname{pol}}$.

Abstract: Let $\mathbb{P}$ be the set of primes. Suppose that $P_{i}$, $i=1,2,\ldots,9$ are nine subsets of $\mathbb{P}$ with relative lower density $\underline{d}_{\mathbb{P}}(P_{i})=μ_{i}$. When $\sum_{i=1}^{9} μ_{i}^{3}>8$, we prove that every sufficiently large odd integer can be written as $n=\sum_{i=1}^{9} p_{i}^{3}$ with $p_{i} \in P_{i}$, $i=1,2,\ldots,9$.

</details>

In particular, we derive some new identities for weighted binomial sums involving harmonic numbers and central binomial coefficients.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 通过 Poisson 随机模型成功刻画了 $h(\varphi(n))$ 的平均阶，深化了对不同指数计数函数在 totient 函数复合下分布规律的理解。

Abstract: Following Erdős (1982) and Sanna (2019), we study the arithmetic function $h(n)$, which is defined to be the number of distinct exponents in the prime factorization of a positive integer $n$. Among other things, we show that $$ \sum_{n\leq x}h(φ(n)) \asymp x\left(\frac{\log\log x}{\log\log\log x}\right)^{1/2}, $$ where $φ$ is the Euler totient function. The key ingredient is the Poisson random model for $ω(n,T)$, the number of the prime divisors of $n$ in a given subset of primes $T$, which was introduced in a recent work of Ford.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 建立了任意多项式基下受限数字不可约多项式的通用渐近框架，说明限制结构（而非仅禁止集大小）通过允许数字中单位的相对密度影响主项，推广并强化了函数域缺失数字问题的已有结果。

Abstract: We study monic irreducible polynomials over $\mathbb{F}_q[T]$ whose non-leading digits, with respect to an arbitrary polynomial base $b(T)$, avoid a prescribed set of forbidden digits. Identifying the digit set with the ring $D=\mathbb{F}_q[T]/(b)$, we obtain an asymptotic formula for the number of such irreducible polynomials under conditions given in terms of Fourier parameters of the allowed digit set. The main term contains a singular series measuring the relative density of units among the allowed digits, while the error term is controlled by both pointwise and averaged Fourier estimates. As a consequence, we obtain a general criterion depending only on the cardinality of the forbidden set, as well as stronger results for structured restrictions, including examples in which the forbidden set contains a positive proportion of all digits. In particular, we treat additive cosets, restrictions compatible with the Chinese remainder decomposition of $D$, and coefficient-wise restrictions. The proof adapts the function field circle method for restricted coefficients to arbitrary polynomial bases.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a broad generalization of a recursive formula for the Möbius function $μ(n) = 1-n-\sum_{d=2}^{n-1}μ(d)\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]$ due to George Spencer-Brown. By replacing the greatest integer function $\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]$ in this classical recurrence with an arbitrary arithmetic function $f\left(\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]\right)$ with $f(1)=1$, we define a new generalized family of functions, denoted $\star(n)$. We prove that $\star(p) = -1$ if and only if $p$ is prime. This result yields a surprising algebraic characterization of primes and reveals a deep structural property underlying divisor sums and the greatest integer function.

</details>

Conclusion: 获得了正特征GSpin Shimura簇上特殊除子的S-整性定理，并证明形式特殊自同态的代数性，为将丢番图逼近方法引入正特征Shimura簇算术问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: We prove an $S$-integrality theorem for special divisors on GSpin Shimura varieties in positive characteristic. Let $C$ be a generically ordinary curve in such a Shimura variety not contained in any special divisor. Then, for any increasing sequence of prime-to-$p$ positive integers $m_i$ and any finite set of closed points $S\subset C$, we prove that $C\setminus S$ meets the special divisor $Z(m_i)$ for all but finitely many $i$. The key new input is an algebraicity theorem for formal special endomorphisms which allows us to use techniques from Diophantine approximation. We prove this algebraicity theorem using a punctual monodromy theorem and a positive-characteristic analogue of the Mumford--Tate conjecture.

### [73] [On the positivity of truncated pentagonal number series and some conjectures of Merca](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25739)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将指数映射 m↦m(ν₂(m)+2) 视为正整数上的动力系统；沿其前向轨道把相关商的因子串成链，并证明由 1、4、5 起始的三条轨道两两不交且望远镜化后恰好等于 1/((1−q)(1−q⁴)(1−q⁵))；利用 Liu 工作中指数三元组 (1,4,5) 的可容许性，得到两个非负系数级数的分解。所用工具涉及 2-adic 赋值 ν₂ 与 N_m=m(1+ν₂(m)/2)。

Result: 对由 ∏_{m≥1}(1−q^{2N_m}) 与 ∏_{r≥1}(q^{2^r r};q^{2^{r+1}r})_∞ 加权的截断五边形数级数，成功证明其非负性，从而验证了 Merca 的四个猜想。

Conclusion: 轨道望远镜技术为这类正性结果提供了一种与 Pólya–Szegő 判别法互补的新机制，扩展了现有文献中处理截断五边形数级数正性的方法框架。

Abstract: Let $ν_2(m)$ denote the $2$-adic valuation of a positive integer $m$ and set $N_m=m\bigl(1+ν_2(m)/2\bigr)$. We prove four conjectures of Merca on the nonnegativity of truncated pentagonal number series weighted by the infinite products $\prod_{m\ge 1}(1-q^{2N_m})$ and $\prod_{r\ge 1}\bigl(q^{2^r r};q^{2^{r+1}r}\bigr)_\infty$. Our method is to regard the exponent map $m\mapsto m(ν_2(m)+2)$ as a dynamical system on the positive integers: the factors of the associated quotient link into chains along its forward orbits, and the three orbits seeded at $1$, $4$ and $5$ are pairwise disjoint and telescope to exactly $1/\bigl((1-q)(1-q^4)(1-q^5)\bigr)$. The exponent triple $(1,4,5)$ is admissible in the sense of earlier work by Liu, which yields the desired factorization into two series with nonnegative coefficients. This orbit telescoping technique appears to be a mechanism complementary to the Pólya--Szeg\H o criterion that underlies most existing positivity results of this kind in the literature.

</details>


### [74] [Irrationality Exponents and Partial Quotient Growth in Continued Fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25812)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连分数的度量理论中,部分商$a_{n+1}(x)$的增长率与收敛分母$q_n(x)$之间的关系是核心问题。已有工作分别研究了无理指数(Bugeaud)、部分商增长(Wang--Wu)以及收敛指数与无理指数的联合分布(Song--Tan--Zhang),但将$a_{n+1}$的对数尺度增长与相对$q_n$的上增长速率这一对指标联合刻画尚未解决。

Method: 设$\psi(n)\to\infty$及$\alpha,\beta\in[0,\infty]$,定义联合水平集 $F_{\alpha,\beta}=\{x:\liminf \log a_{n+1}(x)/\psi(n)=\alpha,\ \limsup \log a_{n+1}(x)/\log q_n(x)=\beta\}$,通过连分数的度量理论工具(如质量分布原理与网覆盖)对所有$\alpha,\beta$求出其Hausdorff维数。

Result: 对所有$\alpha,\beta\in[0,\infty]$给出了联合水平集$F_{\alpha,\beta}$的Hausdorff维数的完整表达式,统一并推广了Bugeaud、Wang--Wu及Song--Tan--Zhang的先前结果。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对连分数部分商增长与收敛分母之间联合度量行为的理解,并作为框架涵盖了此前多个关于无理指数、部分商增长及联合分布的经典结论。

Abstract: Let $[a_1(x),a_2(x),\ldots,a_n(x),\ldots]$ be the continued fraction expansion of irrational $x\in[0,1)$, and let $q_n(x)$ be the denominator of the $n$-th convergent. In this paper, we study how the growth rate of $a_{n+1}(x)$ on a prescribed logarithmic size interacts with its upper growth rate relative to $q_n(x)$. For $ψ:\mathbb N\to\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ satisfying $ψ(n)\to \infty$ and $α, β\in [0, \infty]$, define the joint level set
  \[F_{α,β}:=\Big\{x\in [0,1)\colon \liminf_{n\to\infty}\frac{\log (a_{n+1}(x))}{ψ(n)}=α,\ \limsup_{n\to\infty}\frac{\log (a_{n+1}(x))}{\log q_n(x)}=β\Big\}.
  \]
  We determine the Hausdorff dimension of $F_{α,β}$ for all values of $ α$ and $β$. Our results is related to several earlier results on the metric theory of continued fractions, including those of Bugeaud [Math. Ann. {327} (2003)] on irrationality exponents, Wang--Wu [Adv. Math. {218} (2008)] on the growth of partial quotients, and Song--Tan--Zhang [Nonlinearity {37} (2024)] on the joint distribution of convergence and irrationality exponents.

</details>

Abstract: We develop Hachimori's study on the unramified Iwasawa modules using extensions with restricted $p$-ramification. Let $K$ be an algebraic number field, and $p$ a prime number which splits into two distinct primes $\mathfrak{p}$, $\mathfrak{p}'$ in $K$. Assume that $K$ and $p$ satisfies several (somewhat strict) conditions.
  Let $K_\infty /K$ be the cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extension. In the present paper, we give a method to study the structure of the unramified Iwasawa module $X (K_\infty)$ by using abelian $p$-extensions unramified outside $\mathfrak{p}$. We give a sufficient condition for $|X (K_\infty)|$ to be finite in terms of such extensions. We also give a similar criterion for $X (K_\infty)$ to be finitely generated over $\mathbb{Z}_p$.

We give a necessary and sufficient condition for $X (k_\infty)$ to be cyclic over $\mathbb{Z}_2$, which is different from the former results given by either Mouhib-Movahhedi or Mizusawa-Mouhib. We also give several sufficient conditions for the validity of Greenberg's conjecture.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究集合的加性结构与几何结构（如球面点集）之间的相容性，理解 Fourier $L^1$ 范数小这一解析条件对集合几何形态的强限制。

Conclusion: 小的 Fourier $L^1$ 范数排除了众多几何现象；球面 Freiman 模型在此条件下受到强限制，其大部分须集中于少数具加性结构的球冠中。

Abstract: We study geometric inverse problems for finite sets $A\subset \mathbb{Z}^d$ whose Fourier $L^1$ norm, or Wiener norm, \begin{align*} \|A\|:=\int_{\mathbb{T}^d}\Big|\sum_{a\in A}e(a\cdot t)\Big|dt
  \end{align*} is subpolynomial in $|A|$. In particular, we show a variety of geometric phenomena are incompatible with small Fourier $L^1$ norm. Our principal application concerns spherical Freiman models. Suppose $\|A\|=|A|^{o(1)}$ and $D\subseteq A$ is Freiman isomorphic to a set $S$ of lattice points on a sphere; then, $|D|/|A|$ must be polynomially small in $|A|$. Moreover, if $|D|/|A|$ is not too small, almost all of $S$ lies in a small number of spherical caps, and almost all caps have rich additive structure. The proof uses a new mechanism for studying additive structure across subsets of $A$, along with decoupling results of Bourgain-Demeter.

</details>


### [85] [The existence and uniqueness of magic-faced hypercubes, and applications to Khajuraho most-perfect magic squares, cubes, and hypercubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26762)
*Manjul Bhargava*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文定义"面幻超立方体"(magic-faced hypercube),证明阶为2的面幻超立方体在所有维度n≥0均存在,并确定其唯一性(在特定变换群下),并应用于经典Khajuraho 4×4幻方与Coxeter问题的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知线幻超立方体(magic-lined hypercube)对任意阶k≥3、任意维n都存在,但阶为2时,任何维度n≥2都不存在线幻超立方体。为弥补这一空缺,作者将幻条件从"线"放宽到"二维面",研究阶为2的超立方体。

Result: 证明面幻超立方体对每个维度n≥0均存在;在自然变换群下唯一,该变换群大小当n为偶数时为2^n(n+1)!,当n为奇数时为2^n·n·n!。作为应用,恢复了经典的4×4 Khajuraho幻方并加以推广,回答了Coxeter关于作用于384个"最完美"4×4幻方的群的问题,并向高维扩展;证明了在维度n下这些最完美对象在Weyl群W(B_{2n})的某种自然作用下构成单一轨道。

Conclusion: 通过将幻条件由线放宽到面,阶为2的面幻超立方体在所有维度存在且具唯一性,该框架统一并推广了经典4×4幻方与"最完美"幻方理论,并在高维下给出由Weyl群W(B_{2n})主导的轨道结构。

Abstract: A $\textit{magic-lined hypercube}$ (or, simply, $\textit{magic hypercube}$) of order $k$ and dimension $n$ is an arrangement of the numbers $1,\dots,k^n$ in a $k\times\cdots\times k$ ($n$-fold) grid such that every line of $k$ numbers parallel to a coordinate axis has the same magic sum. While such hypercubes exist for every order $k\ge 3$ and every dimension $n$, no magic-lined hypercube of order $2$ exists in any dimension $n\ge 2$.
  For hypercubes of order $2$, we thus relax the magic condition from lines to two-dimensional faces or planes. We call an arrangement of the numbers $1,\dots,2^n$ in a $2\times\cdots\times2$ ($n$-fold) grid \emph{magic-faced} if every $2\times2$ face has the same magic sum. We prove that a magic-faced hypercube of order $2$ exists in every dimension $n\ge0$, and that it is unique up to a certain natural set of transformations of size $2^n(n+1)!$ when $n$ is even and $2^n n\cdot n!$ when $n$ is odd.
  As an application, we recover and generalize the classical $4\times4$ Khajuraho magic square, answer a question of Coxeter on the group acting on the $384$ ``most-perfect" $4\times4$ magic squares, and extend the picture to higher dimensions. In particular, we prove that, in dimension $n$, these most-perfect objects form a single orbit under a certain natural action of the Weyl group $W(B_{2n})$.

</details>


### [86] [Density of large holes among power-free lattice points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25457)
*Francesco Cellarosi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 中非 $r$-free 点构成的最近邻图中 $R$-deep 点与 $R$-large 连通分支代表点的密度，得到了统一的渐近估计，并给出 $d=1$ 时 $r$-free 整数间隙密度的高阶渐近改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Baake–Moody–Pleasants、Pleasants–Huck）给出了正密度空洞构造与稀疏模式频率上界，但缺乏对深点密度及其代表点密度的精确渐近刻画；同时 Grimmett 的结果仅给出 $d=1$ 情形的主项，需要更高阶修正。本文旨在统一处理 $1\le q\le\infty$ 并给出尖锐渐近。

Method: 将非 $r$-free 点集 $W_{d,r}$ 作为顶点集，赋予最近邻邻接；定义 $R$-deep 点（其 $\ell_q$ 球内所有格点均非 $r$-free）与 $R$-large 连通分支，并以字典序最小 $R$-deep 点作为有限 $R$-large 分支的代表。通过概率/计数与 sieve 型估计，对 $R$-deep 点密度及代表点密度作渐近展开。

Result: 对 $1\le q\le\infty$ 一致地证明：当 $R\to\infty$ 时，$R$-deep 点密度与代表点密度均为
\[
\exp\!\left\{ -\frac{v_{d,q}}{\zeta(dr)} \left(d(dr-1)R^d\log R+drR^d\log\log R\right) +O_{d,r}(R^d) \right\},
\]
其中 $v_{d,q}$ 为 $(\mathbb R^d,\|\cdot\|_q)$ 单位球体积。$d=1$ 时，恰好 $g-1$ 个非 $r$-free 整数夹在两个 $r$-free 整数之间的密度为
\[
\exp\!\left\{-\frac{r-1}{\zeta(r)}g\log g-\frac{r}{\zeta(r)}g\log\log g+O_r(g)\right\},
\]
改进了 Grimmett 工作的 $\exp\{-(\frac{r-1}{\zeta(r)}+o(1))g\log g\}$。对固定 $r$，还得到维数 $d=O_r((\log R)^r)$ 下的一致估计。

Conclusion: 本文给出了非 $r$-free 点图中深点与代表性分支密度在 $\ell_q$ 范数下统一的尖锐渐近，深化并推广了正密度空洞与稀疏模式频率的已有结果；$d=1$ 的间隙密度高阶渐近改进了 Grimmett 的估计，并提供了随维数增长的一致控制。

Abstract: For fixed integers $d,r\ge1$ with $dr\ge2$, the $r$-free points of $\mathbb{Z}^d$ are the vectors whose coordinate gcd is not divisible by the $r$th power of any prime. We consider the set $W_{d,r}$ of non-$r$-free points in $\mathbb{Z}^d$ as the vertex set of a graph with nearest-neighbour adjacency. For $1\le q\le\infty$, we say that a point $t\in\mathbb Z^d$ is $R$-deep if $t+\{n\in\mathbb Z^d:\|n\|_q\le R\}\subseteq W_{d,r}$. Thus $R$-deep points are the lattice centres of closed $\ell_q$-balls whose lattice points are all non-$r$-free. We call a connected component $R$-large if it contains an $R$-deep point. We mark each finite $R$-large component by selecting its lexicographically least $R$-deep point as its representative. Uniformly for $1\le q\le\infty$, we show that the density of $R$-deep points and the density of these representatives both equal $$ \exp\!\left\{ -\frac{v_{d,q}}{ζ(dr)} \left(d(dr-1)R^d\log R+drR^d\log\log R\right) +O_{d,r}(R^d) \right\} $$ as $R\to\infty$, where $v_{d,q}$ denotes the volume of the unit ball in $(\mathbb R^d,\|\cdot\|_q)$. For deep points, this sharpens the positive-density hole constructions of Baake, Moody, and Pleasants and of Pleasants and Huck, and the latter authors' upper bounds for sparse-pattern frequencies.
  When $d=1$, we show that the density of $r$-free integers followed by exactly $g-1$ non-$r$-free integers and then another $r$-free integer is $$\exp\!\left\{-\frac{r-1}{ζ(r)}g\log g
  -\frac{r}{ζ(r)}g\log\log g+O_r(g)\right\}$$ as $g\to\infty$, improving the asymptotic $\exp\left\{-\left(\frac{r-1}{ζ(r)}+o(1)\right) g\log g\right\}$ which follows from Grimmett's work. %
  For fixed $r$, we also obtain estimates uniform in growing dimensions $d=O_r((\log R)^r)$.

</details>


### [87] [Equal subset sums and close divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26014)
*Jianfeng Hou,Hongbin Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了α_k = β_k/(1-β_k)，解决了Ford、Green和Koukoulopoulos的猜想，并证明其弱熵阈值与严格熵阈值一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ford、Green和Koukoulopoulos此前提出了关于乘性区间内不同除数个数与随机子集同和问题两个指数之间的猜想关系，本文旨在解决这一猜想并统一其熵阈值理论。

Method: 结合flag refinement与熵凹性，给出对任意平移一致的近似子集和上界；再利用独立几何素数指数模型将该上界转移到除数问题上。

Result: 证明了α_k = β_k/(1-β_k)，并证明弱熵阈值与严格熵阈值重合。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了Invent. Math. 232 (2023)中提出的猜想，并统一了随机子集同和问题的熵阈值，方法上建立了近似子集和上界与除数计数之间的转移桥梁。

Abstract: For $k\geq2$, let $α_k$ be the supremum of the exponents $a$ for which almost every integer $n$ has $k$ distinct divisors in a multiplicative interval of relative length $(\log n)^{-a}$. Select each positive integer $i$ independently with probability $1/i$, forming a random set $\mathbf A$, and let $β_k$ be the supremum of the $c<1$ for which, with probability tending to one as $D\to\infty$, the set $\mathbf A\cap[D^c,D]$ has $k$ distinct subsets with the same sum. We prove that $α_k=β_k/(1-β_k)$, resolving a conjecture of Ford, Green and Koukoulopoulos [Invent. Math. 232 (2023), 1027--1160]. We also prove that their weak and strict entropy thresholds coincide. The proof combines flag refinement and entropy concavity with an upper bound for approximate subset sums that is uniform in arbitrary translations. A model with independent geometric prime exponents then transfers this bound to divisors.

</details>

### [88] [Pseudo-Riemannian geodesic orbit nilmanifolds of signature $\boldsymbol{(n-3,3)}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25142)
*Zhiqi Chen,Shaoxiang Zhang,Yiyi Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究度量指标为三的伪黎曼测地轨道幂零流形（signature (n-3,3)），在度量在[n,n]上非退化时证明n为交换、2步或4步幂零（2步结论失效但3步仍不可能），并构造(6,3)九维例子证其sharpness；在度量退化时证明存在不变各向同性子空间并给出双重扩张归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测地轨道性质在黎曼几何中蕴含齐性，并涵盖弱对称与自然可约等重要类。伪黎曼（不定度量）情形比黎曼情形更为精细；近年虽已在Lorentz和trans-Lorentz测地轨道流形上取得结构结果，但对度量指标为三的幂零情形尚缺乏系统结构分析，本文即填补这一空白。

Method: 设M=G/H为signature (n-3,3)的测地轨道伪黎曼流形，且G的幂零解析子群可迁。取约化分解g=h⊕n（向量空间直和），n幂零。分两种情形研究：当度量在[n,n]上非退化时，分析n的幂零步数；当度量在[n,n]上退化时，构造不变各向同性子空间并建立双重扩张归约。

Result: 度量在[n,n]非退化时，n为交换、2步或4步幂零；与黎曼、Lorentz、trans-Lorentz情形不同，2步结论不成立，但3步幂零仍不可能。4步情形限于Lorentz导代数且正交补指标为二，其结构迫使该补中存在不变全各向同性二平面。构造出signature (6,3)的九维显式例子证明sharpness。度量在[n,n]退化时，证明存在不变各向同性子空间，它中心化其正交补，从而给出到严格更小指标的测地轨道度量幂零李代数的双重扩张归约。八维例子表明这种相对中心性不必蕴含在整个李代数中的中心性。

Conclusion: 在指标三的伪黎曼测地轨道幂零流形中，非退化情形下幂零步数被限制为1、2或4（3步不可能），4步结构极为特殊且伴随不变全各向同性二平面；退化情形可通过不变各向同性子空间实现双重扩张归约。相对中心性弱于全代数中心性，指标下降归约为进一步分类提供了途径。

Abstract: The geodesic orbit property is useful and interesting in Riemannian geometry. It implies homogeneity and has important classes of Riemannian manifolds as special cases, such as weakly symmetric Riemannian manifolds and naturally reductive Riemannian manifolds. The corresponding results for indefinite metric manifolds are much more delicate than in Riemannian signature, but in the last few years important corresponding structural results were proved for geodesic orbit Lorentz and trans-Lorentz manifolds. %Here we carry out a major step in the structural analysis of geodesic orbit Lorentz nilmanifolds. Here we study pseudo-Riemannian geodesic orbit nilmanifolds of metric index three. Those are the geodesic orbit pseudo-Riemannian manifolds $M = G/H$ of signature $(n-3,3)$ such that a nilpotent analytic subgroup of $G$ is transitive on $M$. Suppose that there is a reductive decomposition $\g = \h \oplus \n$ (vector space direct sum) with $\n$ nilpotent. When the metric is nondegenerate on $[\n,\n]$ we show that $\n$ is abelian, $2$-step or $4$-step nilpotent. In contrast to the Riemannian, Lorentzian, and trans-Lorentz cases, the two-step conclusion therefore fails, but $3$-step nilpotency remains impossible. The $4$-step case is confined to a Lorentzian derived algebra with an orthogonal complement of index two, and its structure forces an invariant totally isotropic two-plane in that complement. An explicit nine-dimensional example of signature $(6,3)$ proves sharpness. When the metric is degenerate on $[\n,\n]$ we prove the existence of an invariant isotropic subspace that centralizes its orthogonal complement, yielding a double-extension reduction to a geodesic orbit metric nilpotent Lie algebra of strictly smaller index. An eight-dimensional example shows that this relative centrality need not imply centrality in the full Lie algebra.

</details>


### [89] [A gluing construction of $Z$-extremal Kähler metrics and new examples of Bismut Hermitian-Einstein $3$-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25250)
*Vestislav Apostolov,Abdellah Lahdili,Kuan-Hui Lee,Frédéric Rochon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为一类Kähler流形/轨形上的6阶几何非线性偏微分方程建立了粘合构造，并将其应用于奇点消解与点爆破，从而构造出新的Bismut Hermitian Einstein结构及超引力解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类6阶非线性PDE源自多个重要几何与物理问题：Bismut Hermitian Einstein复3维流形、IIB型与D=11超引力中的AdS₃×Y₇和AdS₂×Y₉解，以及Dervan的Z-临界Kähler度量。研究者希望通过对该方程构造粘合解，获得新的几何结构并生成新的超引力解实例。

Method: 在Kähler流形或轨形上，针对该类6阶几何非线性PDE建立粘合（gluing）构造。作为应用，证明了两类定理：(1) 对具有crepant奇点且容许该PDE解的轨形，给出奇点消解定理；(2) 对容许该PDE解的光滑复3维流形，给出点爆破定理。

Result: 在光滑流形 3(S²×S⁴)♯4(S³×S³)、5(S²×S⁴)♯6(S³×S³) 与 S¹×9(S²×S³) 上得到新的正则Bismut Hermitian Einstein结构实例；并构造出无穷多种拓扑类型的7维紧流形Y₇，为AdS₃×Y₇的IIB型及AdS₂×T²×Y⁷的D=11超引力模型提供新解。

Conclusion: 通过6阶非线性PDE的粘合构造及消解/爆破定理，本文统一地从几何分析角度生成了新的Bismut Hermitian Einstein结构与超引力解，扩展了相关流形类与物理模型解的存在性范围。

Abstract: We present a gluing construction for a class of 6th order geometric non-linear PDE's on a Kähler manifold or orbifold, arising in the study of Bismut Hermitian Einstein complex $3$-folds, the supersymmetric ${\rm AdS}_3 \times Y_7$ and ${\rm AdS}_2 \times Y_9$ solutions of type IIB and $D=11$ supergravity models in physics, and in Dervan's Z-critical Kähler metrics. As applications, we obtain a desingularization theorem for orbifolds with crepant singularities admitting a solution of the PDE, as well as a blowing-up of points theorem for smooth complex $3$-folds admitting such solutions. These results yield new examples of regular Bismut Hermitian Einstein structures on the smooth manifolds $3(S^2\times S^4)\sharp 4(S^3\times S^3)$, $5(S^2\times S^4)\sharp 6(S^3\times S^3)$ and $S^1\times 9(S^2\times S^3)$, as well as infinite topological types of smooth compact $7$-dimensional manifolds $Y_7$, providing new solutions to the supersymmetric ${\rm AdS}_3 \times Y_7$ type IIB and ${\rm AdS}_2 \times T^2 \times Y^7$ type $D=11$ supergravity models.

</details>


### [90] [Futaki invariant on lcK manifolds with potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25268)
*Giacomo Perri*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了Futaki-Hattori-Ornea引入的Futaki不变量在每类具有势函数的紧致局部共形Kähler流形上恒为零，回答了Ornea与Verbitsky提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ornea与Verbitsky此前提出疑问：Futaki、Hattori与Ornea定义的Futaki不变量在局部共形Kähler流形上的行为如何？该不变量此前主要用于Kähler几何中刻画常标量曲率度量的存在性障碍，其在更一般的局部共形Kähler（lcK）情形下的性质尚不明确，本文旨在解决这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 针对紧致局部共形Kähler且具有势函数（potential）的流形，通过分析该Futaki不变量的定义结构与其在lcK几何框架下的变换性质，证明其恒等于零。核心论证依赖于势函数存在性所带来的李导数/向量场与不变量的对应关系。

Result: 证明了Futaki-Hattori-Ornea Futaki不变量在每类紧致、局部共形Kähler且具有势函数的流形上恒为零，即该不变量在这类流形上不构成障碍。

Conclusion: 该结果肯定性地回答了Ornea与Verbitsky的问题，表明在具有势函数的紧致lcK流形上Futaki不变量恒消失，从而揭示了此类不变量在lcK几何与Kähler几何中的本质差异。

Abstract: We prove that the Futaki invariant introduced by Futaki, Hattori, and Ornea vanishes identically on every compact locally conformally Kähler manifold with potential, thereby answering a question raised by Ornea and Verbitsky.

</details>


### [91] [Two Growth Filtrations for Drift Laplacians: Compatibility, Rigidity, and an Inverse Hermite/Laguerre Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25289)
*Elham Matinpour*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究完备非紧黎曼流形上漂移拉普拉斯算子的谱与特征函数增长阶的对应关系，建立加权Agmon估计与谱比较，并给出α=2条件下λ_k≍γ_k乃至≍k的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非紧流形上，漂移拉普拉斯算子L_f=Δ-⟨∇f,∇⟩的谱离散性与特征函数增长之间的关系尚需系统刻画。作者希望在不依赖曲率界与孤立子方程的前提下，用权重f的多项式增长假设，将特征函数的增长阶滤过与特征值滤过进行比较，从而理解谱渐近。

Method: 在L²(M,e^{-f}dV)上研究L_f，假设f、|∇f|、Δf有多项式界（H1-H3，增长指数α>0），特征函数在f^{1/α}水平集上有有限多项式增长阶γ_k且γ_k→∞（H4）。证明离散性与加权Agmon估计；用矩估计以增长阶和有限尺度前因子控制集中半径；通过下局部化条件给出谱比较的一个方向。分别在精确warped product（一维基）与具有传递等距对称的一般流形上，附加径向增长与径向漂移正则性假设；并研究不变特征函数为多项式的情形。

Result: 证明α>1时的离散性与加权Agmon估计，并给出集中半径的矩估计。下局部化条件给出谱比较的一个方向。对精确warped product，在径向增长条件与径向漂移正则性下得到α=2且径向部分λ_k≍γ_k。对一般(M,g)，在水平集具传递等距对称、不变扇区增长条件与径向漂移正则性下得到α=2且λ_k≍γ_k≍k。不变特征函数为多项式时无需漂移正则性即得α=2，归一化后约化方程为Hermite或广义Laguerre方程。给出显式旋转对称反例：即使α=2，兼容关系λ_k≍γ_k^{(2α-2)/α}对全谱也可能失效。

Conclusion: 特征函数增长阶与特征值的兼容关系并非普遍成立，需依赖对称性、warped structure或多项式性等附加条件；在合适假设下可得到α=2及λ_k≍γ_k≍k。整个论证不依赖曲率界或孤立子方程，为漂移拉普拉斯谱理论提供了一套基于权重增长的自洽框架。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g)$ be a complete, connected, noncompact Riemannian manifold, and let $f\ge0$ be a proper $C^2$ weight. We assume polynomial bounds on $f$, $|\nabla f|$, and $Δf$ (Assumptions H1-H3), with growth exponent $α>0$. We study the drift Laplacian $L_f=Δ-\langle\nabla f,\nabla\cdot\rangle$ on $L^2(M,e^{-f}dV)$. We assume that its eigenfunctions have finite polynomial growth orders $γ_k$, measured on the level sets of $f^{1/α}$. We also assume that $γ_k\to\infty$ in spectral order (Assumption H4). We compare this growth filtration with the filtration by eigenvalue $λ_k$. For $α>1$, we study the relation $λ_k\asympγ_k^{(2α-2)/α}$, called compatibility. An explicit rotationally symmetric example shows that this relation can fail for the full spectrum, even when $α=2$.
  We prove discreteness and weighted Agmon estimates for $α>1$. A moment estimate bounds the concentration radius in terms of the growth order and a finite-scale prefactor. A lower localization condition gives one direction of the spectral comparison. For exact warped products with a one-dimensional base, we impose the radial growth condition and a regularity assumption on the radial drift. These give $α=2$ and $λ_k\asympγ_k$ in the radial sector. For general $(M,g)$, we assume a transitive isometric symmetry of the level sets instead of a warped-product structure. The invariant-sector growth condition and radial-drift regularity then give $α=2$ and $λ_k\asympγ_k\asymp k$. Finally, exact polynomiality of the invariant eigenfunctions implies $α=2$ without the drift regularity assumptions. The reduced equation is then Hermite or generalized Laguerre after normalization. The arguments use neither curvature bounds nor soliton equations.

</details>


### [92] [The dHYM equation on crepant resolutions of Calabi-Yau cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25309)
*Eder M. Correa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在非紧Calabi-Yau流形上研究dHYM方程，通过Calabi ansatz将完全非线性PDE约化为标量渐近自治ODE，给出拓扑相位存在性条件，并构造首个非环面Calabi-Yau上非dHYM的Hermitian-Einstein联络实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dHYM方程源自镜像对称与特殊联络理论，此前构造多限于环面或紧情形，缺乏对非紧、非环面Calabi-Yau流形的显式解与非dHYM反例。作者欲借助有理齐性簇的Lie理论数据与余齐一对称性，扩展可解范围并获得新的判别工具。

Method: 取Z = Tot(K_X)，X为Fano有理齐性簇，利用反规范极化使Z成为仿射锥顶点奇点的解消。借助Calabi ansatz给出的Ricci-平坦Kähler度量的余齐一对称性，将dHYM完全非线性PDE约化为标量、渐近自治ODE，再通过ODE分析确定拓扑相位的精确存在条件，并研究解的整体性、退化与刚性。

Result: 确定了保证dHYM解整体存在的拓扑相位精确条件；证明Z上每个全纯线丛都存在光滑整体Hermitian联络求解dHYM，只要总相位落入由Lie理论数据确定的显式开区间；给出dHYM解退化为经典HYM联络的刚性分类；构造出非环面Calabi-Yau流形上线丛的首个显式非平凡Hermitian-Einstein联络且非dHYM的反例。

Conclusion: dHYM方程可通过余齐一对称性降维为ODE进行研究，所得构造推广了既有结果并给出一大批新例；ODE方法同时揭示dHYM与HYM之间的联系与差异，并首次给出非环面情形下非dHYM的Hermitian-Einstein联络显式实例。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the deformed Hermitian-Yang-Mills (dHYM) equation on the non-compact Calabi-Yau manifold $Z = {\rm{Tot}}({\bf{K}}_{X})$, where $X$ is a rational homogeneous variety. Since $X$ is a Fano variety, $Z$ is a resolution of the singularity at the vertex of the affine cone ${\rm{Aff}}(X)$, provided the cone is built using the anticanonical polarization ${\bf{L}} = {\bf{K}}_{X}^{-1}$. Using the cohomogeneity-one symmetry of the Ricci-flat Kähler metric obtained via the Calabi ansatz on $Z$, we reduce the fully nonlinear PDE underlying the dHYM equation to a scalar, asymptotically autonomous ordinary differential equation (ODE). From this, we determine the exact condition on the topological phase that guarantees global existence of solutions. As an application, we show that every holomorphic line bundle over $Z$ admits a smooth, globally defined Hermitian connection solving the dHYM equation, provided the total phase lies in an explicit open interval determined by the Lie-theoretic data. Also, we prove a rigidity result classifying the exact geometric conditions under which the dHYM solution collapses into a classical Hermitian-Yang-Mills (HYM) connection. The results established generalize previous constructions and provide a substantial new class of examples. Furthermore, the approach presented allows one to study the behavior of the dHYM solutions through ODE methods. Using this approach, we construct the first explicit non-trivial example of a Hermitian-Einstein connection on a line bundle over a non-toric Calabi-Yau manifold which is not dHYM.

</details>
