### [93] [A continuous Positive Mass Theorem for perturbations of Euclidean space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25550)
*Paula Burkhardt-Guim*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在弱意义下（Ricci流意义）具有非负标量曲率、且充分一致接近欧氏空间的 C^0-渐近平坦黎曼度量满足正质量定理，其质量由作者此前引入的 ADM 质量的 C^0 类比给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典正质量定理通常要求度量具有较高的正则性（如 C^{1,1} 或 Sobolev 正则性），而低正则性（仅 C^0 渐近平坦）情形下的正质量定理尚不明确，因此需要将结论推广到弱正则度量的框架中。

Method: 作者考虑 C^0-渐近平坦黎曼度量，要求其为欧氏空间的 C^0 扰动、充分一致地接近欧氏空间，并在 Ricci 流意义下具有非负标量曲率；随后借助作者此前引入的经典 ADM 质量的 C^0 类比来定义质量并加以证明。

Result: 对于满足上述条件的 C^0-渐近平坦度量，证明了其质量非负，即在弱正则性设定下正质量定理成立。

Conclusion: 该工作将正质量定理推广至低正则性（C^0 渐近平坦、Ricci 流意义下非负标量曲率）的度量情形，并表明作者所定义的 ADM 质量的 C^0 类比是刻画此类度量质量的合适工具。

Abstract: We prove a Positive Mass Theorem for $C^0$-asymptotically flat Riemannian metrics with nonnegative scalar curvature in a weak sense that are sufficiently uniformly close to Euclidean space. More precisely, we show that a $C^0$-asymptotically flat Riemannian metric that is a $C^0$ perturbation of Euclidean space with nonnegative scalar curvature in the sense of Ricci flow has nonnegative mass, where the mass is given by a $C^0$ analog of the classical ADM mass previously introduced by the author.

</details>


### [94] [Divisorial Rigidity and Regularity of Boundary Cases for the Supercritical LYZ Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25553)
*Jixiang Fu,Shing-Tung Yau,Dekai Zhang,Ziyi Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在紧Kähler流形上研究处于稳定性边界的超临界LYZ方程。在数值半稳定假设下，证明了修正nef锥上的定量正性估计，推出失稳素除子构成有限例外族；并在存在光滑半子解时证明相关相交形式在这些除子类张成空间上负定，进而构造对数奇异子解，得到在补集上光滑的有界Bedford–Taylor解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界LYZ方程在稳定性边界处的研究尚不充分，其解的奇异性结构与失稳素除子的关系需要刻画。本文旨在数值半稳定条件下，理解稳定性边界附近的解的正则性与奇点分布，并将代数稳定性与解析几何解联系起来。

Method: 首先在数值半稳定假设下，于修正nef锥上建立定量正性估计，从而识别失稳素除子并证明其构成有限例外族；其次假设存在光滑半子解（semisubsolution），证明相关相交形式在这些除子类张成空间上负定；最后利用该刚性构造沿除子并集的对数奇异子解，进而得到Bounded Bedford–Taylor解。

Result: 证明了修正nef锥上的定量正性估计；失稳素除子构成有限例外族；在光滑半子解存在时，相关相交形式在失稳除子类的张成空间上负定；构造出对数奇异子解，并获得在除子并集补集上光滑的有界Bedford–Taylor解。

Conclusion: 在数值半稳定与光滑半子解存在的条件下，超临界LYZ方程的奇异性可被有限族失稳素除子所刻画，且解在对数奇异框架下具有较好的正则性，为稳定性边界情形提供了定量的正性与刚性结果。

Abstract: We study the supercritical LYZ equation on compact Kähler manifolds at the boundary of stability. Under numerical semistability, we prove a quantitative positivity estimate on the modified nef cone. It implies that the destabilizing prime divisors form a finite exceptional family. Assuming the existence of a smooth semisubsolution, we prove that the associated intersection form is negative definite on the span of classes of these divisors. Using this rigidity, we then construct a logarithmic singular subsolution along their union and obtain a bounded Bedford--Taylor solution which is smooth on its complement.

</details>


### [95] [Hamiltonian-Type Spectral Functionals for Time-Dependent Perturbed Hodge--de Rham Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25676)
*Sining Wei,Yong Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究Hawkins哈密顿作用量中由Dirac型算子$D_t=d+\delta_{g_t}+\Psi_t$诱导的标量加权残差泛函，通过符号计算将失效（lapse）修正化为散度项与Dirichlet项，并在带warped collar的紧流形上给出加权KKW项与交换子修正的相容分解及显式边界残差，证明collar导数与微扰贡献在加权KKW分解中相消，空边界时恢复闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hawkins的哈密顿作用量含有由Dirac型算子表达的项，其上标量加权残差泛函的边界行为尚不清楚。作者希望明确速度贡献对度量变分的依赖、化简失效修正，并在带边流形上确定KKW项与交换子修正的相容分解，以便得到可计算的边界残差，并检验在无边情形下是否退化回已知的闭式结果。

Method: 设定偶数维$n\geq4$定向流形上的光滑度量族$g_t$，在固定外丛上考虑自伴Dirac型算子$D_t=d+\delta_{g_t}+\Psi_t$。利用系数导数$\partial_tD_t$计算标量加权残差泛函；通过直接符号计算化简失效修正；对非平凡测试函数分部积分保留混合梯度项；在紧流形含warped collar的情况下指定加权KKW项与交换子修正的相容分解，并显式计算其边界残差。

Result: 速度贡献仅依赖于$\partial_tg_t$；失效修正被约化为一个散度项和一个Dirichlet项，非平凡测试函数经分部积分后留下混合梯度项；在带warped collar的紧流形上得到加权KKW项与交换子修正的相容分解，并显式算出其边界残差；加权KKW分解中collar导数与微扰贡献相消，剩余边界项涉及标量权重的法向导数；边界为空时恢复闭式公式。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Hawkins哈密顿作用量中标量加权残差泛函的符号化简与边界分析框架，明确了速度项、失效修正的散度—Dirichlet结构以及warped collar上KKW与交换子项的相容分解与相消机制，并验证了无边极限下与已有闭式公式的一致性。

Abstract: Let $g_t$ be a smooth family of Riemannian metrics on an oriented manifold of even dimension $n\geq4$, and let $D_t=d+δ_{g_t}+Ψ_t$ on the fixed exterior bundle, with $Ψ_t$ self-adjoint. We evaluate a scalar-weighted residue functional obtained from the operator expression in Hawkins' Hamiltonian action using the coefficient derivative $\partial_tD_t$. The velocity contribution depends only on $\partial_tg_t$. A direct symbol calculation reduces the lapse correction to a divergence and a Dirichlet term; for a nonconstant test function, integration by parts retains a mixed gradient term. For compact manifolds with a warped collar, we specify compatible factorizations of the weighted KKW term and of the commutator correction. Their boundary residues are computed explicitly. The collar-derivative and perturbation contributions cancel in the weighted KKW factorization, and the remaining boundary terms involve normal derivatives of the scalar weights. The closed formula is recovered when the boundary is empty.

</details>


### [96] [Kähler Rigidity for Constant Chern Holomorphic Sectional Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25762)
*Xiangsen Qin,Yuanhong Tian*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了两类关于常Chern全纯截面曲率的紧Hermitian流形刚性定理：在Fujiki类中非正情形度量必为Kähler并完全刚性，零曲率情形平坦；平衡三维正曲率情形等距于复射影空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究常Chern全纯截面曲率下紧Hermitian流形的刚性，力图在无平衡或pluriclosed假设下建立Kähler性，并刻画极值情形（负、零、正曲率）的几何结构。

Method: 非正情形基于Kähler背景上的积分Chern–Lu恒等式；负曲率借助twisted Kähler–Einstein体积下界导出典范丰沛性，并用加权Stokes恒等式与体积比矩不等式刻画度量；零曲率用张量Bochner论证在Ricci平坦背景上得到平行性；平衡三维正曲率情形通过微分相容性与挠率能量恒等式给出强制性估计，用对所有挠率秩成立的有理矩阵分解证明。

Result: Fujiki类上常非正Chern全纯截面曲率的Hermitian度量必为Kähler；负曲率时流形射影、典范丛丰沛，度量为标准负Kähler–Einstein度量且万有覆叠为复双曲空间；零曲率时度量平坦，流形被复环面有限étale覆叠。常正Chern全纯截面曲率的紧平衡三维流形复等距于带缩放Fubini–Study度量的复射影三维空间。

Conclusion: 在无需平衡或pluriclosed假设下，Chern全纯截面曲率的常值性对Fujiki类Hermitian度量施加了强刚性，将其归结为Kähler（乃至Kähler–Einstein或平坦、复双曲）情形；正曲率平衡三维情形亦完全分类为复射影空间。

Abstract: We prove two rigidity theorems for compact Hermitian manifolds with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature. On a manifold in Fujiki's class $\mathcal C$, every Hermitian metric of constant nonpositive Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is Kähler, without a balanced or pluriclosed assumption. In the negative case, the manifold is projective with ample canonical bundle, and the given metric is the normalized negative Kähler--Einstein metric, with complex hyperbolic universal cover. In the zero case, the metric is flat and the manifold admits a finite étale cover by a complex torus. We also prove that a compact balanced threefold of constant positive Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is holomorphically isometric to complex projective three-space with a scaled Fubini--Study metric. The nonpositive argument uses an integrated Chern--Lu identity on a Kähler background. For negative curvature, a lower bound for twisted Kähler--Einstein volumes yields canonical ampleness, and weighted Stokes identities and volume-ratio moment inequalities identify the given metric. At zero curvature, a tensor Bochner argument gives parallelness on a Ricci-flat background. For balanced threefolds, differential compatibility and the torsion energy identities yield a coercive estimate, proved by a rational matrix decomposition valid at every torsion rank.

</details>


### [97] [Compact Vaisman and l.c.K. manifolds with nonnegative bisectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25880)
*Jiangtao Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究了非负双截面曲率下紧致Vaisman流形与l.c.K.流形的结构，证明了Vaisman流形的单值化定理，以及l.c.K.流形在非负Chern双截面曲率下的三分类结构定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Mok在Kähler情形下的单值化定理，并刻画非负（Chern）双截面曲率约束下紧致Vaisman流形与l.c.K.流形的几何结构。

Method: 在紧致Vaisman流形与l.c.K.流形上，引入非负双截面曲率（以及非负Chern双截面曲率）作为曲率假设，结合单值化方法与结构分析进行刻画。

Result: 定理1.3：给出紧致Vaisman流形的单值化定理，推广了Mok的Kähler单值化定理。定理1.6：对非负Chern双截面曲率的紧致l.c.K.流形给出结构三分：底复流形要么容许Kähler度量，要么容许Vaisman度量，要么其万有覆盖共形等价于一个非完备Kähler流形与正维复欧氏空间的乘积。

Conclusion: 紧致Vaisman与l.c.K.流形在非负双截面曲率条件下具有刚性/单值化结构；l.c.K.情形呈现Kähler、Vaisman或特殊乘积型三者之一的结构，统一并推广了Kähler几何中的相关单值化结果。

Abstract: We study the structure of compact Vaisman and l.c.K. manifolds with nonnegative bisectional curvature in this paper. Our first main result (cf. Theorem 1.3) is a uniformization theorem for compact Vaisman manifolds, extending Mok's uniformization theorem in the Kähler setting (cf. [Mok88]). Our second result (cf. Theorem 1.6) gives a structural trichotomy for compact l.c.K. manifolds with nonnegative Chern bisectional curvature: the underlying complex manifold admits a Kähler metric, admits a Vaisman metric, or its universal cover is conformally equivalent to the product of an incomplete Kähler manifold and a positive-dimensional complex Euclidean space.

</details>


### [98] [Weakly Einstein and weakly $η$-Einstein structures on almost contact metric three-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26024)
*Sun Hyang Chun,Yunhee Euh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究了几乎接触度量三维流形上的弱Einstein与弱η-Einstein结构，给出Ricci算子Q的逐点结构描述，证明接触度量三流形在Qξ=0时Q=0或rank Q=1，并分类了单连通齐性弱Einstein接触度量三流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱Einstein与弱η-Einstein条件是Einstein条件的自然推广，但它们在几乎接触度量三维流形上的整体结构与分类尚不清楚，作者希望刻画Ricci算子Q的逐点形式并理解接触度量假设在其中的作用。

Method: 采用逐点代数与张量分析的方法：先在几乎接触度量三流形上分析Ricci算子Q的可能结构形式，区分η-Einstein形式、Qξ=0与弱Einstein三种情形；再在接触度量情形下研究Qξ=0的约束，并构造反例说明接触度量假设的必要性；最后对单连通齐性接触度量三流形进行弱Einstein分类。

Result: 得到弱η-Einstein几乎接触度量三流形上Q的逐点结构描述：每一点处Q或具η-Einstein形式、或Qξ=0、或度量为弱Einstein。证明若接触度量三流形满足Qξ=0，则Q=0或rank Q=1，从而该度量要么平坦要么弱Einstein但非Einstein。构造了几乎接触度量三流形满足Qξ=0而ricci算子秩为二的例子，说明接触度量假设不可去掉。完成了单连通齐性弱Einstein接触度量三流形的分类。

Conclusion: 弱η-Einstein结构在三维几乎接触度量情形具有严格的逐点分类性质，接触度量条件将Qξ=0时的Ricci算子秩限制为0或1，并且单连通齐性弱Einstein接触度量三流形可被完全分类。

Abstract: We study weakly Einstein and weakly $η$-Einstein structures on almost contact metric three-manifolds. We first obtain a pointwise structural description of the Ricci operator $Q$ on a weakly $η$-Einstein almost contact metric three-manifold. More precisely, at each point, either $Q$ has the $η$-Einstein form, $Qξ=0$, or the metric is weakly Einstein. We then prove that if a contact metric three-manifold satisfies $Qξ=0$, then either $Q=0$ or $\operatorname{rank}Q=1$. Hence, such a metric is either flat or weakly Einstein but not Einstein. We also construct examples showing that the contact metric assumption is essential: there exist almost contact metric three-manifolds satisfying $Qξ=0$ whose Ricci operators have rank two. Finally, we classify simply connected homogeneous contact metric three-manifolds that are weakly Einstein.

</details>


### [99] [Lower bounds for the critical CR GJMS operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26371)
*Yuya Takeuchi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为闭伪厄米流形上的临界CR GJMS算子建立了下界，并在三维情形证明了CR Paneitz算子的下有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究临界CR GJMS算子与CR Paneitz算子的谱下界问题，探讨在何种几何假设（如可嵌入性）下可获得下界估计，并由此理解算子谱的累积行为与值域性质。

Method: 结合Heisenberg演算与基于Heisenberg群上次Laplacian与Reeb向量场联合函数演算的平方根构造。

Result: 在2n+1维、假设可嵌入时，得到涉及n+1阶Heisenberg Sobolev范数、模去L^2项、在核的正交补上的下界；在三维情形，无需可嵌入性即证明CR Paneitz算子有下界。作为应用，Rossi球上CR Paneitz算子无穷多个负特征值仅以零为聚点，从而该算子不具有闭值域。

Conclusion: 临界CR GJMS算子与CR Paneitz算子在适当条件下具有谱下界，且该下界可刻画其负谱的累积性质与值域的非闭性。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the critical CR GJMS operator on closed pseudo-Hermitian manifolds. In dimension $2n+1$, assuming embeddability, we obtain a lower bound involving the Heisenberg Sobolev norm of order $n+1$, modulo an $L^{2}$ term, on the orthogonal complement of the kernel. In dimension three, we prove that the CR Paneitz operator is bounded below without assuming embeddability. The proofs combine the Heisenberg calculus with a square-root construction based on the joint functional calculus of the sub-Laplacian and the Reeb vector field on the Heisenberg group. As an application, we show that the infinitely many negative eigenvalues of the CR Paneitz operator on the Rossi sphere have zero as their only accumulation point. Consequently, this operator does not have closed range.

</details>


### [100] [Balanced Bismut torsion-parallel manifold with constant holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26089)
*Qingsong Wang,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了平衡BTP Hermitian流形上常全纯截面曲率猜想在任意维数成立，并构造了一个反例否定满秩B猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Kähler几何中一个长期猜想认为：紧Hermitian流形若具有常全纯截面曲率，则必为Kähler或Chern平坦。该猜想在维数2已证，维数≥3除若干特殊情形外仍开放。BTP（Bismut挠率平行）流形是重要特殊情形，已有工作覆盖非平衡情形及低维平衡情形，高维平衡情形尚待解决。

Method: 针对平衡BTP紧Hermitian流形，在任意维数下进行一般性分析，证明常全纯截面曲率猜想；同时构造一个紧平衡BTP五维流形，其B矩阵满秩，用于检验满秩B猜想。

Result: 在任意维数下证明了平衡BTP流形满足常全纯截面曲率猜想；构造出一个紧平衡BTP五维流形，其B满秩，既非Kähler也非Chern平坦，其Chern全纯截面曲率非常数，从而否定了满秩B猜想。

Conclusion: 平衡BTP情形下常全纯截面曲率猜想得到完整解决；满秩B猜想不成立。

Abstract: A long-lasting conjecture in non-Kähler geometry says that any compact Hermitian manifold with constant holomorphic sectional curvature must be either Kähler or Chern flat. The conjecture is known to be true in dimension 2 but remains open in dimensions 3 or higher, except in several special cases. In this article we consider compact Hermitian manifolds that are Bismut torsion-parallel (BTP for brevity), which means that the Bismut connection has parallel torsion. For BTP manifolds that are not balanced, the conjecture was proved by Chen and Zheng. They also confirmed the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in dimension 3, utilizing a classification result for such threefolds by Zhao and Zheng. In a recent work, we confirmed the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in dimension 4 through a detailed case-by-case analysis. In this article, we establish the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in all dimensions. We also disprove the full-rank $B$ conjecture by constructing a compact balanced BTP fivefold with full-rank $B$ that is neither Kähler nor Chern flat; its Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is nonconstant.

</details>


### [101] [Curvature property on Hermitian Lie algebras with abelian ideals of codimension two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26191)
*Shuwen Chen,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究具有余维二交换理想的幺模Hermitian Lie代数的曲率行为，证明常Chern全纯截面曲率蕴含Chern平坦；对非Chern的典范度量联络，常全纯截面曲率蕴含Kähler平坦且商代数为交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在特定代数结构（含余维二交换理想）约束下，Hermitian Lie代数的各种全纯截面曲率条件如何限制其几何（曲率）性质，厘清Chern联络与其他典范度量联络在曲率刚性上的差异。

Method: 在幺模Hermitian Lie代数 $(\mathfrak g,J,g)$ 上，假定存在实余维二的交换理想 $\mathfrak a$，分析度量 $g$ 的曲率张量；分别考察Chern联络与一般典范度量联络 $D_s^r$ 的常全纯截面曲率条件，并借助代数结构（如 $\mathfrak g/\mathfrak a$）进行推理。

Result: 若 $g$ 具有常Chern全纯截面曲率，则 $g$ 必为Chern平坦；对任意非Chern的典范度量联络 $D_s^r$，若其具有常全纯截面曲率，则 $g$ 为Kähler平坦，且此时商代数 $\mathfrak g/\mathfrak a$ 是交换的。

Conclusion: 全纯截面曲率的常值性对该类Hermitian Lie代数施加了强刚性：Chern情形退化为Chern平坦，非Chern典范联络情形进一步退化为Kähler平坦并迫使商代数交换。

Abstract: Let $(\mathfrak g,J,g)$ be a unimodular Hermitian Lie algebra containing an abelian ideal $\mathfrak a$ of real codimension two. We study the curvature behaviour of $g$ and show that, if $g$ has constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature, then it must be Chern flat. We also show that, for every canonical metric connection $D_s^r$ of $g$ other than the Chern connection, if $D_s^r$ has constant holomorphic sectional curvature, then $g$ is Kähler flat, and in this case $\mathfrak g/\mathfrak a$ is abelian.

</details>


### [102] [Integral of Scalar Curvature: Sharp Asymptotic Bounds and Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26357)
*Guoyi Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究非负截面曲率完备非紧流形上标量曲率积分与体积增长（AVR）之间的定量关系，给出上界估计及刚性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非负截面曲率条件下，标量曲率积分受体积增长与几何结构（极点、灵魂）约束；本文旨在建立显式的总标量曲率积分上界并获得等号情形下的刚性刻画。

Method: 对 $3\le n\le6$ 的完备非紧流形，利用体积增长（AVR）与灵魂结构分析，证明 $\lim_{r\to\infty}r^{2-n}\int_{B_p(r)}\Sc_g\,dV_g$ 的上界；对有极点的情形采用独立的距离球面方法；并对闭流形给出尖锐的总标量积分界。

Result: 得到 $\lim_{r\to\infty}r^{2-n}\int_{B_p(r)}\Sc_g\,dV_g\le8\pi\omega_{n-2}(1-\mathrm{AVR}(M^n,g))$ 及相应刚性；正维灵魂情形下界与等号分类成立；有极点时上界改进为 $4\pi\omega_{n-2}(1-\mathrm{AVR}(M^n,g))$。

Conclusion: 标量曲率总积分被体积增长渐近比与几何结构精确控制，等号情形可由刚性分类刻画，且极点与灵魂条件给出更精细的估计。

Abstract: For any complete noncompact manifolds $(M^n, g)$ of nonnegative sectional curvature, with $3\le n\le6$, we obtain $\displaystyle \lim_{r\to\infty}r^{2-n}\int_{B_p(r)}\Sc_g\,dV_g\le8πω_{n-2}(1-\AVR(M^n,g))$ and the corresponding rigidity. The latter bound and its equality classification hold in $(M^n, g)$ with positive-dimensional souls. With a pole, $\displaystyle \lim_{r\to\infty}r^{2-n}\int_{B_p(r)}\Sc_g\,dV_g\le4πω_{n-2}(1-\AVR(M^n,g))$ follows from an independent distance-sphere proof. We also prove the sharp total scalar-integral bound for closed manifolds whose c

</details>


### [103] [Type changing, compact generalized Calabi--Yaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26387)
*Gil R. Cavalcanti*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用Fei在twistor空间分支覆盖上的非Kähler Calabi-Yau构造，首次给出类型改变的广义Calabi-Yau流形的紧致例子。该结构建立在亏格三超椭圆曲面与四环面的乘积Σ₃×𝕋⁴上，一般情形为type 1，并可变为type 3，起点为非Kähler Calabi-Yau结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Calabi-Yau流形的类型会发生改变，但此前缺乏紧致的实例。本文旨在构造首个紧致的类型改变广义Calabi-Yau例子，以验证并丰富该现象。

Method: 采用Fei基于twistor空间分支覆盖构造非Kähler Calabi-Yau流形的方法，在亏格三超椭圆曲面与四环面的乘积Σ₃×𝕋⁴上，从非Kähler Calabi-Yau结构出发构造所需结构。

Result: 在Σ₃×𝕋⁴上成功构造出紧致的广义Calabi-Yau结构，其类型一般情形为type 1，并会发生类型改变变为type 3。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个紧致的类型改变广义Calabi-Yau流形例子，表明此类现象在紧致情形下确实存在。

Abstract: We provide the first compact examples of type-changing generalized Calabi--Yau manifolds using Fei's construction of non-Kähler Calabi--Yau manifolds on branched covers of twistor spaces \cite{Fei16}. The structure, which is generically of type 1 and changes to type 3, is constructed on the product of a hyperelliptic surface of genus three and a four-torus, $Σ_3 \times \mathbb{T}^4$, with starting point a non-Kähler Calabi--Yau structure.

</details>


### [104] [Kählerian oxidation and reduction of Lie algebras with Kähler structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26417)
*Mustapha Bachaou,Ignacio Bajo,Mohamed Louzari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了李代数上Kähler结构的约化与氧化代数框架，证明每个维数大于4的不可分解准幂零Kähler幂零李代数都可通过有限次Kähler中心平面氧化从基代数（{0}、R²或具有强非幂零复结构的代数）逐步重构，且复结构幂零时所有中间约化也保持幂零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李代数（尤其是幂零情形）上的Kähler结构，旨在通过引入约化和氧化的代数框架来刻画幂零李代数上Kähler结构的结构性质，从而对不可分解准幂零Kähler幂零李代数进行分类与重构。

Method: 作者引入李代数经由一维和二维理想的Kähler约化与氧化代数框架，并利用不可分解、准幂零的Kähler幂零李代数的归纳重构方法进行研究。

Result: 主要结构定理表明：每个维数大于4的不可分解准幂零Kähler幂零李代数都可通过有限次Kähler中心平面氧化被归纳重构，其约化序列源自{0}、R²或具有强非幂零复结构的基代数；若复结构幂零，则所有中间约化也具有幂零复结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为幂零李代数上Kähler结构提供了一种归纳式的约化/氧化分类框架，说明其可由基本构件通过中心平面氧化逐步构造，并揭示了复结构幂零性在约化过程中的保持性质。

Abstract: A Kähler structure on a real Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ is a pair $(ω, J)$ of a 2-cocycle and a complex structure on $\mathfrak{g}$ which are compatible in the sense that $J$ is skew-symmetric with respect to $ω$. This paper investigates Kähler structures on Lie algebras, with a particular focus on the nilpotent case. We introduce an algebraic framework for the Kählerian reduction and oxidation of Lie algebras by one-dimensional and two-dimensional ideals. Our main structural result demonstrates that every indecomposable, quasi-nilpotent Kählerian nilpotent Lie algebra of dimension greater than four can be inductively reconstructed through a finite sequence of Kählerian central oxidations by planes. This reduction sequence originates from a base algebra that is either $\{0\}$, $\mathbb{R}^2$, or one admitting a strongly non-nilpotent complex structure. Moreover, if the complex structure is nilpotent, all intermediate reductions also have a nilpotent complex structure.

</details>


### [105] [ALH Gravitational Instantons and Rational Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26439)
*Yifan Chen,Chunhui Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在光滑有理曲面上，围绕零自交反典范椭圆曲线的补集构造了完备的ALH超凯勒度量，并在反向给出ALH引力瞬子的紧化刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Filip–Tosatti提出的存在性问题，并与Haskins–Hein–Nordström所提出的图像相关联。

Method: 构造性方法：在光滑有理曲面上取自交数为零的反典范椭圆曲线，在其补集上构造完备ALH超凯勒度量；并借助诱导复结构对ALH超凯勒引力瞬子进行紧化分析。

Result: 证明了此类度量存在，即该类补集上存在完备ALH超凯勒度量，从而肯定回答了Filip–Tosatti的存在性问题；同时证明了反向结论：每个超凯勒ALH引力瞬子，对每个诱导复结构，都可由有理曲面中的一条反典范椭圆曲线紧化。

Conclusion: 建立了ALH超凯勒度量与有理曲面中反典范椭圆曲线之间的对应关系，给出了存在性与紧化分类的双向刻画。

Abstract: We construct complete ALH hyperkähler metrics on complements of anticanonical elliptic curves with zero self-intersection in smooth rational surfaces. This answers the existence question of Filip--Tosatti, related to the picture proposed by Haskins--Hein--Nordström. Conversely, we prove that every hyperkähler ALH gravitational instanton admits, for each induced complex structure, a compactification by an anticanonical elliptic curve in a rational surface.

</details>


### [106] [The optimal diameter estimate for positive Yamabe metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26455)
*Kazuo Akutagawa,Mijia Lai,Chao Li,Harish Seshadri,Zhixin Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了连通闭流形上正Yamabe度量的最优直径估计，是经典Myers直径估计与Cheng极大直径定理在标量曲率情形下的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Myers直径估计和Cheng极大直径定理均建立在Ricci曲率条件下，而标量曲率情形下是否存在类似的直径估计尚不清楚。作者希望将这两类结果推广到标量曲率的框架中。

Method: 在连通闭流形上，针对正Yamabe度量，通过几何分析手段（涉及Yamabe度量的标量曲率性质）建立并证明直径估计。

Result: 得到了连通闭流形上正Yamabe度量的最优直径估计，该估计被证明是最优的。

Conclusion: 该结果为经典Myers直径估计和Cheng极大直径定理提供了标量曲率版本的对应结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the optimal diameter estimate for positive Yamabe metrics on a connected, closed manifold. It is a scalar curvature version of both classical Myers' diameter estimate and Cheng's maximal diameter theorem on Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [107] [Volume Growth of Self-Shrinkers of the $Q_k$-Flow in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26559)
*Junior Tavares,Detang Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在无曲率假设下，利用适当函数与对称张量的微分不等式，为完备黎曼流形建立一般体积估计，并应用于欧氏空间中一类可定向超曲面的多项式体积增长与有限加权体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体积估计结果多依赖曲率假设，适用范围受限；作者希望去掉曲率条件，以更一般的微分不等式刻画体积增长，从而覆盖更广的超曲面类。

Method: 在完备黎曼流形上，围绕一个适当函数（proper function）与一个对称张量给出合适的微分不等式，据此推导一般性体积估计；再将结论应用于欧氏空间中真浸入的可定向超曲面。

Result: 证明了一类真浸入欧氏空间的可定向超曲面具有至多多项式体积增长，并具有有限加权体积；该类包括Q_k-流的某些自收缩子，以及位置向量法向分量有界的真浸入极小超曲面。

Conclusion: 在不假设曲率的前提下，通过微分不等式获得统一体积估计框架，为研究自收缩子与极小超曲面等几何对象的体积增长提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a general volume estimate for complete Riemannian manifolds under suitable differential inequalities involving a proper function and a symmetric tensor, without imposing curvature assumptions. As an application, we prove that a class of orientable hypersurfaces properly immersed in Euclidean space has at most polynomial volume growth and finite weighted volume. This class includes, in particular, certain self-shrinkers of the $Q_k$-flow and properly immersed minimal hypersurfaces for which the normal component of the position vector is bounded.

</details>


### [108] [$L^2$ Curvature Bounds for Area-Minimizing Currents Mod(2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26592)
*Zachary Lihn*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明模2面积最小流第二基本形式的内点L²先验估计，并导出有界面积模2面积最小曲面的有限微分同胚类结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模2面积最小流缺乏定向性，经典极小曲面正则性工具难以直接适用，且此前对第二基本形式的整体L²控制未知，这限制了曲面的紧性与有限性分类。

Method: 对任意维数n≥2与任意余维数的模2面积最小流，建立第二基本形式的内点L²先验估计，估计量仅依赖维数、余维数与质量上界。

Result: 得到模2面积最小流第二基本形式的内点L²界，该界由维数、余维数和质量上界控制；作为推论，面积有界的模2面积最小曲面只落在有限多个微分同胚类中。

Conclusion: 该结果回答了文献中关于模2面积最小曲面有限微分同胚类的问题，并为模2极小曲面的刚性/紧性研究提供了定量工具。

Abstract: We prove \emph{a priori} interior $L^2$ bounds for the second fundamental form of area-minimizing currents modulo $2$ in every dimension $n\geq 2$ and arbitrary codimension. The bounds depend only on the dimension, codimension, and an upper bound for the mass. As a corollary, we show that area-minimizing surfaces modulo $2$ with bounded area lie in finitely many diffeomorphism classes. This answers a question of \cite{Liu2020WeakCounterexamples}.

</details>


### [109] [Immersed hypersurfaces with positive first Newton transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26665)
*Jiangcheng You,Heng Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了满足第一牛顿变换正定条件的闭合余定向超曲面浸入可界定紧致浸入流形，结合Ros与Pinkall的刚性结果推出常标量曲率闭超曲面必为球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将常标量曲率闭超曲面的刚性（球面性）问题，从已知特殊情形推广到R^{n+1}中一般的闭合余定向超曲面浸入，需要一个更一般的"填充/界定"定理作为桥梁。

Method: 以第一牛顿变换（first Newton transformation）正定为关键假设，构造并证明该类超曲面浸入可界定一个紧致浸入流形，且保持给定的边界映射与外侧余定向；再结合Ros与Pinkall的刚性定理进行推论。

Result: 建立了填充定理：R^{n+1}（n≥2）中具正定第一牛顿变换的任意闭合余定向超曲面浸入，均可界定具给定边界映射和外侧余定向的紧致浸入流形；由此得出R^{n+1}中任一闭合连通常标量曲率超曲面浸入必为圆球面。

Conclusion: 第一牛顿变换正定是保证超曲面可填充的充分条件，并借助该填充定理与已有刚性结果，完整刻画了常标量曲率闭超曲面的球面刚性，推广了经典结论的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove that every closed cooriented hypersurface immersion in $\R^{n+1}$, $n\ge 2$, with positive definite first Newton transformation bounds a compact immersed manifold with the prescribed boundary map and outward coorientation. Combined with the rigidity results of Ros and Pinkall, this filling theorem implies that every closed connected hypersurface immersed in $\R^{n+1}$ with constant scalar curvature is a round sphere.

</details>


### [110] [On the Willmore energy of Möbius bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26745)
*Jacob Bernstein,Daniel Ketover*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中以大圆为边界的 Möbius 带中，最小 Willmore 能量由 Morse 指标为 2 的嵌入极小 Möbius 带实现；作者构造 2 参数"典则族"并用 min-max 方法证明，该族可探测曲面的 Euler 数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中以大圆为边界的 Möbius 带的最小 Willmore 能量及其极小曲面（对应 Lawson Möbius 带），并以此推进 Kusner 1989 年关于 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-不变 Klein 瓶的猜想（即 $\tau_{1,2}$ 最小化 Willmore 能量）。这需要一种能感知非可定向曲面拓扑（Euler 数）的变分框架。

Method: 对任意以大圆为边界的 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中非可定向曲面，构造一个 2 参数"典则族"（canonical family），并用 min-max 论证；该族受 Marques–Neves 探测可定向曲面亏格的 5 参数族启发，能够探测曲面的 Euler 数。

Result: 在以大圆为边界的 Möbius 带中，最小 Willmore 能量由嵌入极小 Möbius 带实现，其 Morse 指标为 2。对 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-不变 Klein 瓶，将 Kusner 1989 猜想归约为 Lawson Möbius 带的若干猜想性刻画之一。

Conclusion: 该 min-max 方法及其典则族为处理 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中非可定向曲面的 Willmore 能量极小化提供了有效工具，并建立了 Möbius 带能量极小与 Klein 瓶猜想之间的联系。

Abstract: We show that among Möbius bands in $\mathbb{S}^3$ bounded by a great circle, the minimal Willmore energy is realized by an embedded minimal Möbius band with Morse index two. To prove this, we introduce a $2$-parameter ``canonical family" associated to any non-orientable surface in $\mathbb{S}^3$ with boundary a great circle and apply a min-max argument. The canonical family detects the Euler number of the surface and is inspired by the $5$-parameter family discovered by F.C. Marques and A. Neves detecting the genus of an orientable surface in $\mathbb{S}^3$. For $\mathbb{Z}_2$-invariant Klein bottles, this reduces R. Kusner's 1989 conjecture that $τ_{1,2}$ minimizes the Willmore energy for a Klein bottle immersed in $\mathbb{S}^3$ to any of several conjectural characterizations of the Lawson Möbius band.

</details>


### [111] [The $J$-Null Locus and Local Regularity of Weak Solutions of the Semistable $J$-Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26340)
*Hao Fang,Biao Ma*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明半稳定$J$-方程中数值$J$-零轨迹与Murakami弱解的$C^2$-奇异轨迹重合，从而该奇异轨迹为具有有限不可约分支的真解析子集，弱解在$J$-零轨迹外局部光滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画半稳定$J$-方程弱解的奇异性结构，建立数值$J$-零轨迹与弱解环境$C^2$-奇异轨迹之间的联系，并给出奇异轨迹的解析性质。

Method: 依赖两个分析工具：(1)奇异$J$-下解的regularization theorem，证明Demailly整体正则化保持定量严格锥条件，使具有指定解析极点的奇异严格下解可在极点集外被光滑严格下解替代；(2)针对这些下解的相对先验估计，包括由行列式控制得到的相对$L^\infty$-估计，以及给出正则轨迹紧子集上一致$C^2$界的加权二阶估计。

Result: 数值$J$-零轨迹与Murakami弱解的环境$C^2$-奇异轨迹重合；该奇异轨迹是真解析子集且具有有限个不可约分支；弱解在$J$-零轨迹外局部光滑。

Conclusion: 半稳定$J$-方程弱解的奇异性完全由$J$-零轨迹刻画，且弱解在其外部光滑，奇异轨迹具有良好的解析几何结构。

Abstract: For the semistable $J$-equation, we prove that the numerical $J$-null locus coincides with the ambient $C^2$-singular locus of Murakami's weak solution. Consequently, this singular locus is a proper analytic subset with finitely many irreducible components, and the weak solution is locally smooth outside the $J$-null locus.
  The proof relies on two analytic ingredients. First, we establish a regularization theorem for singular $J$-subsolutions, showing that Demailly's global regularization preserves quantitative strict cone conditions. Thus, singular strict subsolutions with prescribed analytic poles can be replaced by smooth strict subsolutions away from their pole sets. Second, we derive relative a priori estimates adapted to these subsolutions: a relative $L^\infty$-estimate from determinant control and a weighted second order estimate yielding uniform $C^2$-bounds on compact subsets of the regular locus.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Low-Twist Matrix Covariants of Exterior Powers: Vanishing and Modular Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25029)
*Jingchuan Ma,Yanhua Liu,Qiaoyun Huang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究多项式表示 $\operatorname{Sym}^d(\Lambda^r V)$ 与 $\operatorname{End}(V)\otimes\det(V)^\ell$ 之间的 $GL(V)$-等变映射，确定第二个行列式扭空间，并对最小扭给出任意特征 $\neq 2$ 下 Hom 空间消失的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量矩阵给出必要条件 $rd=n\ell$，该条件筛选出前两个行列式扭；作者希望系统刻画这些扭下的等变映射空间，并区分特征零与一般特征（含非半单情形）的行为。

Method: 特征零情形：将移动三列系数转化为三行外幂 plethysm，把整族约化为 $\operatorname{Sym}^3(k^3)$ 的两个有限外幂分解。最小扭情形：使用 block-swap 符号与 universal root subgroups 构造 actual-Hom 证明。附录采用计算机辅助精确计算。

Result: 特征零下确定第二扭空间：对 $d=2m,n=3m$ 的三向量，证明 $\langle h_{2m}[e_3],s_{(3,2^{3m-2},1)}\rangle=0$，标量系数在 $(2^{3m})$ 处 $m=2$ 时为 1、$m\ge3$ 时为 0。最小扭：若 $r\ge3$ 奇数、$d\ge3$、$n=rd$，则 Hom 空间在任意 $\mathrm{char}\neq2$ 域上消失。附录记录 $\operatorname{Sym}^6(\Lambda^3 k^9)$ 的一个孤立特征五标量类，并解释其 divided-dual 泛函在所有纯六次幂上为零。

Conclusion: 文章完整刻画了前两个行列式扭下的等变映射：第二扭由有限分解完全确定，最小扭在 $\mathrm{char}\neq2$（含非半单奇特征）下普遍消失；特征五存在孤立的例外标量类，凸显正特征现象的特殊性。

Abstract: We study $GL(V)$-equivariant morphisms between the polynomial representations $\operatorname{Sym}^d(Λ^r V)$ and $\operatorname{End}(V)\otimes\det(V)^\ell$. Scalar matrices impose the necessary condition $rd=n\ell$, which singles out the first two determinant twists. In characteristic zero we determine the second-twist spaces for trivectors in the moving family $d=2m$ and $n=3m$. Equivalently, for every $m\geq 2$ we prove $\langle h_{2m}[e_3],s_{(3,2^{3m-2},1)}\rangle=0$, while the scalar coefficient at $(2^{3m})$ equals one for $m=2$ and zero for $m\geq 3$. The proof converts the moving three-column coefficient to a three-row exterior plethysm and reduces the entire family to two finite exterior-power decompositions of $\operatorname{Sym}^3(k^3)$. For the minimal twist we give an actual-Hom proof over every field of characteristic different from two: if $r\geq 3$ is odd, $d\geq 3$, and $n=rd$, then the corresponding Hom space vanishes. This proof uses block-swap signs and universal root subgroups, and therefore remains valid in nonsemisimple odd characteristic. An exact computer-assisted appendix records an isolated characteristic-five scalar class for $\operatorname{Sym}^6(Λ^3 k^9)$ and explains why its divided-dual functional vanishes on all pure sixth powers.

</details>


### [113] [Spectral extremal hypergraphs without long Berge cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25030)
*Lihua Feng,Lu Lu,Tingzeng Wu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文确定了不含长度至少为k的Berge圈的n顶点r-一致超图在充分大n时的最大邻接张量谱半径，并刻画了唯一极值超图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超图中禁止特定长度Berge圈这一Turán型条件下，邻接张量谱半径的极值问题，将图谱Turán理论推广到超图邻接张量情形。

Method: 针对固定整数r≥3与k≥2r+1，记s=⌊(k-1)/2⌋，结合邻接张量谱半径的极值分析与Berge圈禁止条件的结构约束，对充分大n进行渐近极值构造与上界估计。

Result: k=2s+1为奇数时，唯一极值超图由固定s-集外至多含一个顶点的所有r-集组成；k=2s+2为偶数时，额外包含固定s-集外一对顶点与集内r-2个顶点组成的r-集。最大谱半径为spex_r(n,k)=[C(s,r-1)((r-1)/s)^((r-1)/r)+o(1)]n^((r-1)/r)。

Conclusion: 在禁止长度至少为k的Berge圈条件下，超图邻接张量谱半径的极值问题得到渐近确定，且极值结构在奇偶情形下分别由单外部顶点和外部顶点对刻画。

Abstract: Let $r\ge 3$ and $k\ge 2r+1$ be fixed integers. We determine, for all sufficiently large $n$, the maximum adjacency-tensor spectral radius of an $n$-vertex $r$-uniform hypergraph containing no Berge cycle of length at least $k$. Write $s=\left\lfloor\frac{k-1}{2}\right\rfloor$. If $k=2s+1$ is odd, the unique extremal hypergraph consists of all $r$-sets containing at most one vertex outside a fixed $s$-set. If $k=2s+2$ is even, one additionally includes all $r$-sets containing a fixed pair outside the $s$-set together with $r-2$ vertices inside it. Consequently, the maximum spectral radius is given as \[ \operatorname{spex}_r(n,k) = \left[ \binom{s}{r-1} \left(\frac{r-1}{s}\right)^{(r-1)/r} +o(1) \right]n^{(r-1)/r}. \]

</details>


### [114] [Quadratic-Defect Completions of Spherical $2$-Design Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25032)
*Kuan-Cheng Chien,Ming-Hsuan Kang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何通过加入额外轨道，将有限群轨道产生的球面2-设计补全为球面4-设计，并允许一般理论中的权重。通过将2-设计方程的失效保留为二次缺陷，把四次矩问题约化为缺陷的均值与协方差条件，从而给出修正轨道总权重的尖锐下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面设计在有限群轨道上的构造与补全问题具有重要理论意义；已有理论多关注无权重情形，而允许权重的一般框架尚缺乏对补全复杂度（所需轨道数与权重）的定量刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对不可约实正交G-模W，令D=End_G(W)∈{R,C,H}，考虑二重表示W⊕W，将2-设计方程M*M=½I₂的失效记为二次缺陷。当不变四次型由Hermitian Gram矩阵决定时，把四次矩问题约化为缺陷空间上的均值与协方差条件，并据此推导修正轨道总权重的下界及等号时的结构。

Result: 得到修正轨道总权重的尖锐下界；等号时归一化缺陷构成关联缺陷空间中的加权球面2-设计。四次条件对任意维数r≥1的多量子比特Clifford群成立，给出缺陷空间固定为三维的无界维族；在最少修正轨道的等号情形中，缺陷几何恒为正四面体。此外构造了S^11中378点的W(E_6)-不变球面4-设计，并证明其在包含球面2-设计轨道的无权重不变补全中是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作为带权重的球面设计补全提供了统一的二次缺陷与均值-协方差框架，给出了尖锐下界与等号分类，并通过多量子比特Clifford群族和W(E_6)实例展示了理论的适用范围与最优性。

Abstract: We study how spherical $2$-designs arising from finite group orbits can be completed to spherical $4$-designs by adjoining further orbits, allowing weights in the general theory. For an irreducible real orthogonal $G$-module $W$ with $\mathbb D=\operatorname{End}_G(W)\in\{\mathbb R,\mathbb C,\mathbb H\}$, we consider the multiplicity-two representation $W\oplus W$ and retain the failure of the $2$-design equation $M^*M=\frac12 I_2$ as a quadratic defect. When the invariant quartics are determined by the Hermitian Gram matrix, the fourth-moment problem reduces to a mean and covariance condition on these defects. This yields a sharp lower bound for the total weight of the correction orbits; at equality, their normalized defects form a weighted spherical $2$-design in the associated defect space. The quartic condition holds for the multiqubit Clifford groups in every dimension $r\geq 1$, giving an unbounded-dimensional family with a fixed three-dimensional defect space; among equality cases using the minimum number of correction orbits, the defect geometry is always a regular tetrahedron. As a complementary unweighted example, we construct a $378$-point $W(E_6)$-invariant spherical $4$-design in $S^{11}$ and prove that it is sharp among unweighted invariant completions containing a spherical $2$-design orbit.

</details>


### [115] [Discrete Concavity of Token-Graph Spectral Radii via Lorentzian Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25037)
*Weiqi Jiang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限图 k-令牌图的加权邻接与度矩阵之和 A_k+ϑD_k 的最大特征值随 k 的变化规律，证明其离散凹性、单调性，进而验证谱半径单调性猜想，并给出基于洛伦兹多项式与热核的对数凹性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apte、Parekh 和 Sud 提出了无符号拉普拉斯谱半径与邻接谱半径关于令牌层 k 的单调性猜想。作者希望用统一框架证明更一般的 ϑ 参数族谱单调性，并揭示其背后的组合/代数结构。

Method: 将各令牌层编码为一个洛伦兹多项式；证明四变量算子符号在每条边热门上保持洛伦兹性，并通过 Lie-Trotter 公式将该保持性传递到整个半群；利用大时间增长率恢复最大特征值；再由热含量在 k 上的对数凹性推出谱序列的离散凹性。

Result: 对任意 -1≤ϑ≤1，k↦λ_max(A_k+ϑD_k) 离散凹；结合补对称性得序列在中层前非减。取 ϑ=1 与 ϑ=0 即得无符号拉普拉斯与邻接谱半径单调性猜想。对任意 t≥0，热含量 C(n,k)^{-1}1*e^{t(A_k+ϑD_k)}1 关于 k 对数凹。相同构造还从顶部谱投影产生洛伦兹多项式，并证明局部符号恰好刻画参数区间 [-1,1]。

Conclusion: 该工作以洛伦兹多项式与热半群为工具，统一证明了令牌图谱半径的凹性与单调性猜想，并将有效的 ϑ 参数范围精确限定为 [-1,1]。

Abstract: Let $F_k(G)$ be the $k$-token graph of a finite graph with nonnegative edge weights, and let $A_k$ and $D_k$ be its weighted adjacency and degree matrices. For every $-1\leq\vartheta\leq1$, we prove that $k\mapstoλ_{\max}(A_k+\vartheta D_k)$ is discretely concave. Complement symmetry then makes this sequence nondecreasing up to the middle level. At $\vartheta=1$ and $\vartheta=0$, this gives the signless-Laplacian and adjacency spectral-radius monotonicity conjectures of Apte, Parekh, and Sud. The spectral result follows from a finite-time theorem: for every $t\geq0$, the heat contents $\binom{n}{k}^{-1}\mathbf{1}^{*}e^{t(A_k+\vartheta D_k)}\mathbf{1}$ are log-concave in $k$. We encode all token levels in one Lorentzian polynomial. A four-variable operator symbol proves preservation by each edge heat gate, and the Lie-Trotter formula passes this preservation to the full semigroup. Large-time growth rates recover the top eigenvalues. The same construction also yields Lorentzian polynomials from top spectral projections. Finally, we show that the local symbol certifies exactly the parameter range $[-1,1]$.

</details>


### [116] [A Vertex-Linear Threshold for Eventually Turán good and the Cluster Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25038)
*Xiamiao Zhao,Liying Kang,Yuanpei Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文肯定地回答了 Morrison 等人关于 Turán-good 阈值能否降至 v(H) 二次阶的问题，进一步给出线性阶阈值：存在绝对常数 C>0，使任意含边图 H 在 r≥Cv(H) 时严格 K_{r+1}-Turán-good，并应用新方法改进了 Fadnavis 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Morrison、Nir、Norin、Rzążewski 和 Wesolek 证明了对任意含边图 H，当 r≥300v(H)^9 时 H 是 K_{r+1}-Turán-good，并提问该界能否降到 v(H) 的二次阶。本文旨在回答并强化这一问题。

Method: 采用基于聚合物模型（polymer models）的显著不同的簇方法（cluster method），证明顶点线性阶的 Turán-good 阈值。

Result: 证明存在绝对常数 C>0，使任意含边图 H 在 r≥Cv(H) 时严格 K_{r+1}-Turán-good；并证明对任意含边图 H，归一化色多项式 P_H(x)/x^{v(H)} 在所有实数 x≥CΔ(H) 上严格递增，改进了此前 x≥CΔ(H)^{3/2} 的最佳结果。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 Morrison 等人的问题并将阈值降至顶点线性阶，聚合物模型簇方法具有进一步应用；作为示例，证明了 Fadnavis 的猜想。

Abstract: A graph $H$ is $K_{r+1}$-Turán-good if, for every sufficiently large $n$, the Turán graph $T_r(n)$ maximizes the number of copies of $H$ among all $n$-vertex $K_{r+1}$-free graphs, and it is strictly $K_{r+1}$-Turán-good when this extremal graph is unique. Morrison, Nir, Norin, Rzążewski and Wesolek proved that for every graph $H$ with at least one edge, when $r\ge 300v(H)^9$, $H$ is $K_{r+1}$-Turán-good. They asked whether the above bound could be reduced to quadratic order in $v(H)$.
  In this paper, we answer their question affirmatively, and give a stronger result.
  We prove that there is an absolute constant $C>0$ such that every graph $H$ with at least one edge is strictly $K_{r+1}$-Turán-good whenever $r\ge Cv(H)$. Our proof uses a substantially different cluster method based on polymer models. Besides proving the vertex-linear Turán-good threshold, this method appears to have further applications. As one illustration, we prove that for every graph $H$ with at least one edge, the normalized chromatic polynomial $P_H(x)/x^{v(H)}$ is strictly increasing for every real $x\ge CΔ(H)$, which strengthens the previously best result where $x\ge CΔ(H)^{3/2}$. This proves a conjecture of Fadnavis.

</details>


### [117] [A square-root law for equitable coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25041)
*Mohammad F. Marashdeh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在顶点数足够大（|V(G)|≥3χ(G)Δ）的图中，公平染色数由√(Δ/lnΔ)主导而非最大度Δ，精确确定了固定色数下图的最小阶增长Θ(√(Δ/lnΔ))，并给出构造性上界与匹配的概率下界构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知所有适用于一般图的公平染色数上界（自Hajnal–Szemerédi定理起）都与最大度Δ呈线性关系，且星图K_{1,Δ}（其公平染色数为⌈Δ/2⌉+1）表明不存在低于Δ/2的一般性上界。作者希望说明这一障碍源于顶点数不足而非度本身的影响，从而在顶点数足够大时大幅改进上界。

Method: 对满足|V(G)|≥3χ(G)Δ的图给出公平染色数的上界证明；分析色数χ(G)≤(Δ/lnΔ)^{1/3}范围内上界O(χ(G)^{3/2}√(Δ/lnΔ))；对固定ℓ，给出ℓ-可染色大阶图的公平染色数上界(2√2·ℓ√(ℓ-1)+o(1))√(Δ/lnΔ)；并构造概率性下界实例（ℓ=2时为二部图），得到≥(1/3)√((ℓ-1)Δ/lnΔ)；此外通过带预设锚点的构造变体在线性时间内产生二部图的公平染色。

Result: 在|V(G)|≥3χ(G)Δ时，χ(G)≤(Δ/lnΔ)^{1/3}范围内有χ_e(G)=O(χ(G)^{3/2}√(Δ/lnΔ))；固定ℓ的ℓ-可染色大阶图上界为(2√2ℓ√(ℓ-1)+o(1))√(Δ/lnΔ)；概率构造给出任意大阶（ℓ=2时为二部）的下界(1/3)√((ℓ-1)Δ/lnΔ)。因此增长阶Θ(√(Δ/lnΔ))对每个固定色数精确成立，极值常数确定到因子O(χ(G))。所有上界均为构造性的；带锚点构造可在最优线性时间内得到二部图O(√Δ)色的公平染色。

Conclusion: 公平染色数在顶点数足够大时不再受最大度Δ线性支配，而由√(Δ/lnΔ)主导，色数控制其余部分；该增长阶对固定色数精确，且极值常数仅在O(χ(G))因子内存在不确定性。上界的构造性与二部图的线性时间算法使其具有算法意义。

Abstract: An equitable $k$-coloring of a graph partitions its vertex set into $k$ independent sets whose sizes differ by at most one; the least such $k$ is the equitable chromatic number $\chie(G)$. Every known bound on $\chie$ valid for all graphs, beginning with the Hajnal--Szemerédi theorem, is linear in the maximum degree $Δ$, and the star $K_{1,Δ}$, for which $\chie=\ceil{Δ/2}+1$, shows that no general bound below $Δ/2$ exists. We prove that this obstruction is a shortage of vertices rather than an effect of the degree: every graph with $|V(G)|\ge3χ(G)Δ$ satisfies $\chie(G)=O\bigl(χ(G)^{3/2}\sqrt{Δ/\lnΔ}\bigr)$ throughout the range $χ(G)\le(Δ/\lnΔ)^{1/3}$, so that for graphs of large order the degree enters only through $\sqrt{Δ/\lnΔ}$, with the chromatic number governing the rest. For each fixed $\ell$, $\ell$-colorable graphs of sufficiently large order satisfy $\chie\le\bigl(2\sqrt2\,\ell\sqrt{\ell-1}+o(1)\bigr)\sqrt{Δ/\lnΔ}$, while a probabilistic construction supplies graphs of arbitrarily large order, bipartite when $\ell=2$, with $\chie\ge\tfrac13\sqrt{(\ell-1)Δ/\lnΔ}$: the order of growth $Θ\bigl(\sqrt{Δ/\lnΔ}\bigr)$ is exact for every fixed chromatic number, and the extremal constant is determined up to a factor $O(χ(G))$. All upper bounds are constructive, and a prescribed-anchor variant of the construction produces equitable colorings of bipartite graphs with $O(\sqrtΔ)$ colors in optimal linear time.

</details>


### [118] [The unique extremal threemodular lattice of rank 26, the generalized hexagon $(2,8)$, and the tight Cayley-plane $5$-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25086)
*Gerald Höhn*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文从抽象偶秩-26格重构出阶为$(2,8)$的广义六边形，并反向从六边形重构格；调和theta恒等式确定了819个最短向量及其关联方案，证明正范数壳层为球面5-设计；进一步通过Cayley平面投影3-设计的三阶矩重构无迹Albert积，证明紧819点投影5-设计的几何唯一性；最后通过线删除得到$N(A_1^{24})$的无根指数四子格，利用Golay三元组与定向粘合证明唯一性并确定自同构群阶为${}^3D_4(2):3$（完整格群含中心因子$C_2$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立广义六边形与高维格之间的双向重构，揭示组合设计与格论、Cayley平面几何之间的深层联系，并为相关对象的唯一性与自同构群提供统一证明框架。

Method: 采用调和theta恒等式确定最短向量与关联方案；利用秩-26幂等元经短对偶陪集论证饱和性重构格；通过正定Niemeier构造证明存在唯一性；用Cayley平面投影3-设计的三阶矩重构无迹Albert积；最后以线删除构造$N(A_1^{24})$的指数四子格，结合Golay三元组、定向粘合与$L_3(2)\cong L_2(7)$在八个根符号轨道上的作用证明唯一性并确定群阶。

Result: 重构了$(2,8)$阶广义六边形与秩-26格；确定819个最短向量及其关联方案，证明各非空正范数壳层与其对偶均为球面5-设计；证明格的唯一存在性从而六边形唯一；将保角双射升级为$F_4(\mathbb R)$元素，证明紧819点投影5-设计的几何唯一性；构造出含Golay三元组和定向粘合的指数四子格，证明其唯一性并给出自同构群阶，识别为${}^3D_4(2):3$，完整格群含中心因子$C_2$。

Conclusion: 广义六边形与秩-26格可相互重构，且相关设计与子格结构具有唯一性；重构与删除论证与长度-26二进制码构造平行，统一了格、设计与Cayley平面几何的自同构描述。

Abstract: We reconstruct the generalized hexagon of order $(2,8)$ from an abstract even rank-$26$ lattice of determinant $3$ and minimum $4$, and conversely reconstruct the lattice from the hexagon. Harmonic theta identities determine the $819$ shortest vectors in a non-zero discriminant class and their association scheme, and show that every non-empty positive-norm shell of the lattice and its dual is a spherical $5$-design. In the converse direction, the rank-$26$ idempotent gives the lattice, with saturation proved by a short dual-coset argument. A positive-definite Niemeier construction proves existence and uniqueness of the lattice and hence of the hexagon.
  The third moment of a projective $3$-design in the Cayley plane reconstructs the traceless Albert product. This upgrades angle-preserving bijections to elements of $F_4(\mathbb R)$ and proves geometric uniqueness of the tight $819$-point projective $5$-design. Finally, line deletion gives a rootless index-four sublattice of $N(A_1^{24})$ with a Golay trio and an oriented gluing. The gluing data form eight root-sign orbits, on which $L_3(2)\cong L_2(7)$ acts as on $\mathbf P^1(\mathbf{F}_7)$; two explicit Golay permutations prove transitivity. This proves uniqueness and gives the automorphism-group order. The group is identified afterward as ${}^3D_4(2):3$, with a central factor $C_2$ for the full lattice group. The reconstruction and deletion arguments parallel the length-$26$ binary code construction.

</details>


### [119] [Latin Eulerian Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25100)
*Madjid Mirzavaziri,Daniel Yaqubi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 引入拉丁欧拉数作为经典欧拉数的多变量精细化，研究其对称性与总上升统计量的极值下界及分布性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典欧拉数仅统计单序列的上升位置，而拉丁方具有多重列结构；作者希望用多变量精细化刻画拉丁方按列上升的分布，并理解总上升统计量的可达范围与分布形状。

Method: 定义拉丁欧拉数，统计n阶拉丁方按列上升次数；建立基本对称性、单变量边缘分布与精确重数整除性质；对总上升统计量Σ(L)使用符号置换（经典枚举中通常被忽略）给出精确公式，并进行显式构造。

Result: 证明Σ(L)的尖锐孤立界n-1≤Σ(L)≤(n-1)^2，且相邻值n与(n-1)^2-1严格不可达；给出中间范围的显式构造，并由此提出总上升分布的单峰性猜想。

Conclusion: 拉丁欧拉数为拉丁方的列上升提供统一的多变量框架，其总上升统计量具有孤立的极值与由符号置换精确控制的中间结构；总上升分布的单峰性仍为猜想。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Latin Eulerian numbers} $\LE{n\atop k_1,\dots,k_n}$, a multivariate refinement of classical Eulerian numbers counting order-$n$ Latin squares by column ascents. We establish their fundamental symmetries, univariate marginals, and an exact multiplicity divisibility property. For the total ascent statistic $Σ(L)=\sum_i k_i(L)$, we prove the sharp, isolated bounds $n-1 \le Σ(L) \le (n-1)^2$, demonstrating that the adjacent values $n$ and $(n-1)^2-1$ are strictly unattainable. To analyze intermediate values, we show that symbol permutations typically ignored in classical enumeration directly govern $Σ$ via an exact formula. This insight yields explicit constructions for the interior range, motivates a unimodality conjecture for the total ascent distribution.

</details>


### [120] [The weighted union-set conjecture is false](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25101)
*Yimeng Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否定了由Gowers提出的union-set猜想的一个加权推广，构造了满足条件却使每个坐标仅占极小权重的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frankl提出的union-set猜想存在加权推广，Gowers（文献[Gow16]）曾提出该推广，本文旨在检验其是否成立并给出反例。

Method: 在有限地面集的子集上构造非负权函数w，满足w(∅)=0且对任意子集A、B有w(A∪B)≥min{w(A),w(B)}，同时使每个坐标所属子集的权重占总权重的比例可任意小。

Result: 成功构造出反例，证明该加权推广的union-set猜想不成立；每个坐标仅出现在总权重中任意小的比例内。

Conclusion: 该加权推广的union-set猜想（[Gow16]所提）被否定，表明其原始形式不能被直接加权化。

Abstract: We disprove a weighted generalization of the union-set conjecture [Fra95] raised in [Gow16]. We construct nonnegative weights on subsets of a finite ground set satisfying $w(\varnothing)=0$ and $w(A\cup B)\ge\min\{w(A),w(B)\}$, while every coordinate belongs to an arbitrarily small fraction of the total weight.

</details>


### [121] [Characterization of tree-child networks in terms of mu vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25177)
*Toni Fuentes,Vincent Moulton,Katharina T. Huber,Joan Carles Pons*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文用μ-表示刻画tree-child系统发育网络，给出基于有序树路径分解的结构刻画，并将其转化为N^n中有有限子集M的纯向量条件，证明M是tree-child网络μ-表示当且仅当M满足tree-child μ-相容性，从而提供可判定准则，为重构与算法应用奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tree-child系统发育网络的μ-表示缺乏便于判定的可行性准则，限制了基于μ-表示的重构与算法化处理；作者希望在向量层面刻画该类网络的表示特征。

Method: 先对tree-child网络进行有序树路径分解的结构刻画，再把该分解翻译为N^n中有有限子集M上的纯向量条件，并据此定义tree-child μ-相容性作为判定标准。

Result: 证明了M是某个tree-child系统发育网络的μ-表示，当且仅当M满足tree-child μ-相容性，给出了该表示的可行性判据。

Conclusion: 该可行性准则可作为基于μ-表示的重构及后续算法应用的基础；文中结果源于进行中的研究，将在后续版本中进一步完善并置入更完整的研究脉络。

Abstract: We characterize tree-child phylogenetic networks in terms of their mu-representations. First, we give a structural characterization of tree-child networks by means of ordered tree-path decompositions. We then translate this decomposition into a set of purely vectorial conditions on finite subsets M in N^n. We prove that such a set M is the mu-representation of a tree-child phylogenetic network if and only if it is tree-child mu-compatible. This provides a feasibility criterion for tree-child mu-representations which can be used as a basis for reconstruction and further algorithmic applications. Note that this paper presents results arising from ongoing research on tree-child networks and that the results will be further developed and placed into proper context in subsequent versions.

</details>


### [122] [On Smooth Combinatorial Products of Simplices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25181)
*Juliana Curtis,Tuong Le,Chayim Lowen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明光滑多面体（组合同构于单纯形乘积）的格弱Minkowski被加项都具有二次三角形剖分，通过将其等同于Nakajima多面体，给出此类多面体的弱Minkowski被加项相对IDP的新证明，并证明了一类2-面为三角形和梯形的简单多面体的结构定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光滑多面体的格弱Minkowski被加项的三角形剖分性质及其相对整分解性质（IDP），并借助与Nakajima多面体的联系统一处理这类多面体。

Method: 将组合同构于单纯形乘积的光滑多面体这一类与Nakajima多面体类进行等同识别，并结合关于2-面为三角形和梯形的简单多面体的结构定理进行论证。

Result: 证明了此类光滑多面体的每个格弱Minkowski被加项都具有二次三角形剖分；并证明2-面为三角形和梯形的简单多面体的结构定理。

Conclusion: 给出了同一光滑多面体的两个格弱Minkowski被加项相对IDP的新证明，表明该性质可由二次三角形剖分与Nakajima多面体的刻画导出。

Abstract: We show that every lattice weak Minkowski summand of a smooth polytope combinatorially isomorphic to a product of simplices has a quadratic triangulation. This is achieved by identifying this class of polytopes with the class of Nakajima polytopes. We combine these results to give a new proof that two lattice weak Minkowski summands of the same such smooth polytope are relatively IDP. Along the way, we prove a structure theorem for simple polytopes whose 2-faces are triangles and trapezoids.

</details>


### [123] [Maximizing the number of cliques in $K_{r+1}$-free graphs with forbidden properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25196)
*Aleyah Dawkins,Rachel Kirsch*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在 $K_{r+1}$-free 图中研究避免多种哈密顿相关性质的极值边数与 $t$-团数，推广了此前关于非哈密顿图的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ferrero 与 Lesniak（2018）给出了 $r$-部非哈密顿图的最大边数；作者此前进一步确定了 $K_{r+1}$-free 图中非哈密顿图及满足 Pósa 定理相关低度顶点条件的图的最大边数与 $t$-团数。本文旨在利用上述第二个定理，将对哈密顿性的刻划推广到更一般的图性质。

Method: 应用关于低度顶点条件的定理 (2)，将定理 (1) 从哈密顿性推广到其他性质。具体地，确定 $K_{r+1}$-free 图中分别避免可迹性、哈密顿连通性、$k$-路径哈密顿性、$k$-哈密顿性、$k$-哈密顿连通性以及 $k$-连通性的图的最大边数与 $t$-团数，并刻划达到最大边数的所有极值图。同时证明了对避免任意稳定性质（该性质对足够大的完全图成立）的 $K_{r+1}$-free 图，其边数与 $t$-团数的上界。

Result: 确定了 $K_{r+1}$-free 图中避免可迹性、哈密顿连通性、$k$-路径哈密顿性、$k$-哈密顿性、$k$-哈密顿连通性和 $k$-连通性的图的最大边数与 $t$-团数，并找出所有达到最大边数的极值图；此外给出了避免任意稳定性质的 $K_{r+1}$-free 图中边数与 $t$-团数的上界。

Conclusion: 本文将 Ferrero–Lesniak 型非哈密顿图极值结果系统推广至更广泛的图性质，利用低度顶点条件统一处理多类哈密顿相关性质，并为一般稳定性质提供了边数与 $t$-团数上界，刻划了相应极值结构。

Abstract: Ferrero and Lesniak in 2018 found the maximum numbers of edges in $r$-partite non-Hamiltonian graphs. Recently we found the maximum numbers of edges and $t$-cliques in $K_{r+1}$-free graphs (1) that are not Hamiltonian or (2) that satisfy a condition on low-degree vertices related to Pósa's theorem. Applying theorem (2), here we extend theorem (1) from Hamiltonicity to other properties. We determine the maximum numbers of edges and $t$-cliques in $K_{r+1}$-free graphs that avoid one of the following properties: traceability, Hamiltonian-connectedness, $k$-path Hamiltonicity, $k$-Hamiltonicity, $k$-Hamiltonian-connectedness, and $k$-connectedness. We find all extremal graphs having the maximum numbers of edges. On the way, we prove upper bounds on the numbers of edges and $t$-cliques in $K_{r+1}$-free graphs that avoid an arbitrary stable property that holds for sufficiently large complete graphs.

</details>


### [124] [An infinite family of intransitive directed strongly regular graphs with rank 6 Weisfeiler--Leman closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25282)
*Štefan Gyürki*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 构造了一族无限的有向强正则图，其全自同构群非传递，且Weisfeiler–Leman闭包是秩为6的结合方案，达到存在真DSRG合并的结合方案的最小秩；并给出具有相同闭包性质的刚性零星DSRG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨有向强正则图（DSRG）的Weisfeiler–Leman闭包结构及其最小可能秩，并揭示组合正则性、群论对称性与WL正则性刻画的是图结构的不同层面。

Method: 构造无限族非传递全自同构群的DSRG，计算其Weisfeiler–Leman闭包（结合方案）并确定其秩；同时构造具有相同闭包性质的刚性零星DSRG进行对比。

Result: 得到无限族DSRG，其全自同构群非传递、WL闭包为秩6的结合方案，且该秩为实现真DSRG合并的结合方案的最小可能秩；另获得具有相同闭包性质的刚性零星DSRG。

Conclusion: 组合正则性、群论对称性与Weisfeiler–Leman正则性刻画的是图结构本质不同的方面。

Abstract: We construct an infinite family of directed strongly regular graphs (DSRGs) with intransitive full automorphism groups whose Weisfeiler--Leman closure are association schemes of rank 6. This is the smallest possible rank for an association scheme admitting a proper DSRG merging. We also exhibit rigid sporadic DSRGs with the same closure property, showing that combinatorial regularity, group-theoretic symmetry, and Weisfeiler--Leman regularity capture fundamentally different aspects of graph structure.

</details>


### [125] [A Polytopal Realization of Higher-Categorical Associahedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25316)
*Spencer Backman,Nathaniel Bottman,Daria Poliakova*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文给出范畴 n-结合面体（categorical n-associahedra）的一个多面体实现，其法扇是作者此前提出的 velocity fan 的一个修改，该修改由 OpenAI 的 Astra 模型发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范畴 n-结合面体此前主要以抽象或组合方式刻画，缺乏显式的多面体（polytopal）实现。作者希望构造一个具体的多面体，使其法扇与先前引入的 velocity fan 相关联，从而将组合/范畴结构与凸几何联系起来。

Method: 构造一个多面体实现，并研究其法扇；通过对作者先前的 velocity fan 进行修改（modification）得到新的扇结构，进而由该扇确定多面体。该修改的发现借助了 OpenAI 的 Astra 模型。

Result: 得到范畴 n-结合面体的多面体实现，其法扇恰为修改后的 velocity fan。

Conclusion: 范畴 n-结合面体可被实现为具有特定法扇的多面体，表明 velocity fan 的适当修改可作为其正规扇；同时展示了 AI 工具在数学构造发现中的作用。

Abstract: We describe a polytopal realization of categorical $n$-associahedra. The normal fan of this polytopal realization is a modification of the authors' velocity fan and was found by OpenAI's Astra model.

</details>


### [126] [Asymmetric Homomorphism Thresholds for Graphs of Large Odd Girth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25390)
*Romain Bourneuf,Raphael Steiner,Stéphan Thomassé,Yuval Wigderson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定从奇围长至少7的图到无三角形图（及K_t）的同态阈值，证明δ_hom({C3,C5},{C3})=1/9、δ_hom({C3,C5},{K_t})=1/3t，并推广至任意奇围长情形，从而否定相关猜想，且与零色数阈值形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同态阈值（homomorphism threshold）这一介于色数阈值与同态之间的问题，探究从高奇围长图到受限目标类是否存在正阈值，并检验Gishboliner、Hurley和Wigderson提出的相关猜想。

Method: 下界构造基于高维Borsuk图；上界证明使用正则性引理（regularity arguments）以恢复这些构造背后的结构。

Result: 证明δ_hom({C3,C5},{C3})=1/9；推广得δ_hom({C3,C5},{K_t})=1/3t（t≥3）；并证明对任意真单调类C，奇围长至少7的图到有界大小C中图的同态阈值均为正；进一步推广到任意奇围长2k+3到奇围长至少2k-1的真单调子类，阈值亦为正。

Conclusion: 上述结果否定了Gishboliner、Hurley和Wigderson的猜想，并揭示了同态阈值与相应零色数阈值结果之间的尖锐对比。

Abstract: We determine the asymmetric homomorphism threshold from graphs of odd girth at least $7$ to triangle-free graphs, showing that $δ_{\mathrm{hom}}(\{C_3,C_5\},\{C_3\})=\frac{1}{9}$. Equivalently, for every $\varepsilon>0$, every $n$-vertex graph of odd girth at least $7$ and minimum degree at least $(1/9+\varepsilon)n$ admits a homomorphism to a triangle-free graph of size bounded by a function of $\varepsilon$, while there exist graphs of odd girth at least $7$ and minimum degree at least $(1/9-\varepsilon)n$ for which no such bounded-size triangle-free homomorphic image exists.
  More generally, for every $t\geq 3$, we prove $δ_{\mathrm{hom}}(\{C_3,C_5\},\{K_t\})=\frac{1}{3t}$. In particular, for every proper monotone class $\mathcal C$ of graphs, the threshold for graphs of odd girth at least $7$ to admit a homomorphism to a bounded-size graph in $\mathcal C$ is positive. We further extend this phenomenon to arbitrary odd girth: for every $k\geq 2$ and every proper monotone subclass $\mathcal C$ of the class of graphs of odd girth at least $2k-1$, the threshold for graphs of odd girth at least $2k+3$ to admit a homomorphism to a bounded-size graph in $\mathcal C$ is positive.
  These results disprove conjectures of Gishboliner, Hurley and Wigderson and exhibit a sharp contrast with the corresponding zero chromatic-threshold results. Our lower-bound constructions are based on high-dimensional Borsuk graphs, while the matching upper bounds use regularity arguments to recover the structure underlying these constructions.

</details>


### [127] [On inversion sets of joins in weak Bruhat order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25395)
*Ming Yean Lim*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出有限 Coxeter 群在弱序下计算并（join）的递归算法，并证明了 Dyer 关于并的反演集等于各因子反演集之并的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众所周知任意 Coxeter 群关于弱序构成交半格，有限时构成格；但有限情形下弱序中并的具体计算，以及 Dyer 关于并的反演集刻画猜想尚未完整解决。

Method: 第一部分：针对任意有限 Coxeter 群 W，提出一个递归算法来计算弱序下的并。第二部分：在 W 有限的假设下，证明 Dyer 的猜想，即将并的反演集表示为各因子反演集的并。

Result: 得到了有限 Coxeter 群弱序下并的递归计算算法；并在有限情形下完成了 Dyer 猜想的证明，给出并用各因子反演集之并表示的公式。部分证明与 Claude Opus 5 协作完成。

Conclusion: 有限 Coxeter 群弱序中的并可被有效递归计算，且其反演集可由各因子反演集之并刻画，从而完整验证了 Dyer 的猜想。

Abstract: It is well-known that any Coxeter group $W$ is a meet-semilattice with respect to weak order. Furthermore, when $W$ is finite, it is a lattice. In the first part of this paper, we give a recursive algorithm that computes joins in weak order for arbitrary finite $W$. In the second part, we complete the proof of a conjecture of Dyer expressing the inversion set of a join in terms of the union of inversion sets of each factor, under the assumption that $W$ is finite.
  Some proofs were developed in collaboration with Claude Opus 5.

</details>


### [128] [Counterexamples to the Ramos conjecture for two hyperplanes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26723)
*Florian Frick*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了 $4n-2$ 个非退化高斯测度，证明其不能被两个仿射超平面同时等分，从而推翻了两超平面情形的 Ramos 猜想，并刻画了高斯等分阈值与相位恢复所需正定二次测量数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多个概率测度能否被有限个仿射超平面同时等分（equipartition）这一几何测度论问题，特别关注 Ramos 猜想在两超平面情形下的正确性，以及等分所需最小维数与测度数量的定量关系。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{R}^{6n-3}$ 上构造 $4n-2$ 个非退化高斯测度作为反例；结合已知上界给出最小维数公式；将高斯等分阈值与相位检索（phase retrieval）所需的正定二次测量最小数目建立等价刻画；利用改进的复多项式乘法构造正定二次测量。

Result: 对每个 $n\ge2$ 构造出 $4n-2$ 个非退化高斯测度，不能被两个仿射超平面同时等分，推翻 Ramos 猜想；证明 $3\cdot2^{s-1}-2$ 是保证 $2^s-2$ 个绝对连续概率测度存在公共两超平面等分的最小维数（$s\ge3$）；改进的复多项式乘法在偶数维 $r\ge4$ 中给出 $2r-2$ 个正定测量，且在 $r=2^k+2\ (k\ge1)$ 时达到最优。

Conclusion: Ramos 猜想在两面超平面情形不成立；高斯等分问题可通过相位检索的正定二次测量数进行刻画，所得构造在特定维度下达到最优。

Abstract: For every $n\ge2$, we construct $4n-2$ nondegenerate Gaussian measures on $\mathbb{R}^{6n-3}$ that cannot be simultaneously equipartitioned by two affine hyperplanes. This disproves the Ramos conjecture for two hyperplanes. Combined with known upper bounds, the construction shows that $3\cdot2^{s-1}-2$ is the least dimension guaranteeing a common two-hyperplane equipartition of $2^s-2$ absolutely continuous probability measures, for every $s\ge3$. We characterize the Gaussian equipartition threshold in terms of the least number of positive definite quadratic measurements needed for phase retrieval. Modified complex polynomial multiplication gives $2r-2$ positive definite measurements in every even dimension $r\ge4$. This number is optimal when $r=2^k+2$, $k\ge1$.

</details>


### [129] [Back Stable Standard Elementary Monomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25445)
*Carlos Rodriguez*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入后稳定标准初等单项式及其完全齐次类似物，它们构成后稳定Schubert环的基，并用其刻画左稳定Schubert多项式的展开；通过Dynkin反转交换两基，轨道和恢复Stanley对称函数的系数，并将移位特殊化多项式用后稳定标准初等单项式系数表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究后稳定Schubert环的基结构，理解左稳定Schubert多项式的展开系数，并揭示其与Stanley对称函数及Fomin–Kirillov移位特殊化多项式之间的联系。

Method: 引入后稳定标准初等单项式及其完全齐次类比，构造后稳定Schubert环的两组基；利用Dynkin反转研究两基的交换关系；通过轨道和将后稳定系数与Stanley对称函数的初等、完全齐次系数对应；最后分析计数约化pipe dreams的移位特殊化多项式。

Result: 后稳定标准初等单项式及其完全齐次类比构成后稳定Schubert环的基，记录左稳定Schubert多项式的最终标准初等单项式展开；Dynkin反转交换两基且保持系数不变；后稳定系数的轨道和恢复Stanley对称函数的初等与完全齐次系数；移位特殊化多项式可用后稳定标准初等单项式系数表示。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了后稳定Schubert环中标准初等单项式基与完全齐次基的对偶结构，深化了对Schubert多项式稳定性、Stanley对称函数及移位特殊化多项式的统一理解。

Abstract: We introduce back stable standard elementary monomials and their complete homogeneous analogues. These families form bases of the back stable Schubert ring and record the eventual standard elementary monomial expansions of left-stabilized Schubert polynomials. Dynkin reversal exchanges the two bases while preserving their coefficients. We also show that orbit sums of the back stable coefficients recover the elementary and complete homogeneous coefficients of Stanley symmetric functions. Finally, we study the shifted specialization polynomial $\mathcal{T}_w(t)=\mathfrak{S}_{1^t\times w}(1)$, considered by Fomin and Kirillov, which counts reduced pipe dreams of $1^t\times w$. We express this polynomial in terms of back stable standard elementary monomial coefficients.

</details>


### [130] [A Refined Sum-Product Estimate via Higher Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25711)
*Kaiqiang Zhang,Yuyu Wang,Yuanguo Zeng*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 结合Rudnev–Stevens乘法斜率估计、Cushman高能正则化、Shakan的$d^+$–$d^\times$分解与Solymosi经典和积估计，证明了改进的和积不等式$|AA|^{204}|A+A|^{301}\gtrsim|A|^{675}$，从而得到$\max\{|A+A|,|AA|\}\gg_\varepsilon|A|^{135/101-\varepsilon}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在和积问题中，人们关心$\max\{|A+A|,|AA|\}$相对$|A|$的下界指数。经典方法给出次二次增长下界，但指数仍有提升空间。本文旨在通过整合近期若干强力工具，推进和积指数的当前记录。

Method: 证明围绕两条中间估计展开：对$B\subset\mathbb{R}_{>0}$有$d^\times(B)|BB|^{12}|B+B|^{16}\gtrsim|B|^{38}$；对$U\subset\mathbb{R}$有$d^+(U)^{17}|U+U|^{29}\gtrsim|U|^{46}$。核心手段是复合Rudnev–Stevens乘法斜率估计、Cushman高能正则化、Shakan的$d^+$–$d^\times$分解与Solymosi和积估计，通过能量与覆盖数（$d^+$、$d^\times$）的相互作用导出双变量不等式，再组合为整体$|AA|^{204}|A+A|^{301}\gtrsim|A|^{675}$。

Result: 得到$\max\{|A+A|,|AA|\}\gg_\varepsilon|A|^{135/101-\varepsilon}$，对任意$\varepsilon>0$成立；等价地由$|AA|^{204}|A+A|^{301}\gtrsim|A|^{675}$推出，指数$135/101\approx1.3366$，较此前记录有所提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过联合斜率估计、高能正则化、$d^+$–$d^\times$分解等现代工具，可在实数域上进一步推进和积指数的下界，且方法框架具有可扩展性。

Abstract: Let $A\subset\mathbb{R}$ be a finite set. Combining the multiplicative slope estimate of Rudnev--Stevens, Cushman's higher-energy regularization, Shakan's $d^+$--$d^\times$ decomposition, and Solymosi's classical sum--product estimate, we prove \[ |AA|^{204}|A+A|^{301}\gtrsim |A|^{675}, \] where $\gtrsim$ suppresses a fixed polylogarithmic factor in $|A|$. Consequently, for every $\varepsilon>0$, \[ \max\{|A+A|,|AA|\}\gg_\varepsilon |A|^{135/101-\varepsilon}. \] The proof is organized around two intermediate estimates. For every nonempty finite set $B\subset\mathbb{R}_{>0}$, \[ d^\times(B)|BB|^{12}|B+B|^{16}\gtrsim |B|^{38}, \] whereas for every nonempty finite set $U\subset\mathbb{R}$, \[ d^+(U)^{17}|U+U|^{29}\gtrsim |U|^{46}. \]

</details>


### [131] [A common interleaver for two antichain polynomials on $[k]$$\times$ $P_{n,s}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25717)
*Jian Ding,Lingen Ding*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在Jiang工作的基础上，将方法从[k]×[2]×[n]推广至[k]×P_{n,s}（P_{n,s}为两行Ferrers形状），证明了反链生成多项式相关的猜想4.2，并由此蕴含着猜想4.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一作者与Dong提出了关于反链生成多项式的三个猜想；Jiang已证明关于实根性和γ-正性的猜想4.3与4.5，本文致力于证明剩余的猜想4.2，并利用其蕴含关系补全相关结论。

Method: 将Jiang处理[k]×[2]×[n]的方法推广到[k]×P_{n,s}（P_{n,s}为两行Ferrers形状）；对一族与相邻形状相关的二元多项式建立实稳定性，再应用Chudnovsky-Seymour相容性准则获得公共交错多项式（common interleaver）。

Result: 证明了猜想4.2，并建立了相邻形状相关二元多项式族的实稳定性，同时得到相应的公共交错多项式。

Conclusion: 猜想4.2得证；结合[DD]中的蕴含关系，猜想4.2也蕴含猜想4.3，从而与Jiang关于4.5的结果共同推进了对上述三个猜想的完整解决。

Abstract: The first author and Dong \cite{DD} proposed three conjectures on antichain generating polynomials. Jiang \cite{Jiang} recently proved Conjectures 4.3 and 4.5, concerning real-rootedness and \(γ\)-positivity. We prove Conjecture 4.2 by adapting his method from \([k]\times [2] \times [n]\) to \([k]\times P_{n,s} \), where \(P_{n,s}\) is a two-row Ferrers shape. We establish real stability for a family of bivariate polynomials associated with adjacent shapes, then apply the Chudnovsky-Seymour compatibility criterion to obtain a common interleaver. As noted in \cite{DD}, Conjecture 4.2 also implies Conjecture 4.3.

</details>


### [132] [Large Planar Point Sets Contain 4 Collinear Points or Almost 7-Cliques, and Related Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25727)
*Bhaswar B. Bhattacharya,Sandip Das,Sk Samim Islam,Aashirwad Mohapatra,Saumya Sen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了足够大的平面点集要么含四点共线，要么含七个点且至多一对不可见；并将可见性图中保证的图类从六点完全图推广到色数≤5的图及特定条件下的色数6图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 Bonnet (2026) 证明了足够大的平面点集（无四点共线）中存在六点两两可见，即可见性图包含 K6。本文旨在推进这一结果，向 big-line-big-clique 猜想的下一步（K7）逼近，并把保证的单一点集结构推广为更一般的图嵌入。

Method: 通过极值与结构组合方法分析平面点集的可见性图（两点可见指连线不经过其他点），利用色数条件与 color-critical edge 刻画可嵌入的图类。

Result: 足够大的有限平面点集要么含四点共线，要么含七点且至多一对不可见。更一般地，对任意固定图 H，若其色数≤5，或色数为6且含 color-critical edge，则足够大且无四点共线的平面点集的可见性图必包含 H 的副本。

Conclusion: 结果推广了 Bonnet 的六点可见结论，将保证的图类扩展到更广的色数条件，并距 big-line-big-clique 猜想下一开放情形（从七点可见减少一条可见边）仅差一步。

Abstract: We prove that every sufficiently large finite planar point set contains either four collinear points or seven points with at most one non-visible pair. More generally, we show that for every fixed graph $H$ with chromatic number at most five, or with chromatic number six and a color-critical edge, the visibility graph of every sufficiently large finite planar point set with no four collinear points contains a copy of $H$. These results extend the recent breakthrough of Bonnet (2026), guaranteeing six pairwise visible points, and come within one visibility edge of the next open case of the big-line-big-clique conjecture.

</details>


### [133] [Zero-Run Spectra of the $(3,2)$ Raney numbers Modulo Primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25742)
*Sen-Peng Eu,Zai-Ting Huang,Louis Kao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究(3,2)-Raney数模素数p的零游程结构，分p=2、p=3、p≥5三种情形分别刻画零游程长度、谱及非零项数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示(3,2)-Raney数模素数p后零游程的分布规律，理解p的取值如何导致截然不同的组合结构。

Method: 对每个素数p确定零游程长度的从左至右极大值、完整零游程谱，以及0≤n<p^m中非零项的精确计数；p=2用Fibbinary整数、Fibonacci数与Jacobsthal数刻画奇数项及极大值位置，p=3刻画非零项及其剩余，p≥5用模p幂的残差区间多尺度系统。

Result: 结果按p=2、p=3、p≥5分为三类且行为差异显著；p=2给出奇数项刻画与极大值位置，p=3给出非零项及其剩余，p≥5由多尺度残差区间系统得到记录值与完整零游程谱。

Conclusion: (3,2)-Raney数的零游程结构对模素数呈现三分情形，其规律分别由不同算术与组合工具刻画。

Abstract: We study the zero-run structure of the $(3,2)$-Raney numbers modulo a prime $p$. For every prime $p$, we determine the left-to-right maxima of the zero-run lengths, the complete zero-run spectrum, and the exact number of nonzero entries in $0\le n<p^m$.
  Interestingly, these results fall into three cases: $p=2$, $p=3$, and $p\geq5$, and the behaviors in these three cases are very different. For $p=2$, we characterize exactly the odd terms and determine the positions of the left-to-right maxima and the results involve Fibbinary integers, Fibonacci numbers, and Jacobsthal numbers. For $p=3$, we characterize exactly the nonzero terms and determine their residues. For $p\ge 5$, the zero runs are governed by a multiscale system of residue intervals modulo powers of $p$, from which both the record values and the complete zero-run spectrum are obtained.

</details>


### [134] [Some results on the distance spectral radius and edge-disjoint spanning trees of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25794)
*Yongbin Gao,Ligong Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文补全并推广了基于距离谱半径的边不相交生成树数目($\tau(G)\ge k$)的谱极值条件，覆盖了此前遗留的阶数区间 $2k\le n\le2k+5$，并在最小度条件下及一个更强最小度条件下给出了相应的尖锐条件与唯一极图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fan、He 与 Zhao 此前给出了 $n\ge2k+6$ 时使 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 成立的尖锐距离谱半径条件，但区间 $2k\le n\le2k+5$ 尚未解决。本文旨在填补该缺口，使结果对所有 $n\ge2k$ 完整，并进一步在最小度约束下推广相关刻画。

Method: 以距离谱半径 $\rho_D(G)$ 作为参数，通过谱极值理论与图结构分析，确定各阶数区间下的极图：证明 Fan-He-Zhao 极图在 $n\ge2k+2$ 仍有效，并针对 $n=2k$ 与 $n=2k+1$ 确定唯一极图；在 $\delta(G)\ge k$ 下刻画所有极图；在 $\delta(G)\ge6k-4$ 且 $n\ge2\delta(G)+2$ 时给出尖锐条件与唯一极图。

Result: 补全了 $2k\le n\le2k+5$ 的缺口，使 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 的尖锐距离谱半径条件对所有 $n\ge2k$ 成立；Fan-He-Zhao 极图对 $n\ge2k+2$ 依旧有效，而 $n=2k$ 与 $n=2k+1$ 各有唯一极图；在 $\delta(G)\ge k$ 下得到尖锐条件并刻画全部极图；在 $\delta(G)\ge6k-4$、$n\ge2\delta(G)+2$ 时亦得到保证 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 的尖锐条件及唯一极图。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了距离谱半径与边不相交生成树数目之间的极值问题，并在最小度条件下给出更精细的极图刻画，为相关谱极值研究提供了完整结论。

Abstract: Let $τ(G)$ denote the maximum number of edge-disjoint spanning trees in a connected graph $G$ of order $n$, and let $ρ_D(G)$ denote its distance spectral radius. For an integer $k\ge2$, Fan, He and Zhao [Discrete Appl. Math. 376 (2025) 31--40] obtained a sharp distance spectral radius condition for $τ(G)\ge k$ when $n\ge2k+6$. In this paper, we fill the gap $2k\le n\le2k+5$ and thus complete the result for all $n\ge2k$. The extremal graph given by Fan, He and Zhao remains valid for $n\ge2k+2$, while we determine the unique extremal graph for each of the orders $n=2k$ and $n=2k+1$. We further obtain sharp distance spectral radius conditions and characterize all extremal graphs under the minimum degree condition $δ(G)\ge k$ for all $n\ge2k$. Finally, for graphs with the stronger minimum degree condition $δ(G)\ge6k-4$ and order $n\ge2δ(G)+2$, we obtain a sharp distance spectral radius condition ensuring $τ(G)\ge k$ and determine the unique extremal graph.

</details>


### [135] [Exact Ehrhart Series of Birkhoff Polytopes via Constant Terms and Finite-Field Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25863)
*Xinru Jiang,Guoce Xin,Chen Zhang,Yueming Zhong*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出计算Birkhoff多面体Ehrhart级数与World Cup问题计数级数的精确方法，借助常数项、有限域与对称函数技巧，将计算复杂度降到每素数O_n(m^{n-5}+m^4)，并完整给出到12阶的级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Birkhoff多面体的Ehrhart级数（等价于行和列和均为r的非负整数矩阵计数H_n(r)）以及仅对角线为0的World Cup问题，此前缺乏高效精确的计算手段，需要新的代数组合方法以获得更高阶的完整级数。

Method: 用常数项与有限域方法：通过根过滤器将H_n(r)表示为h_r(M)^n的加权和，其中h_r为完全齐次对称多项式，M取遍r+1次单位根的多重集；利用常数项消去将h_r(M)的求值化为对M中重复元素a的求和，涉及次数为μ_a-2的广义Todd系数；再用对称函数技巧处理选定重数类上h_r(M)^n的求和。对每个可容许素数，算法需O_n(m^{n-5}+m^4)次域运算（固定n≥5）。

Result: 结合显式上界与中国剩余定理恢复整数计数，并用Ehrhart对称性确定完整级数。证明方法正确，并计算了两族问题直到12阶的完整级数；附录中列出Birkhoff级数10–12阶和World Cup级数9–12阶。

Conclusion: 该常数项与有限域方法为Birkhoff多面体及World Cup问题提供了精确且相对高效的计算框架，成功将已知完整级数推进到12阶，验证了方法的正确性与可扩展性。

Abstract: The Ehrhart series of the $n$th Birkhoff polytope is $\sum_{r\geq0}H_n(r)z^r$, where $H_n(r)$ counts nonnegative integer $n\times n$ matrices whose row and column sums all equal $r$. We present an exact method for computing this series using constant terms and finite fields. A root filter expresses $H_n(r)$ as a weighted sum of the values $h_r(M)^n$, where $h_r$ is the complete homogeneous symmetric polynomial and $M$ ranges over multisets of $m$th roots of unity with $m=r+1$. Constant-term cancellation reduces the evaluation of $h_r(M)$ to a sum over repeated elements $a$ of $M$. For a particular $a$ of multiplicity $μ_a$, the computation uses a generalized Todd coefficient of degree $μ_a-2$. Sums of $h_r(M)^n$ over selected multiplicity classes are handled using symmetric function techniques. Together with the remaining individual evaluations, this gives $O_n(m^{n-5}+m^4)$ field operations for each admissible prime and fixed $n\geq5$. An explicit bound and the Chinese remainder theorem recover the integer counts, and Ehrhart symmetry determines the full series. The same method applies to the World Cup problem, which counts the same matrices with diagonal entries required to be $0$. We prove correctness and compute complete series for both families through order $12$. The Birkhoff series for orders $10$--$12$ and the World Cup series for orders $9$--$12$ are tabulated in the appendices.

</details>


### [136] [Structural Characterizations and Algebraic Realizations of a Family of Regular Integral Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25957)
*Tapa Manna,Supriyo Dutta,Baby Bhattacharya*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类新的整谱图族：其构造方式不同于Cayley图，建立了谱唯一性定理，给出由小图生成大图的递归构造，并研究了其作为有限2-群Proper Prime Order Element Graph补图的代数实现及非同构群产生同谱图的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整谱图（所有特征值均为整数）极为稀少，在所有n顶点图中的占比渐近趋于零（2^{-Ω(n)}），构造新的整谱图族具有挑战性；且已有无穷族大多依赖交换群上的Cayley图，因此需要一种非Cayley型的新构造途径。

Method: 从群出发构造一类新图族，其构造方式区别于Cayley图；建立谱唯一性定理；提出从小规模图递归生成更大规模同族图的构造方法；并将这些图作为有限2-群的Proper Prime Order Element Graph的补图进行代数实现，给出刻画此类实现的条件。

Result: 所得图族为无穷族且具有可扩展性；每张图在其邻接谱下于所有有限简单图中被唯一确定；给出了成为有限2-群Proper Prime Order Element Graph补图的充要/刻画条件；发现来自不同非同构群的图之间存在同谱图。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一类非Cayley型、由群出发构造的整谱图无穷族，兼具谱唯一性与递归可扩展性，并通过与有限2-群相关图的补图实现建立联系，同时揭示了非同构群可产生同谱图。

Abstract: All the eigenvalues of an integral graphs are integers. Integral graphs are extremely rare. They form an asymptotically vanishing fraction $2^{-Ω(n)}$ among all graphs on $n$ vertices. It makes the construction of a new family of integral graphs a challenging task. Also, most of the known infinite family of integral graphs rely on Cayley graphs over Abelian groups. In this article, we introduce a new family of integral graphs obtained from the groups. The construction of our graphs from groups is different from the construction of Cayley graphs. A spectral uniqueness theorem is established, which shows that each member of the infinite family is determined by its adjacency spectrum among all finite simple graphs. We also present recursive constructions that generates larger members of the family from smaller ones, providing a scalable class of integral graphs. Finally, we investigate algebraic realizations of these graphs as complements of Proper Prime Order Element Graphs of finite $2$-groups and obtain conditions characterizing such realizations. We also observe that the graphs obtained from different non-isomorphic groups have cospectral graphs.

</details>


### [137] [A Sharp Product Bound for Disjoint Cross-Intersecting 3-Graphs with Covering Number Three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26206)
*Arthur F. Ramos,David B. Hulak,Ruy J. G. B. de Queiroz*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Lin、Frankl和Wu关于两个不相交的、覆盖数为3的3-一致交叉相交超图大小乘积上界为100的猜想，并给出达到该界的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lin、Frankl和Wu已证明两个覆盖数为3的3-一致交叉相交超图大小乘积不超过121，并猜想若两族不相交则该紧界降为100。本文旨在证明这一猜想。

Method: 证明较小族至多有11条边；固定后可将另一族扩充为其3-横截的外部族。按匹配数分类，归结为一个初等相交情形与两个有限内核。证明两种编码及每条剪枝规则的正确性，并用C++20穷举计算得到外阻塞最大值（族大小4至11对应21,19,16,14,12,11,10,9），构造性见证用Python独立验证。

Result: 当较小族大小为4至11时，外部阻塞最大值依次为21、19、16、14、12、11、10、9；六个顶点上两个十边族达到乘积100。

Conclusion: 猜想成立：不相交条件下两个覆盖数为3的3-一致交叉相交超图大小乘积的紧界为100，且该界可由六个顶点上的两个十边族达到。

Abstract: Lin, Frankl, and Wu proved that the product of the sizes of two cross-intersecting 3-uniform hypergraphs with covering number three is at most 121. They conjectured that requiring the families to be disjoint lowers the sharp bound to 100. We prove this conjecture. The smaller family has at most eleven edges; after fixing it, the other family may be enlarged to its external family of 3-transversals. Splitting by matching number leaves an elementary intersecting case and two finite kernels. We prove the correctness of both encodings and every pruning rule, and exhaustive C++20 computations give the external-blocker maxima 21, 19, 16, 14, 12, 11, 10, 9 for family sizes 4 through 11. Concrete witnesses are checked independently in Python. A pair of ten-edge families on six vertices attains product 100.

</details>


### [138] [First Subconstituents of Orthogonal Graphs in Even Characteristic:Automorphisms, Cliques, Cores, and Binary Triple Transitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26240)
*Kai Zhou,Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 在偶特征下，正交图奇异点的第一子成分被证明是仿射极图的补图，由此确定其全自同构群、独立数、色数、团结构及核，并给出二元局部图三重传递性的简洁证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画正交图第一子成分的组合与代数结构，确定其自同构群、独立集、色数、团数等不变量，并揭示其与仿射极图、卵形集、Delsarte团的关系。

Method: 利用偶特征下第一子成分为仿射极图补图的识别；用仿射半相似群描述全自同构群；将最大独立集刻画为极大全奇异子空间的仿射陪集；通过基点的卵形集与Delsarte团建立双射；对F2情形精确计算最大团数并证明其单轨道性，求核；用统一的Witt扩张论证证明二元局部图的三重传递性。

Result: 全自同构群为仿射半相似群并可显式扩张至环境图；最大独立集是极大全奇异子空间的仿射陪集，由此得到独立数与色数；plus型中Delsarte团与通过基点的卵形集双射，导出尖锐的秩阈值；F2上确定各维最大团数，证明所有最大团构成单一轨道，并得到每个第一子成分的核；给出二元局部图三重传递性的简短证明。

Conclusion: 偶特征正交图第一子成分的结构可通过仿射极图补图统一刻画，相关代数与组合不变量（自同构群、独立数、色数、团数、核、传递性）被完整确定，并建立了Delsarte团与卵形集的对应及尖锐秩阈值。

Abstract: In even characteristic, the first subconstituent of an orthogonal graph at a singular point is shown to be the complement of an affine polar graph. This identification yields the full automorphism group as an affine semisimilarity group and provides explicit extensions to the ambient graph. Maximum independent sets are affine cosets of maximal totally singular subspaces, giving the independence and chromatic numbers. In plus type, Delsarte cliques correspond bijectively to ovoids through the base point, leading to a sharp rank threshold. Over $\mathbb{F}_2$, we determine the exact maximum clique number in every dimension, prove that all maximum cliques form a single orbit, and obtain the core of every first subconstituent. A uniform Witt-extension argument gives a short proof of triple transitivity for the binary local graphs.

</details>


### [139] [Sparse chromatic graphs and the complete-graph triangle bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26247)
*Shuyan Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了：若图G的色数至少为r且边数至多cr³log²r（c>0为绝对常数），则G至少含binom(r,3)个三角形。证明分三步：稀疏核论证、阶数一致稳定性定理、以及含大团时的精确界。关键技术包括Harris的边—三角着色估计、Fox–Tidor–Zhang的修正独立集论证，以及新的稠密公共调色板列表型边—三角估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究色数与三角形数量的定量关系，可视为对Erdős–Rado型问题及K_r-free图极值问题的细化。旨在确立：色数达到r所需的边数下界若被控制在接近线性（cr³log²r）范围内，则图必然被迫产生至少binom(r,3)个三角形，从而给出色数—三角形计数之间的精确阈值型刻画。

Method: 证明由三个组件构成。第一，基于Harris的三角形敏感着色估计的稀疏核论证，从任意反例中抽取一个阶为O(r)、色数至少(1-η)r的诱导子图。第二，证明阶数一致稳定性定理：对任意β>0存在γ>0（与线性阶界中的常数无关），使得任意足够大的、阶为O(s)且团数ω(J)≤(1-β)s的s-临界图J至少含有binom(s,3)+γs³个三角形；该部分结合超额方法与Fox–Tidor–Zhang的修正独立集论证。第三，证明当图含有阶至少(1-δ)r的团时的精确界，使用新的稠密公共调色板列表型边—三角估计：若每个列表占据固定公共调色板的正比例，则边—三角着色界在常数因子内保持成立。

Result: 存在绝对常数c>0，使得任意色数至少为r且边数至多cr³log²r的图至少包含binom(r,3)个三角形。同时建立了阶数一致的稳定性定理（临界图在团数受限时三角形数超过binom(s,3)达γs³）以及大团情形下的精确界；并给出公共调色板列表型边—三角估计，证明在固定正比例占用下该着色界至多差一个常数因子。

Conclusion: 论文在色数与三角形计数之间建立了定量联系，给出边数上限为cr³log²r时三角形数的精确下界binom(r,3)。其贡献在于引入稀疏核抽取、阶数一致稳定性以及公共调色板列表型边—三角估计等工具，统一处理了无大团与含大团两类情形，为相关极值与着色问题提供了新方法。

Abstract: We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that every graph of chromatic number at least $r$ and at most $cr^3\log^2 r$ edges contains at least $\binom r3$ triangles. The proof has three ingredients. First, a sparse-core argument based on a triangle-sensitive coloring estimate of Harris extracts, from any counterexample, an induced subgraph of order $O(r)$ and chromatic number at least $(1-η)r$. Second, we prove an order-uniform stability theorem: for every $β>0$ there is $γ>0$, independent of the constant in the linear order bound, such that every sufficiently large $s$-critical graph $J$ of order $O(s)$ with $ω(J)\le (1-β)s$ has at least $\binom s3+γs^3$ triangles. The proof combines the excess method and the modified-independent-set argument of Fox, Tidor, and Zhang. Third, we prove the exact bound when the graph contains a clique of order at least $(1-δ)r$. This uses a new dense common-palette list analogue of Harris's edge--triangle estimate: if every list occupies a fixed positive proportion of a common palette, then the edge-triangle coloring bound survives up to a constant factor.

</details>


### [140] [Proofs of two conjectures of Berkovich and Dhar on hook lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26324)
*Rong Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Berkovich与Dhar两个猜想的更强形式，涉及有界分拆中长度为2的钩对及系数不等式，并给出组合与解析两类证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berkovich与Dhar提出了关于有界分拆中长度2钩对计数及系数不等式的猜想，作者希望证明并加强这些结果，从而比较奇分拆与互异分拆中此类钩对的总数。

Method: 组合证明：基于Glaisher双射构造显式的保权单射；解析证明：利用有限乘积恒等式与已知钩生成函数。

Result: 证明了两个猜想的更强形式：系数不等式成立，并由此推出相应钩对不等式；对固定大小及最大部分共同上界，奇分拆中此类钩对总数不少于互异分拆中的总数。

Conclusion: 系数不等式蕴含钩对不等式，所得结果强化了Berkovich与Dhar的猜想，且组合与解析两种途径相互印证。

Abstract: We prove stronger forms of two conjectures of Berkovich and Dhar concerning a coefficientwise inequality and pairs of hooks of length two in bounded partitions. The coefficientwise inequality implies the corresponding inequality for hook pairs. In particular, for each fixed size and common bound on the largest part, the total number of such pairs in odd partitions is at least that in distinct partitions. We give combinatorial proofs by explicit weight-preserving injections based on Glaisher's bijection and analytic proofs using finite product identities and known hook generating functions.

</details>


### [141] [Rainbow spanning configurations in uniformly coloured pseudorandom graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26329)
*Elad Aigner-Horev,Dan Hefetz,Yury Person,Michael Trushkin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个针对均匀边染色伪随机图中彩虹生成结构的定量调色板转移原理，并将其应用于K_k-因子、完美匹配、哈密顿圈和给定有界度生成树的彩虹版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在伪随机图中，生成结构的嵌入结果已经较为成熟，但在边染色情形下寻找彩虹副本（即所有边颜色互异）仍缺乏系统工具。作者希望建立一个"转移原理"，把已有的（非染色）生成结构嵌入结论自动转化为均匀边染色图中的彩虹嵌入结论，从而统一处理多种生成结构。

Method: 提出定量调色板转移原理：输入是生成结构在满足适当bijumbled条件且最小度足够大的图H中的嵌入结果；输出是在一个均匀边染色图G中该结构的彩虹副本几乎必然存在，其中G的bijumbled度与最小度与H相当（有时相同）。随后将该原理应用于bijumbled图，得到K_k-因子（含完美匹配）、哈密顿圈以及给定有界度生成树的彩虹版本。

Result: 在所有结果中，调色板大小比目标结构规模多出εn（ε为任意小但固定的正常数，n为结构阶数），并在此条件下几乎必然得到相应彩虹结构的存在性。

Conclusion: 该转移原理提供了一种从非染色嵌入结果导出彩虹嵌入结果的通用框架，统一涵盖了K_k-因子、完美匹配、哈密顿圈和有界度生成树等多种生成结构，且调色板规模的额外冗余仅为εn。

Abstract: We prove a quantitative palette-transference principle for rainbow spanning configurations in uniformly edge-coloured pseudorandom graphs. The input to our transference principle is an embedding result of a spanning configuration in an appropriately bijumbled graph $H$ with sufficiently large minimum degree. The output of our transference principle is the asymptotically almost sure existence of a rainbow copy of the same configuration in a uniformly edge-coloured graph $G$ whose bijumbledness and minimum are comparable and sometimes coincide with those of $H$.
  We then apply our transference principle in order to asymptotically almost surely obtain $K_k$-factors, including perfect matchings, Hamilton cycles, and a prescribed bounded-degree spanning tree in bijumbled graphs with appropriate parameters.
