In all of our results, the palette size exceeds the size of the target configuration by $\varepsilon n$, where $\varepsilon > 0$ is arbitrarily small yet fixed, and $n$ is the order of the configuration.

</details>


### [142] [Pairs of lattice complements with complementary homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26397)
*Sara Faridi,Dharm Veer,Volkmar Welker*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究有限格序复形的同调是否存在"整体到局部"原理：格在度数 a+b-2 的同调非零是否迫使存在一对格补元，其下方开区间分别在度数 a 和 b 上同调非零。作者用多分次自由消解的语言重述该问题，给出支持性证据，并通过反例否定了该问题的若干强化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格序复形同调的"整体到局部"联系是代数组合与交换代数中的核心关切；若成立，可将高阶同调的非零性归约到局部开区间的同调性质。作者同时受多分次自由消解框架的驱动，希望在此语境下检验这一原理及其强化形式。

Method: 以有限格的格序复形（order complex）为对象，考察度数 a+b-2 的同调非零性；引入格补元（lattice complements）及其下方开区间，分析其同调度数；并将问题翻译为多分次自由消解（multigraded free resolutions）的语言进行等价刻画。

Result: 提供了支持该类格补元对存在的正面证据；同时对原问题的强化版本构造了反例，表明更强的"整体到局部"断言不成立。

Conclusion: 有限格格序复形同调的"整体到局部"原理在弱形式下获得正面证据支持，但其强化版本被反例否定；多分次自由消解的表述为后续研究提供了统一的框架。

Abstract: In this paper we study whether there is a global to local principle for the homology of order complexes of finite lattices. We explore whether the non-vanishing of the homology of a lattice in degree $a+b-2$ forces the existence of a pair of lattice-complements whose open intervals below have non-vanishing homology in degrees $a$ and $b$ respectively. We consider the same question also in the language of multigraded free resolutions. We provide positive evidence for the existence of such pairs and give counterexamples to strengthenings of the question.

</details>


### [143] [Minimising the harmonic sum of cycle lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26401)
*Richard Montgomery,Aleksa Milojević,Alexey Pokrovskiy,Benny Sudakov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Erdős 1981年提出的关于二部图最小化 $s(G)=\sum_{\ell\in C(G)}1/\ell$ 的猜想，对充分大的 $k$ 成立，并证明了 $K_{k,n-k}$ 在相应边数条件下的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解图的密度与其环长谱丰富程度的定量关系是极值图论的核心主题。Erdős 与 Hajnal 于1966年提出用 $s(G)=\sum_{\ell\in C(G)}1/\ell$ 作为环长谱 $C(G)$ 的丰富程度度量，Erdős 由此提出一系列逐渐加强的猜想，认为在平均度相同或边数相同的条件下完全二部图最小化 $s(G)$。

Method: 论文针对 Erdos 1981 年的最强猜想展开研究：在 $n$ 个顶点且边数至少为 $k(n-k)$（$k\leq n/2$）的图中，$K_{k,n-k}$ 最小化 $s(G)$。作者证明了一个更强的结论：对任意满足 $e(G)>(k-1)(n-k+1)$ 且 $n\geq 2k$ 的 $n$ 阶图 $G$，均有 $s(G)\geq\sum_{\ell=2}^{k}1/(2\ell)$。

Result: 对充分大的 $k$，论文完整证明了 Erdos 1981 年的此猜想；进一步证明了更强的边数阈值形式 $e(G)>(k-1)(n-k+1)$，$n\geq 2k$ 时成立 $s(G)\geq\sum_{\ell=2}^{k}1/(2\ell)$。

Conclusion: 完全二部图 $K_{k,n-k}$ 是边数至少为 $k(n-k)$ 的图中达到上述 $s(G)$ 下界等号成立的唯一图，从而解决了 Erdos 猜想并给出了唯一性刻画。

Abstract: A central theme in extremal graph theory is to understand the relationship between the density of a graph and the richness of its cycle length spectrum, which is the set of distinct cycle lengths occurring in the graph. In 1966, Erdős and Hajnal suggested studying $s(G):=\sum_{\ell\in{C}(G)}1/\ell$ as a measure of the richness of the cycle length spectrum ${C}(G)$ of a graph $G$.
  Through a series of increasingly strong conjectures, Erdős suggested that the complete bipartite graphs minimise $s(G)$ among all graphs $G$ with the same average degree. The sharpest such conjecture, from 1981, states that the graph $K_{k, n-k}$ minimises $s(G)$ among all $n$-vertex graphs with at least $k(n-k)$ edges (where $k\leq n/2$). We prove this conjecture for all sufficiently large $k$, by showing the stronger statement that any $n$-vertex graph $G$ with $e(G)>(k-1)(n-k+1)$ and $n\geq 2k$ satisfies $s(G)\geq\sum_{\ell=2}^{k}1/(2\ell)$. Moreover, we show that the complete bipartite graph $K_{k,n-k}$ is the unique graph with at least $k(n-k)$ edges that achieves equality here.

</details>


### [144] [Simple symmetric Venn diagrams with 17 and 19 curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26546)
*Chris Dzoba*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 构造出17条和19条曲线的简单旋转对称Venn图，并用Lean 4形式化验证部分证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前简单旋转对称Venn图仅知到13条曲线，而已知素数条曲线的对称Venn图含多重交点(非简单)。本文旨在获得更大规模的简单对称Venn图。

Method: 在球面旋转不变四边形剖分上，用Metropolis游走搜索(允许区域暂时重复)，起点为Griggs-Killian-Savage图的去重解。

Result: 得到4个17曲线图与9个19曲线图，均为简单且旋转对称；每个图附可机器检验的证书，每尺寸各一份证书用Lean 4形式化证明。所有图均非单调，故交叉序列搜索方法无法发现。

Conclusion: 首次给出17、19条曲线的简单旋转对称Venn图，验证手段为可机器检验证书与Lean 4正式证明。

Abstract: We exhibit simple, rotationally symmetric Venn diagrams with 17 curves and with 19 curves: n Jordan curves carried to one another by rotation through 2π/n, with every one of the 2^n regions present and connected and, since the diagrams are simple, every crossing on exactly two curves. Symmetric Venn diagrams exist for every prime number of curves (Griggs, Killian and Savage, 2004), but those diagrams have many curves through a point; simple ones were known only up to 13 curves (Mamakani and Ruskey, 2014). Four 17-curve and nine 19-curve diagrams were found by a Metropolis walk on rotation-invariant quadrangulations of the sphere in which regions may temporarily be duplicated, started from the Griggs-Killian-Savage diagram with its multiple crossings resolved. Every diagram is given by a machine-checkable certificate; one certificate of each size has been verified by a formal proof in Lean 4. All of the diagrams are non-monotone, which is why the crossing-sequence searches that found the 11- and 13-curve diagrams could not have found them.

</details>


### [145] [The Erdős--Hajnal hypergraph Ramsey problem for $r_4(6,n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26563)
*Longma Du,Xinyu Hu,Ruilong Liu,Guanghui Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $r_4(6,n)\ge 2^{2^{cn}}$，从而对任意固定 $k\ge4$ 验证了 $r_k(k+2,n)$ 的 Erdős–Hajnal 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ramsey 数 $r_k(s,n)$ 的下界增长是极值组合的核心问题。Erdős–Hajnal 猜想其应达到 $k-1$ 层塔函数级别，此前仅在 $s\ge k+3$ 时被验证；$s=k+2$ 这一临界情形长期未解决。

Method: 通过构造性下界方法，针对 $k=4$、$s=6$ 情形给出 $2^{2^{cn}}$ 级别的 Ramsey 下界，并推广到一般 $k\ge4$ 的 $r_k(k+2,n)$。

Result: 证明 $r_4(6,n)\ge 2^{2^{cn}}$（$c>0$ 为绝对常数），改进作者此前的下界，并由此确认所有固定 $k\ge4$ 时 $r_k(k+2,n)$ 的 Erdős–Hajnal 猜想。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了 $s=k+2$ 临界情形下的 Erdős–Hajnal 猜想，将 Ramsey 数下界推进至塔函数级别。

Abstract: The Ramsey number $r_k(s,n)$ is the smallest integer $N$ such that every $N$-vertex $k$-graph contains either a copy of $K_s^{(k)}$ or an independent set of size $n$. Erdős and Hajnal conjectured that for every fixed $s>k\ge 4$, one has $r_k(s,n)\ge \operatorname{twr}_{k-1}(Ω(n))$. This conjecture was independently verified by Mubayi and Suk, and by Conlon, Fox and Sudakov, for $k\ge4$ and $s\ge k+3$. In this paper, we prove that $r_4(6,n)\ge 2^{2^{cn}}$ for some absolute constant $c>0$, improving upon our previous bound. Consequently, we confirm the Erdős--Hajnal conjecture for $r_k(k+2,n)$ for all fixed $k\ge4$.

</details>


### [146] [Periodic Tilings of Cardinality Twice a Prime or Nine in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26576)
*Hu Tan,Ying Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：基数 $2q$（$q$ 为奇素数）或基数 $9$ 的任意有限平移铺砌块均存在完全周期性的互补铺砌集，结论在任意维数下成立，且与铺砌块差分生成子群的秩无关。证明结合互素伸缩、单位根消失和与、周期二着色，将铺砌块转移到其内蕴格上讨论，并给出两类基数族的可铺砌性判定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移铺砌（translational tiling）中，补集是否可取自周期性子集是离散几何与组合数论的核心问题。已有结果多依赖格子补集或对维数与格的秩施加限制，而本文关注的是如何在不限制内蕴格秩、不限定维数的情形下，对基数 $2q$（奇素数倍）与 $9$ 这两类铺砌块证明完全周期补集的存在性，并由此得到可判定性算法。

Method: 方法上采用"平移—内蕴格—提升"的框架：先平移铺砌块使其落入其内蕴格，在其上构造周期补集，再证其可扩展到环境格。核心技术包括互素伸缩（coprime dilation）、单位根消失和（vanishing sums of roots of unity）以及周期二着色。对基数 $2q$ 情形，在内蕴格上做谱过滤（spectral filtering），得到格子补集或与铺砌补集相容的周期二重覆盖，再由周期真二着色将其分裂。对 $9$ 点情形，按内蕴秩分类：秩 $1$、$2$ 分别处理；高秩时在内蕴格中做代数约化，得到格子补集或落在有限多条有理仿射圆上的谱支撑。圆支撑的替代情形通过条件密度二分法与例外半密度分量的周期替换论证解决。

Result: 主要结果有三：(1) 基数 $2q$（$q$ 为奇素数）的有限平移铺砌块必存在完全周期补集；(2) 基数 $9$ 的有限平移铺砌块必存在完全周期补集；(3) 两个基数族均获得可铺砌性判定算法。上述结论对任意维数、任意内蕴格秩均成立，且内蕴格上构造的周期补集可提升至环境格。

Conclusion: 论文表明有限平移铺砌块在基数 $2q$ 与 $9$ 两类情形下具有强周期性结构，补集可选取为完全周期集，从而将铺砌存在性问题归约为可判定问题。谱过滤、圆支撑代数约化与周期着色等工具为推广到更一般的基数提供了技术路线。

Abstract: We prove that every finite translational tile of $\mathbb Z^d$ of cardinality $2q$, where $q$ is an odd prime, or of cardinality nine admits a fully periodic tiling complement. The result holds in every dimension, regardless of the rank of the subgroup generated by the differences of points of the tile. The proofs combine coprime dilation, vanishing sums of roots of unity, and periodic two-colorings. After translating the tile, both arguments restrict to its intrinsic lattice, and every periodic complement constructed there extends to the ambient lattice. For cardinality $2q$, spectral filtering in the intrinsic lattice yields either a lattice complement or a periodic twofold covering compatible with a tiling complement. This compatibility allows the covering to be split by a periodic proper two-coloring. For nine points, intrinsic ranks $1$ and $2$ are treated separately; in higher intrinsic rank, the algebraic reduction in the intrinsic lattice yields either a lattice complement or spectral support on finitely many rational affine circles. These results also give a decision algorithm for tileability in both cardinality families. The circle-supported alternative is resolved by a conditional-density dichotomy and a periodic replacement argument for the exceptional half-density components.

</details>


### [147] [Erdős-Ko-Rado properties of Steiner 2-designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26607)
*Sam Adriaensen,Sergey Goryainov,Elena V. Konstantinova,Vedran Krčadinac*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了由Desarguesian极大弧导出的Steiner 2-设计中的最大相交族具有Erdős-Ko-Rado刻画，回答了Goryainov-Konstantinova的问题，并对2-(120,8,1)设计进行了计算研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Goryainov与Konstantinova近期提出的问题，并对Godsil与Meagher的问题寻找反例，同时推进Steiner 2-设计中最大相交族的分类与刻画。

Method: 证明由Desarguesian极大弧导出的Steiner 2-设计族中最大相交族的Erdős-Ko-Rado刻画；对2-(120,8,1)设计进行计算机搜索与计算研究；构造具有tight dual arcs的2-(66,6,1)设计的参数化推广。

Result: 给出了Desarguesian极大弧导出设计的EKR刻画；证明在已知Steiner 2-设计中只有有限多个容许既非canonical也非与subdesign相关联的最大相交族；在2-(120,8,1)设计中发现Godsil-Meagher问题的强反例；构造了2-(66,6,1)设计的参数化推广，其tight dual arcs构成非canonical最大相交族。

Conclusion: 结果解决了Goryainov-Konstantinova的公开问题，表明已知Steiner 2-设计中非canonical/非subdesign类型的最大相交族极为稀有，并提供了Godsil-Meagher问题的反例及新的非canonical最大相交族构造。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove an Erdős-Ko-Rado characterisation of maximum intersecting families of blocks in Steiner $2$-designs arising from Desarguesian maximal arcs. This answers a recent question of Goryainov and Konstantinova, and implies that, among the known Steiner $2$-designs, only finitely many admit a maximum intersecting family that is neither canonical nor associated with a subdesign. We also perform a computational study of $2$-$(120,8,1)$ designs and find strong counterexamples to a problem of Godsil and Meagher. Finally, we give a parametric generalisation of $2$-$(66,6,1)$ designs with tight dual arcs as non-canonical maximum intersecting families.

</details>


### [148] [Resilience of rainbow Hamilton cycles in pseudorandom graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26655)
*Elad Aigner-Horev,Dan Hefetz,Yury Person*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明:在(p,β)-bijumbled图上,满足最小度≥(1/2+ε)pn的生成子图H,对任意全局μpn-有界染色都含彩虹Hamilton圈,并给出计数与渗流结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Coulson、Keevash、Perarnau和Yepremyan关于随机图中彩虹Hamilton圈的存在性问题,并将其推广到确定性的伪随机图(pseudorandom hosts),同时统一处理边冲突规避问题。

Method: 在(p,β)-bijumbled图G的生成子图H上,给定全局μpn-有界染色,利用相对度条件或最小度条件,构造一个O(1/(pn))-spread的概率测度于无冲突Hamilton圈集合上,由此统一推导存在性、计数与渗流结论。

Result: 对任意固定ε∈(0,1/2),在β≤cpn且pn≥M时,δ(H)≥(1/2+ε)pn的H含彩虹Hamilton圈;相对条件°_H(v)≥(1/2+ε)°_G(v)(需δ(G)≥(1-ε/4)pn)下同样成立;两种情形下至少有(apn)^n个此类圈。当pn≥D log n时,以概率D log n/(pn)独立保留边,渐近几乎必然保持彩虹Hamilton性。

Conclusion: 三个结论均可用"规避指定边对(每边至多μpn个冲突伙伴)"替代彩虹性;存在性定理解决了随机图中的相应问题并推广到确定性伪随机图,对数度条件仅用于渗流结论。

Abstract: For every fixed $\varepsilon\in(0,1/2)$, we prove that every spanning subgraph $H$ of an $n$-vertex $(p,β)$-bijumbled graph satisfying $δ(H)\geq(1/2+\varepsilon)pn$ contains a rainbow Hamilton cycle under every globally $μpn$-bounded colouring, provided $β\leq cpn$ and $pn\geq M$ both hold. The same assertion holds under the relative condition $°_H(v)\geq(1/2+\varepsilon)°_G(v)$ for every vertex $v$, provided $δ(G)\geq(1-\varepsilon/4)pn$ holds. Under either degree condition, there are at least $(apn)^n$ such cycles. Here, $c,μ,a,M>0$ depend only on $\varepsilon$; in particular, $pn$ may be a sufficiently large constant. If $pn\geq D\log n$, then, upon fixing a coloured $H$, retaining each edge independently with probability $D\log n/(pn)$ preserves rainbow Hamiltonicity asymptotically almost surely. The logarithmic degree requirement is needed only for this percolation conclusion. The existence theorem answers a problem of Coulson, Keevash, Perarnau and Yepremyan for random graphs and extends it to deterministic pseudorandom hosts.
  In fact, all three conclusions hold with rainbowness replaced by avoidance of prescribed pairs of edges; each edge having at most $μpn$ conflicting partners. We construct an $O(1/(pn))$-spread probability measure on conflict-free Hamilton cycles; this then yields the enumeration and percolation results.

</details>


### [149] [Graphs with Minimum Algebraic Connectivity I: Proofs of Aldous-Fill and Guiduli-Mohar Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26699)
*Maryam Abdi,Ebrahim Ghorbani*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于正则图随机游走松弛时间上界的Aldous–Fill猜想，以及关于最小代数连通度图结构的Guiduli–Mohar猜想和奇正则图对应的Abdi–Ghorbani猜想，并证明了渐近最小代数连通度图具有渐近最大直径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aldous–Fill猜想给出了连通正则图上随机游走最大松弛时间的上界估计，由于松弛时间等于d/μ(G)（μ(G)为代数连通度），该猜想与正则图代数连通度最小化问题密切相关。Guiduli–Mohar猜想和Abdi–Ghorbani猜想则刻画了最小代数连通度图的结构（路径状、两端附近有界、具有规定块结构），Abdi–Ghorbani还猜测渐近最小代数连通度的图具有渐近最大直径。这些猜想此前均未被证明。

Method: 通过对正则图及固定最小度图的代数连通度（Laplacian第二小特征值）进行极值分析，刻画最小化代数连通度的图结构，证明其呈路径状且除两端有界部分外具有规定块结构；并分析代数连通度与直径之间的定量关系，证明渐近最小代数连通度蕴含渐近最大直径。

Result: 证明了Aldous–Fill猜想（最大松弛时间上界为(1+o(1))3n²/(2π²)，偶数n时渐近取等）；证明了Guiduli–Mohar猜想；证明了固定奇度d≥3的d-正则图对应的Abdi–Ghorbani猜想；并证明对每个固定奇度d≥3，d-正则图及固定最小度d≥3的图中，渐近最小代数连通度的图具有渐近最大直径，从而确立了Abdi–Ghorbani另一猜想的相关情形。

Conclusion: 本文解决了正则图随机游走松弛时间与代数连通度最小化领域的多个核心猜想：Aldous–Fill猜想、Guiduli–Mohar猜想及Abdi–Ghorbani的两个猜想（奇度正则图结构猜想与渐近最大直径猜想），揭示了正则图中代数连通度最小化与路径状结构、大直径之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Aldous and Fill (2002) conjectured that the maximum relaxation time of a random walk on a connected regular graph with $n$ vertices is bounded above by $(1+o(1))\frac{3n^2}{2π^2}$, with asymptotic equality for even $n$. Since the relaxation time of a $d$-regular graph $G$ is $d/μ(G)$, where $μ(G)$ denotes its algebraic connectivity, this conjecture is closely related to the problem of minimizing algebraic connectivity among regular graphs. Guiduli and Mohar (1996) conjectured that, for every fixed minimum degree $δ=d\ge 3$ and all sufficiently large orders, graphs with minimum algebraic connectivity are path-like and, apart from bounded portions near their two ends, have a prescribed block structure. For fixed odd degree $d\ge 3$, Abdi and Ghorbani (2004) conjectured that $d$-regular graphs with minimum algebraic connectivity have the same structure. We prove the Aldous--Fill conjecture and the Guiduli--Mohar conjecture, as well as the corresponding conjecture for $d$-regular graphs of fixed odd degree. Finally, we prove that, for every fixed odd degree $d\ge 3$, $d$-regular graphs, as well as graphs of fixed minimum degree $d\ge 3$, whose algebraic connectivity is asymptotically minimum have asymptotically maximum diameter. This establishes the corresponding cases of another conjecture of Abdi and Ghorbani.

</details>


### [150] [Graphs with Minimum Algebraic Connectivity II: Regular Graphs of Even Degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26700)
*Maryam Abdi,Ebrahim Ghorbani*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解决偶数正则图中最小代数连通度的结构刻画问题，证明了Abdi–Ghorbani猜想在偶数情形，并给出渐近公式与直径界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aldous–Fill猜想涉及连通正则图上随机游走的渐近最大松弛时间，可归结为在正则图中最小化代数连通度μ(G)。Guiduli–Mohar猜想刻画了最小代数连通度图的结构（path-like并具指定块结构），Abdi–Ghorbani将其推广到d-正则图。Part I已证明Aldous–Fill、Guiduli–Mohar及奇数度的Abdi–Ghorbani猜想，剩偶数度情形未解决。

Method: 通过结构分析与极值图论方法，对固定偶数度d≥4的正则图中的代数连通度进行最小化研究，确立最小化图的结构并推导渐近展开；进而分析此类图的直径及归一化谱隙。

Result: 证明偶数度的Abdi–Ghorbani猜想，完成正则图最小代数连通度的结构刻画；对固定偶数d≥4，最小代数连通度为2(d-2)π²/n²+O_d(n^{-3})；每个最小化图直径为3n/(d+1)+O_d(1)；对固定偶数d≥6，代数连通度渐近最小的d-正则图具有渐近最大直径；对所有偶数正则度（含随n增长的度）得到尖锐的归一化谱隙界。

Conclusion: 偶数情形得以解决，与Part I的奇数情形合并后完整刻画了最小代数连通度的正则图结构；同时给出了最小代数连通度的二阶渐近、最小化图直径的渐近以及渐近最大直径性质，并建立了涵盖增长度的尖锐归一化谱隙界。

Abstract: Aldous and Fill (2002) conjectured the asymptotic maximum relaxation time of a random walk on a connected regular graph. Since the relaxation time of a $d$-regular graph $G$ is $d/μ(G)$, where $μ(G)$ denotes its algebraic connectivity, this conjecture is closely related to the problem of minimizing algebraic connectivity among regular graphs. Guiduli and Mohar (1996) conjectured that, for every fixed minimum degree $δ=d\ge3$ and all sufficiently large orders, graphs with minimum algebraic connectivity are path-like and, apart from bounded portions near their two ends, have a prescribed block structure. Abdi and Ghorbani (2024) proposed an analogous structural conjecture for $d$-regular graphs with minimum algebraic connectivity and fixed degree $d\ge3$. In Part~I, we proved the Aldous--Fill conjecture, the Guiduli--Mohar conjecture, and for odd degrees, the Abdi Ghorbani conjecture. In this paper, we settle the remaining even-degree case, thereby completing the structural characterization of regular graphs with minimum algebraic connectivity. We also prove that, for every fixed even $d\ge4$, the minimum algebraic connectivity at order $n$ is $2(d-2)π^2/n^2+O_d(n^{-3})$, and that every minimizing graph has diameter $3n/(d+1)+O_d(1)$. For every fixed even $d\ge6$, $d$-regular graphs whose algebraic connectivity is asymptotically minimum have asymptotically maximum diameter. Finally, we obtain a sharp normalized-gap bound for all even regular degrees, including degrees that grow with $n$.

</details>
